"vīṭutōṟum mīṉ cuṭutalāl pulāl nāṟṟattōṭu kalantu eḻum pukiyiṉ nīṇṭa oḻuṅku, vayalarukē niṟkum marutamarattu vaḷinta kōṭukaḷil ceṉṟu cūḻntu koḷḷum periya varuvāy ippōtu illiyākip pōkavē, muḻavu mutalāyiṉa muḻaṅka, paliyiṭṭu vaḻipaṭṭa kaṭavuḷ ellām, tām kuṭiyirunta kantakaṅkaḷi viṭṭup pōyviṭṭaṉa. ataṉāl pāḻuṟṟup pōṉa ūr maṉṟaṅkaḷil paṇṭellām, naritiriyuṟṟa mutiyōr ōyvāka amarntu cūtāṭuṅkāl, cūtāṭukaruvikaḷ vīḻntu vīḻntu paṇṇiviṭṭa kuḻikaḷ, uṭalellām puḷḷikaḷik koṇṭa kāṭṭuk kōḻikaḷ iṭṭa muṭṭikaḷāl niṟintuviṭum"

"mīṉcuṭu pukiyiṉ pulavunāṟu neṭuṅkoṭi

vayaluḻi marutiṉ. vāṅkuciṉi valakkum

perunal yāṇariṉ orīi, iṉiyē

kalikeḻu kaṭavuḷ kantam kiviṭap,

palikaṇ māṟiya pāḻpaṭu potiyil

narimū tāḷar nāyiṭak kuḻinta

vallil nallakam niṟiyap palpoṟik

kāṉa vāraṇam īṉam"

puṟam - 52-9-16;





itu pōlavē pāḻuṟṟup pōṉa potiyilil uḷḷa paḷḷaṅkaḷiliruntu koṇṭuvanta taṇṇīri naṟṟiṇi kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. ‘'maṉṟattuk kalluṭip paṭuviṟ kaluḻi” (39 :3-4) atē toki nūlil, ‘'muruṅki marattil, vēṉiṟ kālattil kāyntu muṟṟiya neṟṟu pōṉṟa aḻaku illāta viralkaḷiyuṭiya , accam ūṭṭum, pēroli eḻuppum vāyiyuṭiya pēy, vaḷappattip paṇṭu uṭiyatāki irunta ūriṉkaṇ uḷḷa, teyvatti vaḻipaṭṭu iṭum paliccōṟṟi uṇṇuvāṉ virumpip pāḻmaṉṟam nōkki mōti eḻum”, tuṉpamikka mālippoḻutu viḷakkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. “vēṉil murukkiṉ viḷi tuṇar aṉṉa

māṇā virala valvāyp pēey

mallal mūtūr malarppali uṇīiya

maṉṟam pōḻum puṉkaṇ māli

naṟṟiṇi : 73 : 1-4





mēlum piṟpaṭṭa kālaṅkaḷil, ūrmaṉṟamākap periya māḷikikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭu, maṉṉarkaḷip pāṭip paricil peṟuvāṉ vēṇṭivarum pulavarkaḷum piṟa iravalarkaḷum taṅkum iṭamākavum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. marattālāṉa pala kampaṅkaḷik koṇṭa nakar araṅkiṉ oru pakkam, paḷḷiyākap payaṉpaṭṭatip puṟanāṉūṟu kāṭṭukiṟatu, "palkāl potiyil oruciṟi paḷḷi āka” (puṟam : 375 : 2-3) "maṉṟupaṭu paricilar” (puṟam:135: 17) nakaril uḷḷa, maṉṟam, potiyil pōlum iṭaṅkaḷellām piṟkālattil, kōyil teyvattirumēṉikaḷiṉ uṟiviṭaṅkaḷākiviṭṭaṉa. maṉṟamum potiyil pōlum iṭaṅkaḷellām piṟkālattil, kōyil teyvat tirumēṉikaḷiṉ uṟiviṭaṅkaḷākiviṭṭaṉa. "maṉṟamum potiyilum kantuṭi niliyilum" (tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭi : 226) vaḻakkaṟukkum naṭuvar maṉṟamākavum viḷaṅkiya uṟiyūr nakar araṅkam, puṟanāṉūṟṟil (220 : 7) “mūtūr maṉṟam” eṉak kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu, eṉṟālum uri āciriyar oruvar, maṉṟam eṉpataṟkuk kutirikaḷ ōṭum ceṇṭuveḷi" eṉap poruḷ kūṟiyuḷḷār.

aṟam vaḻaṅkum ūrpperiyavarkaḷ kūṭum ālamarattilum, ūr mutiyōr ōyvu koḷḷum paḻiya kuṭilkaḷilum naṭappaṭṭa kantu eṉum marakkaḻikaḷilum iṭam peṟṟirunta kaṭavuḷ etu eṉṉum viṉā viṭi kāṇamāṭṭātē niṟkiṟatu. ivviṉāviṟku viṭi kāṇpataṟku muṉṉar, nām āyntu koṇṭirukkum ikkālattiṟku mikamika muṟpaṭṭa kālamākiya kaṟkāla kaṭṭattil, aiyattiṟku iṭam iṉṟi vaḻipaṭuvataṟkāka eṉṟē kal liṅkaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu kuṟippiṭal poruntum. ulōkak kālam (vaṭa intiyāvil ceppuk kālam, teṉ intiyāvil irumpukkālam toṭaṅkiya piṉṉarum kal iliṅka vaṭivaṅkaḷ toṭarntu vaḻipaṭṭu varalāyiṉa. liṅkam pōl tōṟṟam aḷikkum. cila poruṭkaḷ, hārappā, mōkaṉ jōtarōkkaḷil tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟil iraṇṭu vakikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. oṉṟu uyarattil ari aṅkulattiliruntu ōr aṭivari vēṟupaṭum caturaṅka viḷiyāṭṭukkāykaḷ pōlum vaṭivuṭiyaṉa; iraṇṭu, kuṟukkē ari aṅkulattiliruntu 3 aṭi allatu 4 aṭi uyaram uḷḷa vaṭṭakkal vaṭivuṭiyaṉa. "avaṟṟuḷ cila kāṇappaṭṭa cūḻniliyōṭu, atu toṭarpāṉa poruḷ kaḷiṉ tokutiyiṉiyum iṇittup pārkkumpōtu, avvuṇmikaḷ vaḻipāṭṭu muṟikku uriya, yātō cila poruḷkaḷ aṅku iruntaṉa eṉṟa muṭiviṟkuc ciṟu cantēkattiṟkum iṭam vikkavilli. avvaṭṭakkal, mesapaṭōmiyāvil kāṇakkiṭitta oru poruḷōṭu orumippāṭuṭiyatāṉa ceṇṭu allatu taṇṭāyutattiṉ taliyāka irukkalām eṉṟa karuttum kūṟappaṭṭatu. āṉāl nūlāciriyariṉ karuttuppaṭi, palapaṭiyālum ēṟṟuk koḷḷak kūṭiya viḷakkam, vaṭṭakkal, yōṉi ākum. caturaṅkakkāy pōlum poruḷ liṅkam ākum eṉpatē ām. yōṉiyum liṅkamum, vaṭakōṭi mutal teṉkōṭivari, kīḻkkōṭi mutal mēṟkōṭi variyāṉa intiyā muḻuvatum, civaṉik kuṟikka ellōrkkum viḷaṅkum vaṭivamākum. liṅka vaḻipāṭu, āriyar varukikku mikavum muṟpaṭṭa, naṉmikap paḻiya vaḻipāṭṭu muṟiyām eṉpatil evvita viṉāviṟkum iṭam illi.'’ eṉkiṟār tiruvāḷar jāṉ mārṣel avarkaḷ. (Archeological Survey of India. Annual Report. 1925-26, page:79)

teṉṉintiyāvil, ki.pi. 500kku muṟpaṭṭa kālattic cērnta pāṭalkaḷil, civaṉ, ālamar celvaṉākavē kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷāṉ eṉpatu īṇṭuk kuṟippiṭal poruntum, puṟanāṉūṟu pāṭṭu oṉṟu, ālamarattik kaṭavuḷukku uriyatākak kūṟukiṟatu. ‘kaṭavuḷ ālam'’ (1:7) vēṟucila pāṭalkaḷ, civaṉi, ālil uṟiyum iṟivaṉām eṉat teḷivāka uṇarttukiṉṟaṉa. “ālamar celvaṉ'’ (ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭi: 1:97; kalittoki : 81:9:83:45), "ālkeḻu kaṭavuḷ” (tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭi : 256). ippāṭalkaḷ, āriyak karuttukaḷ, tamiḻppāṭalkaḷiṭiyē iṭam peṟat toṭaṅkiya kālattic cērntavi eṉṟālum kantu eṉappaṭum kampattil iṭam peṟṟa kaṭavuḷum, ālamarattil iṭam peṟṟa kaṭavuḷum oṉṟē. paṇṭit tamiḻ ilakkiya kālattil civaṉ, oru nilattiṉ teyvamāka iṭam peṟavilli. avaṉ oru ciṟu teyvamē murukaṉukkut tantiyāka uṇarntu muṭivāka, aintu āṟu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil, civa ākama neṟi, tamiḻnāṭṭil paraviyapōtu perunteyvam āyiṉāṉ. vaṭa intiya rutraṉ, toṭakkattil, oru malināṭṭut talivaṉ makaḷi maṇantu koṇṭa oru malikkaṭavuḷāvaṉ. ākavē avaṉ taṅkaḷ tava oḻukkatti mēṟkoḷvāṉ vēṇṭi - malikaḷil ceṉṟu taṅkum yōkiyarkaḷukkuk kaṭavuḷāki viṭṭāṉ. teṉṉāṭṭiṟku avaṉ kuṭipeyarntatum, civavaḻipāṭu ciṟantu vaḷarcciyip peṟṟu viṭṭatu. āṉāl atu nikaḻvataṟku muṉṉar, kiṭikkak kūṭiya naṉimikap paḻiya tamiḻppāṭalkaḷil kāṇappaṭuvatu pōl, tamiḻ nāṭṭil, avaṉ ālamarattuk kaṭavuḷākavē iruntuḷḷāṉ, oruvēḷi, ālamarattu niḻalil oru liṅkam naṭappaṭṭirukkakkūṭum. aṅṅaṉam illi eṉṟāl, avaṉ ālamar celvaṉāka ākiyirukka muṭiyātu. marutam, ilanti, nāval pōlum marattaṭikaḷilum, liṅkam vikkappaṭṭatu. civa vaḻipāṭu peruki, ammaraṅkaḷi uḷḷaṭakkic civaṉ kōyilkaḷ kaṭṭat toṭaṅkiya aintām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟkup piṟaku, aviyellām pukaḻ peṟṟa vaḻipāṭṭiṭaṅkaḷāki viṭṭaṉa. ippōtu teṉṉāṭṭil, mikavum pukaḻ peṟṟu viḷaṅkum civaṉ kōyilkaḷil, iviyum iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. puṉita maraṅkaḷiliruntu veṭṭappaṭṭu, immaram, ammarattaṭi liṅka vaṭivam ākiya iraṇṭiṉ iṟittaṉmiyum koṇṭuviṭṭa kōyil kuṭilkaḷil naṭappaṭṭu, kantu eṉappaṭum akkaḻikaḷum, liṅkamākak karutiyē naṭappaṭṭirukkak kūṭum.

nakaraṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟam

oru nilattup paṇṭaṅkaḷip piṟa nilaṅkaḷiṉ paṇṭaṅkaḷukku viliyākap paṇṭamāṟṟam ceytu koṇṭataṉ viḷivākavē, toṭakkattil nakaraṅkaḷ tōṉṟalāyiṉa. kaṭaṟkari nāṭṭiṉ poruḷām uppu, ēṉiya nāṭukaḷil, iṉṟiyamiyāp poruḷākiviṭṭatu. ataṉāl naṉimikap paḻaṅkālattiliruntē, uppic cila camayam ularnta mīṉiyum ēṟṟik koṇṭa, taḷḷāṭittaḷḷāṭic cellum kaṭṭi vaṇṭikaḷiṉ varici, maṇ cālikaḷik kaṭantu, mali nāṭṭiṉ aṭivarikkaṇṇatāṉa uḷnāṭukaḷukkuc cellalāyiṉa. uppu vaṇṭikaḷum, uppu vaṇikarum pulavarkaḷāl, aṭikkaṭi kūṟappaṭṭaṉar. "kuṉṟiṉ mītu tōṉṟum uppu vaṇikar talivaṉ", "kuṉṟil tōṉṟum kulavumaṇal cērppa" (akam : 310:10) (kuṟippu : 'kulavumaṇal" eṉṟa toṭarik kuva, umaṇar" eṉac coṟcitivu ceytu avvāṟu poruḷ koṇṭuḷḷār. atu tavaṟu. kuṉṟupōl tōṉṟum, maṇalmēṭukaḷ niṟinta kaṭaṟkari, talivaṉē eṉpatutāṉ attoṭar uṇarttum poruḷākum. atē akattil, pāliyil payaṇam koṇṭa uppu vaṇikar, āṅkē mūṉṟu kaṟkaḷāl ceytu koṇṭa aṭuppu uṇarttappaṭṭuḷatu. “umaṇ cāttu iṟanta oḻikal aṭuppu” (akam : 119 : 8). appākkaḷ kūṟum viḷakkat tiliruntu, paṇṭaṅkaḷ vaṇṭi vaṇṭiyāka, ōriṭattiliruntu piṟa iṭaṅkaḷukkuk koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatu terikiṟatu. kaṭalic cārnta nilattil, kaḻinīrāl viḷinta uppi ēṟṟik koṇṭu, malināṭṭi nōkkic cellum vaṇṭiyiṉ, ārakkālkaḷiyuṭiya cakkaram paḷḷattil pāyntuviṭumpōtu, taṉ uraṅkoṇṭu attaḷarcci tīrttu īrttuc cellum, perumpāram tāṅkavalla mikka valiyuṭiya kāḷikku ōr aracaṉ oppuk kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷāṉ.

"kāṉal kaḻiuppu mukantu kalnāṭu maṭukkum

āric cākāṭṭu āḻcci pōkkum

uraṉuṭi nōṉpakaṭṭu aṉṉa eṅkōṉ”

- puṟam : 60 : 6-9





iṭivaḻiyil murivuṟṟup pōyiṉ, āṅku utavuvataṟkākat taṉiyāka ōr accu uṭaṉ koṇṭu cellumaḷavu vaṇṭikaḷil perumpāram ēṟṟappaṭum; avvāṟu taṉiyē eṭuttuc cellum accu ‘'cēm accu” eṉappaṭum. erutukaḷ iḷiyaṉa: nukattaṭiyil itaṟku muṉ pūṭṭi aṟiyātaṉa. vaṇṭiyilō paṇṭap potikaḷ mikap perumaḷavil ēṟṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. mēṭu nōkki ēṟum pōtō, paḷḷam nōkkip pāyum pōtō ētēṉum ākak kūṭum ati muṉkūṭṭi aṟital iyalātu. etaṟkum taṉiyē ōr accu uṭaṉ koṇṭu cēral nallatu eṉa eṇṇum umaṇar, uḷḷa acciṉ kīḻē, vēṟōr accit taṉiyē pirittuk koṇṭu celvar eṉkiṟatu puṟanāṉūṟṟup pāṭṭu oṉṟu.

‘erutē iḷiya nukamuṇa rāvē; :

cakaṭam paṇṭam peritu pey taṉṟē;

aval iḻiyiṉam, mici ēṟiṉum

avaṇatu aṟiyunar yār? eṉa umaṇar

kīḻmarattu yātta cēm accu”

puṟam : 102 : 1-5





uppu vaṇṭikaḷ oḻuṅkākac celvatu, iḷam ciṟārkaḷukkuk kaḷippūṭṭum kaṇkāṭciyām. iṉiya nīr niṟintu, mikka aḻakuṭiyatāṉa cuṉiyil pūtta ceṅkaḻunīriṉ moṭṭu aviḻnta malarnta malarkaḷip puṟa itaḻkaḷi nīkkiviṭṭu muḻu malarkaḷāl āṉa āṭi aciyum alkuliyum, pēraḻaku vāyntu aruḷ oḻukum kaṇkaḷiyum, iṉiya iḷa nakiyiṉiyum uṭiya iḷamakaḷir, pul rueṅka muḷittuk kavartta vaḻiyiṉiyum, neruṅka muḷvittuc ceyta vēliyiṉiyum, pañcu niṟinta muṟṟattiṉiyum uṭiya vīṭṭiṉ orupāl uḷḷa pīrkkaṅ koṭiyum, curikkoṭiyum paṭarntu kiṭakkum, īcca marattu ilikaḷiyum koṇṭa kuppi mītu ēṟi niṉṟu, umaṇar vaṇṭikaḷi, oṉṟu iraṇṭu eṉa eṇṇik koṇṭirukkum kāṭci oṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟṟup pāṭal oṉṟil iṭam peṟṟuḷatu. "tīnīrp peruṅkuṇṭu cuṉippūtta kuvaḷik

kūmpu aviḻmuḻuneṟi puraḷvarum alkul,

ēntueḻil maḻikkaṇ; iṉṉaki makaḷir,

puṉmūcu kavaliya muḷmiṭi vēlip

pañcu muṉṟil ciṟṟil āṅkaṇ

pīri nāṟiya curi ivar maruṅkiṉ

īttu ilik kuppiēṟi, umaṇar

uppoy oḻuki eṇṇum"

- puṟam : 116: 1-8





oru nilattiliruntu oru nilattiṟku uppik koṇṭu celvatu ivvaḷavu periya aḷavil naṭipeṟṟatu eṉṟāl, maṟṟap paṇṭaṅkaḷ, ativiṭa mikap periya aḷavil koṇṭucellappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum eṉa uṟutiyāka nampalām. avvāṟē, ariciyum parutti āṭikaḷum āṟṟup paḷḷattākkup pakutiyiliruntu vaṟaṇṭa pakutikkuk koṇṭu cellappaṭṭaṉa. payaṟu vakikaḷum pālpaṭu poruḷkaḷum mullip pakutiyiliruntu naṉcey nilappakutikkuk koṇṭu cellappaṭṭaṉa. tiṉi, tēṉ, maṟṟum malipaṭuporuḷkaḷ, maṟṟap pakutikaḷukkuk koṇṭu cellappaṭṭaṉa. ippaṇṭaṅkaḷ mulli, kuṟiñci pōlum mēṭṭu nilamum tāḻvāṉa nilapparappum iṇiyum iṭaṅkaḷil, perumaḷavil paṇṭamāṟṟam ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. attaku paṇṭamāṟṟumiṭaṅkaḷellām perunakaraṅkaḷāka vaḷarntuviṭṭaṉa.

ākavē, vaṟaṇṭa nilamām puṉcey nilappakutiyilum, nīrvaḷam mikka naṉcey nilappakutiyum iṇiyum, atāvatu, āṟu, taṉ iṭinilip pōkkik kiviṭuttu, camanilattil amitiyāka ōṭat toṭaṅkiya iṭattil, mutaṉmutalāka nakaraṅkaḷ eḻalāyiṉa. cōḻar talinakar maturi ākiya paḻamperum nakaraṅkaḷiṉ tōṟṟattiṟku ituvē kāraṇam. innakaraṅkaḷ, parutti viḷiyum pakutikaḷiliruntu parutti koṇṭu varappaṭṭu, eṅku āṭiyāka neyyappaṭumō, anta iṭaṅkaḷil, iṉṟē pōl, paṇṭum, āṭi, necavukkup peyarpeṟṟirunta iṭaṅkaḷil, iṭam peṟṟu uḷḷaṉa. ivviṭaṅkaḷil vaḷarntu perukiya vāṇikam, tamiḻaracarkaḷiṉ talinakarām perumiyi, annakaraṅkaḷ toṭakkattilēyē peṟat tuṇi purintaṉa. innakaraṅkaḷ kuṟitta viḷakkam paḻampāṭalkaḷil iṭam peṟavilli.

mukkiya tuṟimukaṅkaḷ

ikkāla kaṭṭattil tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ mikap periya veḷināṭṭu vāṇikam, aṭutta nūṟṟāṇṭil peripuḷūs āciriyarālum, tālamiyālum virivāka eṭuttuk kūṟappaṭṭa eṇṇaṟṟa tuṟimukaṅkaḷ iṭam peṟat tuṇipurintatu. muntiya atikārattil eṭuttuk kāṭṭiya petta jātakā katiyiliruntu, ki.mu. mutal āyirattāṇṭil, kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉam, cōḻa aracarkaḷiṉ mikap periya tuṟimukamākavum, avarkaḷiṉ iraṇṭāvatu talinakarākavum tikaḻntatu eṉpati aṟintukoṇṭōm. camaskiruta eḻuttāḷarkaḷāl pāṇṭiyariṉ nuḻivāyil eṉum poruḷil, ‘'pāṇṭiya kavāṭam” eṉa aḻikkappaṭṭa koṟki, pāṇṭiyar talinakarām perumiyi maturiyiliruntu paṟittuk koṇṭu, pāṇṭiyar talinakar, aṇmiyil, maturikku māṟṟappaṭṭatu eṉat tālami kūṟum kālam vari, takka vittuk koṇṭiruntatu. koṟkiyiṉ mukkiyattum, paṇṭiyōrāl peritum pukaḻappaṭṭa muttu vāṇikattiṉ nilikkaḷamāy uṇmiyilēyē iṭam peṟṟatu. cērarkaḷiṉ mukkiyat tuṟimukaṅkaḷ. mēṟku āciyāvukkum, ekiptukkum, avaṟṟiṟku appālum, miḷakiyum, maṟṟum maṇam taru uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷiyum ēṟṟumati ceyta muciṟiyum, toṇṭiyumām.

aracarkaḷ

nākarīka vāḻvu kāṇā appaḻaṅkālattil, aracaṉuk kāṉa nirvākap paṇikaḷ mikaccilavē; āṭci muṟiyil ' tēvippaṭum oru cilavum, ūr aṟaṅkūr avikaḷāl, naṉmikap paḻiya muṟikkēṟpa niṟivēṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiyarākiya mūvēntarkaḷ, oruvarōṭoruvar toṭarntu pōriṭṭuk koṇṭiruntaṉar. appōtellām nāṭu piṭippataṟkāka eṉṟu illāmal taṅkaḷ vīratti paṟicāṟṟavum, piṟa aracarkaḷik kāṭṭilum, tāmē periyavaṉ, pēraracaṉ eṉṟa niliyi nāṭṭavumē naṭipeṟṟaṉa. ammūṉṟu nāṭukaḷiṉ ellikaḷ oru niliyilēyē iruntaṉa. kaḷattil veṟṟi kaṇṭiruntālum ellikaḷ māṟṟam peṟavilli. amarāvati āṟu, kāviriyōṭu kalakkum mūṉṟu mukkōṇaṅkaḷiṉ potuvāṉa muṉiyil cantikkiṉṟaṉa. anta iṭattil, mūvēntarkaḷiṉ vaḻipāṭukaḷi oru cērap peṟṟavaḷum, aṉpukkaṭavuḷ - vaḷam - vaḻaṅkum kaṭavuḷ eṉṟellām pārāṭṭap peṟupavaḷumāṉa cellāyi eṉṟa peṇ teyvattukku, oru ciṟu kōyil niṟkiṟatu. akkōyil niṟkum iṭam, eṉakku uriyatu, eṉa yārum vātāṭa māṭṭā oruvarkkē uriya iṭamāka uḷatu. avviṭattil kāviriyōṭu kalakkum, "karipōṭṭāṉāṟu" eṉṟa poruḷ potinta peyaruṭiya oru ciṟṟāṟu, iru nāṭukaḷukkumiṭiyē elli vakuttu, mēṟkuk kaṭaṟkari vari nīṇṭirukkum cōḻa nāṭṭiliruntu pirikkiṟatu, kāviriyil akkariyil, paṇṭu iruntataṟkāṉa aṭiccuvaṭu iṉṟum kāṇappaṭum, ceyaṟkiyālāṉa oru mēṭu, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya nāṭukaḷiṉ elliyik kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. (puṟanāṉūṟu 47 ām eṇ ceyyuṭkuk kīḻvarum koḷuvilum, maṇimēkali, 19 ām kāti 126 ām variyilum kuṟippiṭappaṭṭirukkum, kāriyāṟṟut tuñciya neṭuṅkiḷḷi iṟanta kāriyāṟē, intak kari pōṭṭāṉāṟātal kūṭum. piṉṉark kūṟiya maṇimēkalip pakutiyil cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ ellikku arukil uḷḷa kāriyāṟṟiṉ kariyil naṭipeṟṟa pōr oṉṟu kuṟippiṭattakkatu) cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ talinakar, uṟiyūr, āṟṟiṉ kīḻppakutiyil uḷatu. cēra nāṭṭiṉ talinakar karūr āṟṟiṉ mēṟpakutiyil uḷḷatu. pāṇṭiya nāṭṭiṉ talinakar maturi, āṟṟiṟkut teṟkil uḷatu.

aracarkaḷiṉ taliyāya paṇi, nāṭṭi āṭumāṭukaḷik koḷḷiyaṭittuc celpavariṭamiruntu kāttal, kālnaṭi kaḷ, oṉṟu, maṟavar, kaḷḷarkaḷiṉ iṉattalivarkaḷām koḷḷik kūṭṭattalivarkaḷāl kaṭattic cellappaṭum. allatu, annāṭṭu aracaṉōṭu viṭutta aṟikūvalāka, maṟṟoru nāṭṭu aracaṉāl kaṭattic cellappaṭum. ivviraṇṭil etu kuṟittup pōr mēṟkoṇṭālum, aracaṉ taṉ kulattukku uriya māliyōṭu, cōḻaṉāyiṉ, āttimāliyōṭu, pāṇṭiyaṉāyiṉ vēppa māliyōṭu, cēraṉāyiṉ paṉimāliyōṭu, tāṉ kuṟittuc cellum pōrukku uriya aṭiyāḷa māliyiyum, aṇintu celvar. nāṭu kāvali aṭuttu aracaṉukku uriya piṟitoru taliyāya kaṭami, tam pākkaḷ mūlam taṉ pukaḻ pāṭum pulavar pōlum iravalarkaḷi aḻittut taṉṉic cūḻa vittuk koṇṭu, avarkaḷ pārāṭṭu kēṭṭu makiḻntu paricāka, nalla uṇavum, matuvum, niṟiporuḷum koṭuttal. kīḻvarum ceyyuḷ, ciṟitu piṟpaṭṭa kālattic cērnta tēyāyilum, aracarkaḷiṉ ikkaṭamikaḷit teḷivāka uṇarttukiṟatu. aviyē, akkālattum irunta uṇmiyāṉa niliyākum. "kuṟiyāta vaṇmiyum, paki aḻikkum pērāṇmiyum vāynta taliva, uṉ paṭiyic cērnta yāṉikaḷ malikaḷ pōl kāṭci aḷikkiṉṟaṉa. niṉ nāṟpaṭiyiṉ āravārappēroli, kaṭal alipōl muḻuṅkukiṟatu : kūriya muṉiyi uṭiya uṉ vēṟpaṭi miṉṉal pōl oḷi vīcukiṟatu. ittaku peru niliyāl ulakattup pēraracarkaḷellām uḷam oṭuṅkuvataṟku ētuvāya pērāṟṟalōṭu tikaḻkiṉṟaṉi : niṉakkō niṉ nāṭṭiṟkō varakkūṭiya kuṟṟam etuvum, illiyāyiṟṟu; innili, uṉ kuṭikku vaḻivaḻiyāka varum perumiyākum. ataṉāl, ulakattu āṟukaḷ ellām, malikaḷiliruntu vīḻntu virintu kaṭali nōkki aṭivatu pōl, pulavarkaḷum iravalarkaḷum uṉṉi nōkki varuvārāyiṉar".

"āṉā īki, aṭupōr aṇṇal!

yāṉiyum maliyiṉ tōṉṟum; peruma!

niṉ tāṉiyum kaṭal eṉa muḻaṅkum; kūrnuṉi,

vēlum miṉṉiṉ viḷaṅkum ulakattu

aricu talipaṇikkum āṟṟali; ātaliṉ

puritīrntaṉṟu; putuvatō aṉṟē;

..........................

maliyiṉ iḻintu mākkaṭal nōkki

nilavari iḻitarum palyāṟu pōlap

pulavar ellām niṉnōk kiṉarē".

(puṟam : 42 : 7-6; 19-21)





veḷināṭṭu vāṇikam ki.mu. 600 mutal ki.pi. 14 vari

tāriyas āṭciyil

ki.mu. 606-l assiriyap pēraracu kaviḻkkappaṭṭatu. atu nikaḻntatum, pāpilōṉ, āciya vāṇikat talimiyiṭamāka ākiviṭṭatu. yavaṉar, yūtar poyṉīṣiyar, intiyar, cīṉar, eṉṟa ulaka vaṇika iṉaṅkaḷ, taṅkaḷ vaṇikap paṇṭaṅkaḷip , pāpilōṉiya cantikaḷukku eṭuttuc ceṉṟaṉa. ‘ayes ceylas (Aeschylus) eṉpār ‘'ellā vaki makkaḷum vēṟaṟak kalantuviṭṭa oru peruṅkūṭṭam” (Painminikton hoclon) eṉa aḻikkumaḷavu, pāpilōṉ nakarattu makkaḷ kalantuviṭṭaṉar. ki.pi. ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari toṭarntu vaḷam peṟṟirunta teṉṉintiya vaṇikar ki.mu. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭil, nippūril (Nippur) iruntu murāṣa-vukkum, (Murushu), avar makaṉukkum urimiyuṭiyatāṉa periya varttaka niṟuvaṉattiṉ vaṇikat toṭarpuṭiya paṭṭayaṅkaḷil, vaṇikar toṭarpāṉa kuṟippukaḷik kāṇkiṟōm. (J.R.A.S. 1917, P. 237 Kennedy) ki.mu.538l siras (Cyrus), pāpilōṉiyap pēraraci aḻittuviṭṭāṉ. avaṉ vaḻivanta matippārnta tāriyas (Dariwas) ki.mu. 20 ām nūṟṟāṇṭu pōlum kārattil sesōs - ṭiriyes (Sesostrises) eṉṟa oruvaṉāl mutaṉ mutalāka veṭṭappaṭṭu, ki.mu. 15 ām nūṟṟāṇṭil 18vatu araca marapiṉar āṭciyiṉ kīḻ, mīṇṭum tiṟakkappaṭṭa cūyas kālvāyik ki.mu. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭil oru paktiyit tiṟappataṉ mūlam, kaṭal, vaṇika vaḷarccikkut tuṇi purintāṉ. avaṉ, vaṭamēṟku intiyāvit taṉ pēraracōṭu iṇittuk koṇṭāṉ. atu, intukuṣ maliyiṉ kuṟukkākap, pālk pakutikkum, aṅkiruntu eksiṉ (Ewxine) pakutikkuc ceṉṟa, pālivaṉattik, kūṭṭamākak kaṭakkum vaṇikac cāttumuṟi puttuyir peṟa vaḻivakuttu viṭṭatu. atu, kārmēṉiyaṉ (Karmanian) pāliyit toṭṭuc celvatāki, aṅkiruntu mesapaṭōmiyā vaḻiyāka aṉṭiyōk (Antioch) nakarukkum ceṉṟatu. itaṉ mukkiya viḷivu, paṭṭu, mutaṉmutalāka, mēṟku nāṭukaḷic ceṉṟaṭintatu. "kirēkkarkaḷ, paṭṭi aleksāṇ ṭariṉ paṭiyeṭuppu mūlam aṟintu koṇṭaṉar eṉak karutappaṭṭatu. āṉāl atu ataṟku muṉpē, parṣayā mūlam, atu avarkaḷi aṭintirukkakkūṭum." (Schoff's Periplus. P. 264.) innilavaḻi vāṇikam, tāriyasukkup piṟaku, paṭippaṭiyākak kuṟintuviṭṭatu. ekipti veṟṟi koṇṭa piṉṉar, aleksāṇṭar, intiyāvukkum airōppiyāvukkum iṭiyil, periya vāṇika niliyamāka virivāka vaḷarntu viṭṭa aleksāṇṭiriyāp perunakari : uruvākkiṉāṉ. piṉṉar, ammāvīraṉ ṭayar nakaric cūṟiyāṭi aḻittu ataṉ paṇṭi vāṇikattiyum pāḻpaṭutti viṭṭāṉ. itu mēṟku nōkkip perukik koṇṭirunta intiya vaṇikap perukki ōraḷavu aṇipōṭṭut taṭuttu viṭṭatu. aleksāṇṭar iṟappiṟkup piṟaku, asariyāvic caṭṭa tiṭṭaṅkaḷ aṟṟuppōṉa kuḻappanili, āṭcipuriyat toṭaṅki viṭṭatu. pārttiyāvil, oru putiya pēraracu eḻuntatu. cittiyap paḻaṅkuṭiyiṉar, pākṭriyā (Bactria) mītāṉa, tam tiṭīrt tākkutalkaḷit toṭaṅkiviṭṭaṉar. innikaḻccikaḷ, nilavaḻi vāṇikattil aḻivukku vaḻivakuttu viṭṭaṉa. inta viḷivukaḷip payaṉpaṭuttik koṇṭu, ekiptiṉ nalaṉukkākac ceṅkaṭal vāṇika vaḷarccikku, arumuyaṟci eṭuttuk koḷḷumāṟu pilaṭelpisukku (Philadelphus) ki.pi. 285 - 246l, aḻippu viṭuttār. mutal tālami, cūyas kālvāy, ōraḷavu tiṟakkappaṭṭu, vāṇikappōkku varaviṟku vaḻi ceyyappaṭṭatu. vaṇikac cāttu cellum, taṅkum iṭaṅkaḷukkum, kuṭinīrk kiṇaṟukaḷukkum vacati ceytu tarappaṭṭa, palvēṟu vaḻittaṭaṅkaḷ, kaṭalukkum, nil natikkum iṭiyē tiṟakkappaṭṭaṉa. avvaḻittaṭaṅkaḷ muṭiyum iṭaṅkaḷilellām, tuṟikaḷāl kaṇkāṇikkappaṭṭaṉa. taṉ muntiya celvavaḷattiyum pukaḻiyum ekiptu, ōraḷavu tirumpap peṟṟu viṭṭatu. tālamiyiṉ nakarvalattil pileṭelpas nakaril intiya makaḷir, intiya vēṭṭi nāykaḷ, intiyak kākkikaḷ, oṭṭakaṅkaḷ mītu ēṟṟappaṭṭa, intiya maṇapporuḷkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.

kirēkka iṭittarakarkaḷum, naṭuvar iruvar kuḻu āṭcikku uṭpaṭṭa rōmum

kiṟittuva āṇṭu piṟappataṟku muntiya 500 āṇṭukaḷil, airōppāvuṭaṉāṉa, inta intiya vaṇikattil, kirēkkarkaḷ, perum iṭittarakarkaḷāka iruntaṉar. ulakap perunāṭu kaḷukku iṭiyilāṉa, inta virivāṉa vāṇikap paṇṭaṅ kaḷiṉ tamiḻppeyarkaḷ, kirēkkap paḻaṅkuṭiyiṉariṉ tūya kirēkka elleṉes moḻiyāl (Helenes) kaṭaṉ vāṅkap paṭṭaṉa. tiruvāḷarkaḷ sōphōkkils, (Sophocles ) arisṭōpaṉs (Aristophanes) ākiyōr eḻuttukkaḷil, avi iṭam peṟat toṭaṅkiṉa. aviyāvaṉa, tamiḻ ariciyiliruntu “oris” (Oryza) ilavaṅkappaṭṭiyām karuvāviliruntu, ‘'karpiyoṉ'’ (Karpion) tamiḻ iñcivēr, camaskiruta sriṅkiṭuvārā viliruntu (Sringivera) “jikki ‘peros” vālmiḷakik kuṟikkum tamiḻ tippiliyiliruntu ‘'pepperi;” (Peperi), itu ippōtu, airōppiya moḻikaḷil, karuppu miḷakik kuṟikkavum ākiviṭṭatu. paṇṭikkālattil kōvi māvaṭṭattil tōṇṭi eṭukkappaṭṭa tamiḻ viṭūriyattiliruntu ‘perillōs’ (Beryllos) inta nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakka kālattu urōmāṉiyar. avarkaḷuṭiya, paṇṭiya eḷiya vāḻkki muṟiyiyē kaṭippiṭittaṉar. pakaṭṭu vāḻkkiyil avā koṇṭilar. ākavē, naṭuvar iruvar kuḻu (Consular) āṭciyiṉ toṭakka kālattil, intiyap paṇṭaṅkaḷ, urōmāpuriyi aṭiyavilli. annāṭkaḷil, intiyā iṟakkumati ceytaṉavaṟṟil urōma nāṇayam iṭam peṟavilliyākavē, akkāli naṭipeṟṟa ciṟu vāṇikattilum, vāṅkiya paṇṭaṅkaḷ, urōma nāṭṭup paṇam koṭuttu vāṅkappaṭavilla.. pala nūṟu āṇṭu kālamāka iruntuvanta paḻiya taṭattilēyē vāṇikam naṭipeṟṟirukkak kūṭum. naṭipeṟṟiruntatu. āṉāl, naṭuvar iruvar kuḻu āṭciyiṉ piṟpakutivari, urōma aracu, kiḻakku nōkki virivaṭiyavilli. tuṇivumikka urōma vaṇikarkaḷukkup palmirā (Palmyra) taṉṉuṭiya katavu kaḷit tiṟakkavilli. jūliyas cīcarāl , aleksāṇ ṭiriyā, ki.mu. 47 il, kikkoḷḷappaṭṭatu eṉṟālum, kaṭal vāṇikam arāpiyappaṭakukaḷil, kaṭaṟkoḷḷik kārarkaḷiṉ piṭiyil cikkuṇṭirunta kaṭaṟkariyi oṭṭi naṭipeṟṟu vantamiyāl, akkāli vāṇikam, ciṟiya aḷavil, niliyaṟṟa taṉmiyilēyē naṭipeṟṟu vantatu. akkālattil, airōppāvukku, intiyāviliruntu, eṉṉeṉṉa ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭālum, avi. perumpālum urōmukkuc celvatilli. kirēkkattiṟkē ceṉṟaṉa. [J. R. A. S. 1904: Page : 593 - 94. Sewell] tiruvāḷar svel avar āṭcikkālattu nāṇaṅkaḷil, ituvari orucila nāṇayaṅkaḷē intiyāvil kaṇṭeṭukkappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟa uṇmi niliyi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu eḻuntaṉavām.

ikkāla kaṭṭattiṉ iṟutiyil, mattiya tarikkaṭal makkaḷ mītu, aṭuttaṭuttuk koṇṭa veṟṟiyum, naṭattiya koḷḷiyum, urōmap pēraraciṉ karuvūlattiṟku oppukkāṭṭa iyalāp peruñcelvattik koṇṭu vantu cērttaṉa. kīḻ nāṭukaḷic cērnta, matippumikka vaṇikap paṇṭaṅkaḷ mītāṉa cuvi, orē iravil vaḷarntuviṭṭatu. ciṟṟāciyā, ciriyā nāṭukaḷi veṉṟu koṇṭāric , ciṟappittu makkaḷ eṭutta viḻākkaḷ, makkaḷ ārpparippuk kuk kāraṇamākiviṭṭa peruñcelvattāl cuṭaroḷi vīcic ciṟappuṟṟa" (Scoffs periplus.page: 5] paḻiya cikkaṉ, eḷiya vāḻkkimuṟi, pakaṭṭu vāḻviṉ kavarcci muṉ niṟkamāṭṭātu, neṭuṅkālattiṟku muṉpē viṭipeṟṟuk koṇṭatu. ki.mu. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil, tūrakkiḻakku vaṇikap paṇṭaṅkaḷ, kaṇicamāṉa aḷavu, urōmi vantaṭiyalāyiṉ”, [Warminston J. R.A.S., 1940. page 4 sweli.]

urōmap pēraraciṉ toṭakkattil

akasṭas (Augustus) ekipti, ki.mu., 30il veṟṟi koṇṭu, intiyāvukkum, urōmap pēraracukkum iṭiyil, oru nēriṭik kaṭal vāṇika vaḷarccikku muyaṉṟāṉ. ki.mu. 25il, ēṟattāḻa 120 kappalkaḷ, hōrmassiliruntu (Hormus), intiyāvukkup payaṇam ceytatit tām pārttatākat, tiru. sṭirāpō (Stabo) kūṟukiṟār. [Morindle Ancient India, page 6] “intiyāviṉ palvēṟu nāṭukaḷiliruntu araciyal tūtuvarkaḷ akasṭaspāl ceṉṟaṉar. intiyat tūtuvarkaḷ, aṭikkaṭi vantatāka avaṉē kūṟukiṟāṉ” [Warmington Commerce between the Roman Empire and India. page : 35] akkālac cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷ, taṉittaṉit tūtuvarkaḷi aṉuppiyatāka, tiruvāḷar vārmiṅṭaṉ kūṟukiṟār. (Warmington Commerce between the Roman Empire and India. p. 37] itu. akasṭa s kālattil intiyā, urōmuṭaṉ naṭattiya vāṇika aḷavu mikapperiya aḷaviṉatāyp peruka vaḻi ceytatu. ivvāṇikam kuṟittut tiruvāḷar vārmiṅṭaṉ avarkaḷ, “urōmukkum intiyāvukku miṭiyilāṉa vāṇika aḷavi matippiṭṭup pārttāl, akasṭasiṉ uṇmiyāṉa toṭakkanili āṭcik kālattiṉ pōtē, atu intiyāvukkum iṅkilāntukkum iṭiyilāṉa vāṇikam eppōtum iyalpāka irukkum, anta aḷavu aṭintuviṭṭa, kuṟippiṭattakka apperuniliyi nām kaṇṇetirākak kāṇakkūṭum. toṭakka nāḷiliruntē, intiyap paṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ varukip perukkattiṟkup pēraraciṉ paṇṭaṅkaḷik koṭuttuc cariīṭu ceyya muṭiyātu pōyiṟṟu. ataṉ - viḷivu paṇṭaṅkaḷukkup patilāka, urōmāṉiyar, ceṉṟāl tirumpivarāta, aṭitta nāṇayap paṇaṅkaḷi veḷināṭṭu vāṇikam ki.mu. 600 mutal ki.pi. 14 vari 179 veḷi yēṟṟiṉar. [Warmington. Page : 381 nāṇayap paṇaṅkaḷi ēṟṟumati ceyvatil, toṭakkattil oru mōcaṭi veḷippaṭiyākavē ceyya muyalappaṭṭatu. akasṭas nāṇayattiṟkup patilāka, avaṉuṭiya tattup piḷḷikaḷām, kiyas (Gaius) lūciyas (Lucius) eṉpār uruvaṅkaḷ poṟikkappaṭṭa poṉṉukkup patil muṟṟilum poṉ mulām pūcappaṭṭa nāṇayaṅkaḷ perum eṇṇikkiyil intiyāvil kimmāṟiṉa. uḷnāṭṭu makkaḷ, pōli nāṇayaṅkaḷiliruntu, uṇmiyāṉa urōma nāṇayaṅkaḷi, iṉṉamum vēṟupirittu uṇaramāṭṭā, teṉ intiyāvōṭu naṭattum vāṇikattiṟkākavē attakiya nāṇayaṅkaḷi , iṉṉamum vēṟupirittu uṇaramāṭṭā, teṉ intiyāvōṭu naṭattum vāṇikat tiṟkākavē attakiya nāṇayaṅkaḷ taṉiyāka aṭikkappaṭṭa ṉavāka erṉasṭ (Ernst) eṇṇukiṟār" eṉak kūṟukiṟār tiruvāḷar vārmiṅṭaṉ [Warmington p. 139]. āṉāl, tamiḻarkaḷ kūrmativāyntavar eṉpati uṟuti ceytu viṭṭaṉar. kāraṇam, aṟpattaṉamāṉa accōtaṉi mīṇṭum muyalappaṭavilli. innūṟṟāṇṭu kāla aḷavil, mēli nāṭukaḷukku ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭa vaṇikap poruḷkaḷ yāvi?

uyirvilaṅkukaḷiṉ ēṟṟumati

paṇṭikkālattu intiyarkaḷ, uyir vilaṅkukaḷik kaṭalvaḻiyākap, parṣiya vaḷikuṭāviṟkum, āppirikkā viṟkum, cīṉāvukkum aṉuppi vittaṉar. iraṇṭām tālamiyiṉ mayilkaḷiyum, kiḷikaḷiyum, aṉuppi vittamikkum avarkaḷē, perumpālum poṟuppāvar. ētaṉskkuc celyūkas (Seleucus) naṉkoṭiyāka vaḻaṅkiya puli, cullāvum (Sulla) pāmpeyum (Pampey) kāṭcip poruḷākak kāṭṭiya ciṅkaṅkaḷ, oṟṟik kompuk kāṇṭāmirukam, mersalas (Mercllows) nakariṉ āṭaraṅku arppaṇikkappaṭṭapōtu, kūṇṭilō kukiyilō aṭittuk kāṭṭappaṭṭa puli, ākiya vilaṅkukaḷ, cila vaṭa intiyāviyum cila teṉṉintiyā viyum, cērntavi, nilavaḻiyāka aṉuppappaṭṭaṉavām. āṉāl, kirēkka eḻuttāḷarkaḷ palarum, taṅkaḷ nūlkaḷil kuṟippiṭṭirukkum kuraṅkukaḷ, kaṭalvaḻiyākavē ceṉṟirukka vēṇṭum. (Warmington 147, 148, 151) aleksāṇṭa r veṟṟikkup piṉṉar, caṇṭikaḷil perumaḷavil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa intiya yāṉi, payaras (Pyrrhus) eṉpāṉ, cila yāṉikaḷik ki.mu. 28l, airōppāviliruntu ittālikku iṭamāṟṟi aṉuppappaṭṭapōtu urōmāṉiyarkaḷukku mutaṉmutalāka aṟimukamākkappaṭṭatu. kārtta kiṉiyarkaḷ (Carthaginiars) avaṟṟi , āppirikka yāṉikaḷōṭu payaṉ koṇṭu avviru iṉaṅkaḷiyum paḻakka, intiya yāṉip pākarkaḷi niyamittaṉarā eṉpati, eṉṉāl uṟutiyākac colla iyalātu. āṉāl, ki.mu. 25l paṉōrmōs (Panormos) eṉum iṭattil hāsṭrupāl (Hastrubi) intiyarkaḷāl celuttappaṭṭa yāṉikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiṉāṉ eṉpatu kuṟippiṭattakkatu. urōmap pēraracōṭu naṭattiya iraṇṭām puṉik (Punic) pōril, haṉipāl, hāsṭrupāl (Hannibal, Hasdrubal) iruvarum atuvē ceytaṉar. rapiyā (Rapia) eṉumiṭattil naṭipeṟṟa pōril tālamiyiṉ lipiyā nāṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷ, āṇṭiyō cos eṉpāṉiṉ intiyap paṭikaḷ muṉ niṟka muṭiyavilli." (Warmington, Page 151)

intiya vēṭṭi nāykaḷ, veḷināṭṭavarāl, mikuvilikkuriyavāka matikkappaṭṭaṉa. “horō ṭōṭas kūṟṟuppaṭi, tam kālattic cērnta parṣiyar, pāpilōṉiyāvic cuṟṟiyirunta nāṉku periya ciṟṟūrkaḷiṉ varuvāykaḷi, intiya vēṭṭi nāykaḷiṉ uṇaviṟku eṉṟē nitiotukkam ceytuvittaṉar. parṣiyāvic cērnta intiya vēṭṭi nāykaḷum, avipōlum vēṟunāykaḷum, tālami, pilaṭelpās veṟṟi ūrvalattil kāṇappaṭṭatāka, tiru. sṭeciyās (Ctesias) kuṟippiṭukiṟār. koṭiya karaṭiyōṭu naṭattiya pōrāṭṭattil, taṉ talivaṉik kāppataṟkākat taṉ uyirik koṭutta 'ṭavrāṉ' (Tawron) eṉṟa vēṭṭināy kuṟittup pāṭiya iraṇṭu kiyaṟu nilic ceyyuḷkaḷ eḻutappaṭṭa ki.mu. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭic cērnta, nāṇaṟpul tāḷiṉālāṉa kiyeḻuttuppaṭi oṉṟum kiṭittuḷḷatu". (Warmington. Page : 149) teṉ intiya vēṭṭi nāykaḷ, avaṟṟiṉ muraṭṭuttaṉattiṟkākavē naṉku terintiruntaṉa. potuvāka avi, "katanāy" (kaṭuñciṉa nāy, virintōṭum nāy) eṉṟē aḻikkappaṭum. avi, caṅkiliyāl kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. avvāṟu nāy kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum vīṭṭiṉuḷ evarum eḷitil puka iyalātu. kāvalil attuṇik kaṭumiyāṉatu nāy, (toṭar paṭu ñamali" puṟam : 743) toṭar nāyātta tuṉṉarum kaṭinakar (perumpāṇāṟ ṟuppaṭi -125) tām kuṟittuc ceṉṟa pakivarkaḷit tappātu veṟṟi koḷḷavalla vīrarkaḷukkut tāṉ kuṟitta vilaṅkukaḷit tappātu koḷḷum vēṭṭi nāyi uvami kūṟumaḷavu teṉṉāṭṭu vēṭṭi nāykaḷ : ciṟantaṉavāka matikkappaṭṭaṉa. cel nā aṉṉa kaṭuviṟal cuṟṟamoṭu kēḷāmaṉṉā kaṭavulam pukku. (perumpāṇ - 139-140) karaṭi vēṭṭiyil vēṭṭi nāykaḷ payaṉpaṭuttap paṭutali, "uyarnta nākamaraṅkaḷ aṭarntu, kaṭantu celvataṟku aritāki viṭṭa kavartta kāṭṭu vaḻiyil, ciṟiya kaṇṇum periya ciṉṉamum uṭiya āṇ paṉṟi, cēṟṟil vīḻntu puraṇṭataṉāl cēṟu pūcap peṟṟa mutukil puḻutiyum paṭintirukka akkōlattōṭu virintu ōṭi, curukkavali viritta piḷappil vīḻntu paṭṭatāka, atu kaṇṭa aḷavē, vēṭṭi nāykaḷ, kūṭṭamāka virintu, valiyi aḻittu, paṉṟi mītu pāyntu koṉṟu koṇṭa iṟicci pōka, eñciya iṟicciyik kāṉavar koṇṭu celvar" eṉṟa naṟṟiṇi, viḷakkamākak kūṟiyuḷatu:

"pōkiya nākap pōkkarum kavalic

ciṟukaṇ paṉṟip peruñciṉa oṟuttal

cēṟāṭu irumpuṟam nīṟoṭu civaṇa,

veḷpacip paṭīiyar, moytta vaṉpu aḻīik

kōṇāy koṇṭa koḷḷik





kāṉavar peyarkkum ciṟukuṭi.

- naṟṟiṇi : 82 : 6-11)



vēṭṭuvar, kāṭṭil vāḻpavarākavum, virintōṭum māṉ kūṭṭattiyum, turattit tolikka - valla viriviṉiyuṭiya kaṭuñciṉa nāykaḷi uṭiyavarākavum kūṟappaṭṭu uḷḷaṉar. "kāṉuṟi vāḻkkik kata nāy vēṭṭuvaṉ'’ (puṟanāṉūṟu : 33 :1) "māṉkaṇam tolicciya kaṭuvicik kata nāy. vēṭṭuvaṉ". (puṟam : 205 : 8-9) vēṭṭikkuc ceṉṟa talivaṉ, vēṭṭi muṭivuṟṟatum, vēṭṭi mēṟkoṇṭu mūṅkiṟkāṭṭiṉuḷ nuḻintu paravik kiṭakkum taṉ vēṭṭuvat tuṇivarkaḷiyum - vēṭṭi nāykaḷiyum, vēṭṭi muṭivuṟṟatu, vāruṅkaḷ eṉa aḻikkum kuṟippōṭu kompu eṉum ūtu karuviyi ūti oli eḻuppuvaṉ:

muntiya nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil toṭaṅkappaṭṭuviṭṭa kiḷikaḷ, mayilkaḷiṉ ēṟṟumati inta nūṟṟāṇṭilum toṭarntatu. atil aiyamilli, kirēkkarkaḷāl ‘aspis’ (Aspis) eṉa aḻikkappaṭum nākappāmpukaḷ, malippāmpu kaḷ, uḷḷiṭṭa pāmpukaḷ, ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭa piṟa uyiriṉaṅkaḷām intiyāviliruntu koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa oṉpatu aṭi nīḷam pāmpu oṉṟi sṭrāpō ekiptil pārttuḷḷār. (Warmington. Page : 157)

vilaṅkutaru poruḷkaḷ

intiyāviliruntu ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭa mikamika mukkiyamāṉa poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟu tantam. kirēkkarkaḷ, taṅkaḷ nākarīkattiṉ mutirnta niliyil, uruvaccilikaḷil, āṭiyāl maṟikkappaṭāta puṟattōṟṟap pakutikaḷukku, atip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. muttukkaḷ, mutaṉmutalil, jūkurtaṉ (Jugurthine) pōriṉpōtu, urōmukku aṟimukam ceyyappaṭṭu, pāmpē (Pompey) eṉpavarāl, ittāliyiliruntu koṇṭuvarappaṭṭa peruṅkuviyalāl, makkaḷ naṉku aṟinta oru poruḷāka ākkappaṭṭatu. "tālamikaḷiṉ karuvūlaṅkaḷ, akasṭasāl, tirumpak koṇṭuvarappaṭṭapōtu, appēraraciṉ vīḻcciyil, avi, ellōrkkum kiṭikkum eḷiya poruḷāyiṉa. muttukkaḷ kiṭippatu aritākiviṭṭa cicirōviṉ (Cicero) - kālattil, iṉṟiya nāṇayam eṭṭāyiram pavuṉ matippuḷḷa oru muttu hesōpṉaṉ (AesopwS) makaṉāl, metellā (Metelia) kātiliruntu kaḻaṟṟappaṭṭu, vaṟaṭcik kālattil oru perum paṇattokiyi viḻuṅkiṉōm eṉṟa maṉaniṟivu koḷvāṉ vēṇṭi, vēṇṭumeṉṟē tiṭṭamiṭṭu viḻuṅkappaṭṭatu. (Warmington - Page : 163 168169) kiḷiyōpāṭrā, taṉ matuvil, parukuvataṟku muṉṉar, muttukkaḷik karippāḷ eṉpatu naṉku terinta oṉṟu. arakku vaṇṇam ūṭṭappeṟṟa paruttikaḷ parṣiyāvukku aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa. taṉṉuṭiya kālattil pāracīka aracaṉukku, intiyarkaḷāl cevvaṇṭiliruntu peṟṟa oru vaṇṇak kalaviyāl vaṇṇam ūṭṭappaṭṭa parutti nūl aṉuppappaṭṭatāka, stēciyās (Ctesias) kuṟittu vittuḷḷār (Warmington Page : 178).

ekiptiya āvaṇaṅkaḷ, paṭṭik kuṟippiṭavilli. ākavē, paṭṭu, tāriyas (Dariws), jerses (Xeroxes)kaḷiṉ pēraraciṉ mūlamākavē, mattiya tarikkaṭal pakutiyi aṭintatu. ataṉ uṟpatti paṟṟiya cariyāṉa viḷakkatti arisṭāṭṭil koṭuttuḷḷār. paṭṭu tārkaḷil cuṟṟappaṭṭu, arisṭāṭṭil kālattiṟku muṉpē iṟakkumati ceyyappaṭṭatu. ṭayarilum (Tyre) maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷilum, uṭikaḷākat tikkappaṭṭa niliyil piḷiṉiyiṉ kūṟṟuppaṭi, "oru peṇṇiṉ uṭali mūṭum atē niliyil, avaḷuṭiya iyaṟki aḻaki veḷippaṭuttavallatāṉa , uḷḷiruppati veḷikkāṭṭum melliya vali nikar āṭikaḷ neyyappaṭṭaṉa. (Schoff Perplus, Page: 264-265) kirēkka urōmāṉiya eḻuttāḷarkaḷiṭiyē, paruttikkum paṭṭukkumiṭiyē ciṟitu karuttukkuḻappam iruntatu. avarkaḷ, avviraṇṭiyumē, "maram taru vilaṅkumayir", Treewool) eṉa aḻittuḷḷaṉar. maraviṉam tarum poruḷkaḷ

intiya āṭikaḷ, pālastīṉiyarāl, perumaḷavil iṟakkumati ceyyappaṭṭataṟkiṭiyil, intiyap pañcu, aracu nūṟpu ālikaḷiyum, cāyat toḻiṟcālikaḷiyum, pēraracukaḷ, niṟuviyirunta oru cila matakurukkaḷālum ceyalpaṭṭuvanta, ekiptiṟku eṭuttuc cellappaṭṭatu. ekiptiyar, paruttiyiyum, nār iḻikaḷiyum oṉṟu kalantu parutti ūṭiḻiyākavum, nār, pāvu iḻiyākavum, koṇṭa āṭikaḷi neytaṉar. intiyap pañcu, ekiptil, palvēṟu puṉitap paṇikaḷukkum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. palvaṇṇam ūṭṭappaṭṭa parutti nūlkaḷ paṉōpilisil uḷḷa (Panoplis) mempis (Memphis) arukē kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. annūlkaḷil orucila, avaṟṟiṉ vaṭivamippil, intiyak kūṟukaḷik koṇṭuḷḷaṉa."(Warmington Page 212)

ilavaṅkam, ekiptiṟkāka eṉṟē, ciṟappāka otukkappaṭṭatu pōlavē, miḷaku, pāpilōṉiyā, pāracīka vaḷikuṭā , vāṇikaṅkaḷil, vaḻakkattiṟku mēlāṉa tēvippaṭuporuḷāka iruntatu. ataṉuṭiya viṟuviṟuppāṉa peruntēvi, ‘tāriyas'’ (Darius) āṭciyiṉ kīḻ, pāracīkap pēraracu virivaṭintapōtu ēṟpaṭṭatu eṉak karutuvataṟkuk kāraṇam uḷatu. inta vāṇikam, teṉ intiyak kappalkaḷil, kaṭal vaḻiyāka naṭipeṟṟatu. nila vaḻiyāka aṉṟu, etōppiyariṉ (Aethiopians) mēṉi niṟattip peritum otta niṟam vāyntavar. teṟkil, pāracīkattiliruntu veku tolivukku appāl kuṭivāḻpavar, tāriyasākku eṉṟum paṇiyātavar eṉṟa aḷavilēyē herōṭaṭṭas, (Herodotas) tirāviṭarkaḷip paṟṟi aṟintuḷḷār, (Scoff’s Periplus . Page 213) pārttiyā (Parthia) viṉ kuṟukkāka naṭipeṟṟa nila vāṇikamum, tālamikaḷāl ūkkam ūṭṭappaṭṭa kaṭal vāṇikamum, aṟutiyiṭṭuk kūṟamāṭṭā. āṇṭāṇṭu kālamāka ēṟkaṉavē, naṭipeṟṟuvanta ilavaṅkam, maṟṟum maṇapporuḷkaḷiṉ vāṇikattiṉ, perumaḷavilāṉa | perukkiṟku vaḻi ceytu viṭṭaṉa. urōmukkut tēvippaṭum miḷaki, ikkāla kaṭṭattil, poyiṉīṣiyarkaḷum, kārttikiṉiyarkaḷum vaḻaṅkivantaṉar. ki.mu. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭic cērntavarum, intiya maruttiyal - muṟikaḷik kiyāṇṭavarumāṉa 'ippō kirāṭs' eṉpār, miḷaki, "intiya nivāraṇi" eṉa aḻikkiṟār. (Warmington. Page 182) atu, kuḷir kāycca lukkum, veppakkāyccalukkum, payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. makkaḷ, marapuvaḻip paḻakka vaḻakkam kuṟitta ārāycciyil, 'herōṭōṭas' (Herodotus) avar kaḷukku, itu paṟṟik kuṟippiṭuvataṟku etuvum illi eṉṟālum, ki.mu. nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭil, 'tiyōp rasṭas' (Theophrastus) ati oru maruntāka aṟintuḷḷār. ṭiyōs kōriṭas (Dioscorides) karuppu, veḷḷi, vālmiḷakukaḷukkiṭiyilāṉa vēṟupāṭṭi uṇarntuḷḷār. (Scoff's Periplus. Page 213) iñciyum mēli nāṭukaḷi aṭintu maruntākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. āṉāl, atu ōr intiya uṟpattip poruḷ eṉpati, mēlināṭṭavar uṇarntu koḷḷā vakiyil, veṟṟikaramāka maṟittuviṭṭa arāpiya iṭittarakarkaḷāl koṇṭu cellappaṭṭatu. (Warmington Page: 184) ki.mu. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭil, ēṉ, ataṟku muṉpēyum eḷḷeṇṇey, kirēkkarkaḷukku naṉku terintiruntatu. atu intiyāviliruntu aṉuppappaṭṭatu. tālamikaḷ kālattil, atu matikkattakka mukkiyattuvam uṭiyatākiviṭṭatu. (Warmington Page : 206) kirēkkak kōyilkaḷil tēṅkāykaḷ, aticayak kāṭcip poruḷāka vikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa vātaliṉ, kirēkkattiṟkut tēṅkāykaḷum aṉuppappaṭṭatākat terikiṟatu. (Warmington Page : 217) sēpōkkels (Sophocells). arici aṭikaḷ paṟṟip pēcukiṟār. ākavē, kirēkkar, ariciyiyum, ataṉ peyariyum tamiḻnāṭṭiliruntu peṟṟaṉar. palvēṟu payaṉ karuti maraṅkaḷum ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. mitriṭēṭs mītu (Mithridates) koṇṭa veṟṟi kuṟittu eṭutta viḻāvil intiyak karuṅkāli marattik kāṭcip poruḷākak kāṭṭiṉāṉ pāmpē (Pompey). intiyak karuṅkāli maram vāṇikam neṭitu nili peṟṟirunta oṉṟu. ituvum, maṟṟa maraṅkaḷum pāracīka vaḷikuṭāp pakutikku, intiya vaṇikarkaḷāl, koṇṭu cellappaṭṭaṉa. karuṅkāli, nāṟkāli, mēci pōlum taṭṭumuṭṭup poruḷ kaḷukkākavum uruvaccilikaḷukkākavum payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭatu. intiyāviliruntu, kuṟippāka malapār, karṉāṭakam, tiruvāṅkūr maṟṟum vēṟu cila iṭaṅkaḷiliruntu vanta tēkku maraṅkaḷik koṇṭu, pāracīka vaḷikuṭāvil āṭkaḷ kappalkaḷ kaṭṭuvati tiyōprasṭas kāṭṭu kiṟār. (Warmington Page 213-214) “maṭaraṭā'’ (Madarata) eṉa. aṟiyappaṭṭa, titta paṭakukaḷ eṉa aḻikkappaṭṭa, arāpiyariṉ paṭakukaḷ, teṉṉi nāriṉāl, atāvatu tēṅkāy maṭṭikaḷiliruntu eṭutta nāriṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. (Scor’s Periplus Page : 154) ikkāla kaṭṭattil palvēṟu vakiyaṉavāṉa intiyak kaṟkaḷ, mēli nāṭukaḷukku ēṟṟumati ceyyappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟālum inta vāṇikam; aṭutta kāla kaṭṭattilēyē periya aḷavip peṟṟatu.

armiṉiyāvil ōr intiya vaḻipāṭṭu muṟi

mēṟku āciyāvuṭaṉāṉa inta vāṇikattiṉ oru viyattaku viḷivu iṅkē viḷakkappaṭum. atu, armīṉiyāvil, ōr intiya vaḻipāṭṭu muṟiyiṉ nuḻivākum. armīṉiyāvic cērnta ‘arasciṭē” (Arsacide) marapiṉ mutal maṉṉaṉākiya, mutalām vaḷarṣak (Valarshak) (ki.mu149. 127) kālattil, iru intiyat talivarkaḷ, iyūpraṭs āṟṟiṉ mēṟkuk kariyil, vāṉ (Van) eṉṟa ērikkut teṟkil, oru putiya kuṭiyiruppi niṟuvi, kiruṣṇaṉ palatēvaṉ (armīṉiya moḻiyil muṟiyē kicaṉi (Gisani), temeter (Demeter) vaḻipāṭṭiṟkākak kōyilkaḷik kaṭṭiṉar. itu vaṭa intiyāviliruntu vanta, āriya vaḻipāṭṭu muṟiyiṉ perukkākumā? mika uṟutiyāka illi . kiruṣṇa palatēvarkaḷ, tirumāliṉ avatāraṅ kaḷām eṉap purāṇaṅkaḷ kūṟiṉālum, ivviru kaṭavuḷkaḷiyum orucēra vaḻipaṭum taṉivaḻipāṭṭumuṟi, vaṭa intiyāvil ekkālattilum iruntatilli. āṉāl, paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, itaṟkāṉa eṇṇaṟṟa akaccāṉṟukaḷi aḷikkiṉṟaṉa. ivarkaḷil, muṉṉavaṉ māyōṉ eṉṟa peyarāl, mulli nilakkaṭavuḷāvaṉ kalappiyip paṭikkalaṉākavum, paṉiyik koṭiyākavum, koṇṭa, veṇṇiṟak kaṭavuḷ eṉum poruḷil vāliyōṉ allatu veḷḷiyōṉ eṉappaṭum piṉṉavaṉ toṭakkattil uḻavuttoḻil mutalil toṭaṅkappaṭṭa mullikkum, marutattukkum iṭippaṭṭa nilattiṉ kaṭavuḷāvaṉ. muṉṉavaṉ karuppu vaṇṇamum, piṉṉavaṉ, veḷḷi vaṇṇamum, tamiḻp puvalarkaḷiṉ viruppattiṟkuriya aṇi nalaṅkaḷām. kaṭalil vaḷarum valampuric caṅki okkum niṟam vāynta, koli virumpum kalappiyiyum paṉikkoṭiyiyum uṭiyavaṉ" eṉṟum. "mācu pōkak kaḻuvappaṭṭa aḻakiya nīlamaṇi pōlum niṟam vāynta, vāṉuṟa uyarnta karuṭakkoṭi uṭiya, eṉṟum veṟṟiyē virumpuvōṉ" eṉṟum, avarkaḷ muṟiyē pārāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉar.

"kaṭalvaḷar purivaṉi puriyum mēṉi,

aṭal vem nāñcil paṉikkoṭi yōṉum,

maṇṇuṟu tirumaṇi puriyum mēṉi,

viṇuyar puṭkoṭi viṟal vey yōṉum."

puṟanāṉūṟu : 56:3-6





"pāl pōlum veṇṇiṟam vāyntavaṉ. paṉik koṭiyuṭiyāṉ" eṉṟum, "nīlamaṇiyiṉ niṟam vāynta tirumēṉiyuṭiyāṉ; āriyap paṭiyuṭiyāṉ" eṉṟum avarkaḷ mēlum pārāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉar.

"pāl niṟa uruviṉ paṉikkoṭi yōṉum,

nīlniṟa uruviṉ nēmi yōṉam"

puṟanāṉūṟu : 58 : 14 - 15





ivviruvarkaḷiṉ mēṉi niṟaṅkaḷukkiṭiyilāṉa māṟupāṭu, uvami aṇikaḷākap paravalāka āḷap paṭṭuḷatu. eṭuttukkāṭṭukku : ‘'māyōṉ aṉṉa māl varik kavāaṉ : vāliyōṉ aṉṉa vayaṅku veḷḷaruvi, (naṟṟiṇi : 32:7-2) irāmāvatāram tavirttu , tirumāliṉ avatāraṅkaḷil, vēṟu etuvum paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷāl kuṟippiṭappaṭavilliyātaliṉ, ivviruvarum, teṉ intiyāvil, tirumāliṉ avatāraṅkaḷāka aṟimukam āṉavarallar). palatēvaṉ, eppōtum paṉañcāṟu paruki veṟikoṇṭu kiṭappaṉ. paṉimalinta pakutiyil, avaṉ vaḷarccikku atu poruntum. paṉi, kaṅki veḷiyil vaḷarvatilliyākavē, palatēva vaḻipāṭu kaṅkik kariyil uruvākiyiruttal iyalātu. kaṭalukku veku tolivil allāta, teṉṉāṭṭu āṟṟup paḷḷattākkup pakutiyilēyē avvaḻipāṭṭumuṟi tōṉṟiyirukka vēṇṭum.

palatēvaṉukku eṉak kaṭṭappaṭṭa kōyilkaḷ vaṭanāṭṭil iruppatāka nām kēḷvippaṭṭatu illi. āṉāl, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattilum, maturiyilum avaṉukkāṉa kōyilkaḷ, kiruṣṇaṉ kōyili aṭuttaṭuttuk kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

"vālvaḷi mēṉi vāliyōṉ kōyilum

nīlamēṉi neṭiyōṉ kōyilum”

- cilappatikāram : 5 : 71 - 72





pukar veḷḷi nākartam kōṭṭam”

cilappatikāram : 9:10.





“pala tēvaṉ tirumāliṉ paṭukkiyām araviṉ, maṟu avatāram eṉpa. avvaravu nākarkaḷit tammōṭu iraṇṭaṟak koṇṭu nākarākiviṭavē, atuvum tirumāl vaḻipāṭṭu neṟiyil iṭam peṟṟuviṭṭatu. ivi pukār nakarattuk kōyilkaḷ, maturiyil "mēḻi valaṉ uyartta veḷḷi nakaramum” (cilappatikāram : 14 : 9) ivveṭuttuk kāṭṭukkaḷ ellām, piṟkāla ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntatu eṉṟālum, akkāla kaṭṭattilum, vaṭanāṭṭil palatēvaṉukkuk kōyil illi. ivveḷḷik kaṭavuḷ, teṟkiltāṉ mutaṉmutalil eḻuntāṉ eṉṟa muṭiviṟku valivūṭṭukiṟatu. tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ cila cātiyariṭiyē 'veḷḷiyaṉ", veḷḷāṉ" eṉṟa peyarkaḷ, iṉṟum paravalāka iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. veḷḷik kaṭavuḷ vaḻipaṭappeṟṟu, avaṉ peyar, avaṉi vaḻipaṭuvār makkaḷukku iṭappaṭṭa kālam toṭṭu vaḻakkil vantirukka vēṇṭum.) ikkōyilkaḷē allāmal kiruṣṇaṉ, palatēvaṉ ākiya iruvariṉ teyvat tirumēṉikaḷ aṭuttaṭuttu iṭam peṟṟirukkum kōyilkaḷum uḷa. attakiya kōyilkaḷuḷ oṉṟākiya, maturikku veku tolivil illāta tirumāliruñ cōlikkōyil, paripāṭalil, patiṉintāvatu pāṭṭil mika virivāka viḷakkappaṭṭuḷatu. atu, eṭuttukkāṭṭuvataṟku iyalā, neṭum pāṭṭu, mēlum nām ārāyntu koṇṭirukkum kālattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭa kālattic cērntatu. appāṭṭil viḷakkappaṭṭirukkum palatēvaṉ teyvattirumēṉi, ippōtu kāṇāmal pōyviṭṭatu. tirumāl vaḻipāṭṭāḷariṉ matippīṭṭil, palatēvaṉ, kuṟinta nilikkut taḷḷappaṭṭuviṭṭa kālattil, akkōyil iṭittup putitākak kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkak kūṭum. iṉṟum aḻiyāmal irukkum orucila kōyilkaḷil, palatēvaṉ tirumēṉi, iṉṟum niṟkiṟatu. avi, piṟkālac camaya veṟiyarkaḷiṉ aḻiviliruntu tappiviṭṭaṉa pōlum.

anta iṇinta vaḻipāṭṭu muṟi, armīṉiyāvukku vaṭanāṭṭiliruntu ceṉṟatā, teṉṉāṭṭiliruntu ceṉṟatā eṉpatu viṭiyiṟukka muṭiyāta nili irukkumpōtu, atu koṭiya vaṉmuṟic ceyal oṉṟu mūlam aḻivuṟṟup pōyviṭṭatu. kiṟittuvac camayap piracārakar, muṉivar kirikōri eṉpavar (St. Gregory) ki. pi. nāṉkāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭil, puṟaccamayattiṟku etirāṉa tammuṭiya koṭiya veṟuppu kāraṇamāka, viṣap (Vishap) eṉum iṭattilirunta intiyar kuṭiyiruppiṟku etirākak kiṟittuvap peruṅkūṭṭam oṉṟiṟkut talimi tāṅkic ceṉṟār. ataṉ viḷivāka nikaḻnta caṇṭiyil, talimi arccakarkaḷ veṭṭit taḷḷappaṭṭaṉar. teyvat tirumēṉikaḷ uṭittu noṟukkappaṭṭaṉa. kōyilkaḷ - iṭittut tarimaṭṭamākkappaṭṭaṉa. avi niṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷil mātā kōyilkaḷ, kaṭṭappaṭṭu, ciluvikaḷ niṟuvappaṭṭaṉa. āṅkuk kuṭivāḻntavarkaḷil, aiyāyirattukkum mēṟpaṭṭavar kiṟistavarāka matam māṟiviṭṭaṉar. tam camayak kōṭpāṭṭik kiviṭāmal viṭāppiṭiyāka irunta arccakarkaḷiṉ makkaḷ nāṉūṟṟu muppatteṭṭup pērum, kōyil paṇiyāḷarkaḷum, moṭṭi aṭikkap paṭṭu, tolināṭukaḷukkut turattappaṭṭu viṭṭaṉar. (J. R. A. S. 1904; Page : 309 - 314. Kennedy)

tūrakkiḻakku nāṭukaḷ uṭaṉāṉa teṉṉintiyak kaṭal vāṇikam, iṉṟum toṭarntu naṭipeṟṟuvarum makkaḷ kuṭi peyarcciyiṉ toṭakkattiṟku vaḻivakuttatu. paṭittuṇittalivar, tiruvāḷar keriṉi (Gerine) avarkaḷ, kiṟittuva āṇṭu toṭakkattiṟkup pal, nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷukku muṉpē, vaṭa intiyāviliruntum, teṉ intiyāviliruntum, vaṇikarkaḷum putitu kāṇum uḷḷārvam mikkavarkaḷumāṉa ipperum veḷḷam, intō cīṉāvuḷ pāyat toṭaṅki, nilavaḻiyāka, varmā mūlam, attīvakaṟpattiṉ vaṭapakutiyiyum, kaṭalvaḻiyāka, ataṉ teṉ kaṭaṟpakutiyiyum aṭintu, āṅkē, taṅkaḷ kuṭiyiruppukaḷiyum, vaṇikaniliyaṅkaḷiyum niṟuviṉa” eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār. [J. R. A. S. 1904. p. 234 - 37) paḻiya intiyarkaḷiṉ innaṭamāṭṭam kuṟitta virivāṉa viḷakkattiṟku, mēlē kūṟiyatik kāṇka.) pirāmaṇarkaḷum puttarkaḷumākiya āriyar, paṇṭiya intiya nākarīkattiyum, tamiḻar, vāṇikattiyum iṅkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟaṉar. cuvarṇa pūmiyiṉ ellikku appāl, paṇṭikkālattil periya vaṇika miyamāka viḷaṅkiya mārtāṉ (Martaban) eṉum iṭattiṟku aṇittākat, "takkōlam' eṉṟa iṭatti , milintā paṉha (Milindapanha) kuṟippiṭukiṟatu. avviṭattiṉ peyar, ceṉṉikku aṇittāka, ki.pi. pattām nūṟṟāṇṭil oru periya pōr , naṭipeṟṟa takkōlam eṉṟa ūriṉ peyari niṉivūṭṭuvatāka uḷatu.

cīṉāviṉ āṉ marapup pēraracar (Han Emperor) huti (Woti) eṉpāṉ, iṉṟiya turukkarkaḷiṉ muṉṉōrkaḷākiya hiyuṅ-nu (Hiung - Nu) eṉpārik kōpi (Gobi) pālivaṉattiṟku appāl turattiviṭṭāṉ. ataṉpiṉṉar, airōppāvuṭaṉāṉa cīṉap paṭṭuvāṇikam, periya aḷaviṉi eṭṭiviṭṭatu. ivvāṇikam mēṟkoṇṭa vaḻikaḷil oṉṟu, cūyas vaḷikuṭāviliruntu vanta yavaṉavaṇikarkaḷ, cīṉappaṭṭit tamiḻ iṭittarakarkaḷiṭamiruntu peṟṟukkoḷḷum, tamiḻnāṭṭuk kaṭaṟkariyi oṭṭiya vaḻiyām. parmā, malēyā, cīṉak kaṭaṟkari nāṭukaḷuṭaṉāṉa kaṭal vāṇikam, ikkāla kaṭṭattil vaḷarntu perukiṟṟu. "kiḻakkiṟku aṉuppappaṭṭa mika mukkiyamāṉa paṇṭam, miḷaku. miḷaku kuṟitta toṭarnta tēvi, urōmil eḻuvataṟku muṉpē, cīṉāvil iṭaṅkoṇṭiruntatu eṉpatu ūkikkak kūṭiyatē. malapār kaṭaṟkarikkuk, ki.mu. iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭil, ēṉ, ataṟkum muṉpāka, 'jaṅ' (Jwnk) eṉappaṭum taṭṭiyāṉa aṭippākam uṭiya cīṉak kappalkaḷ ceṉṟuvantataṟku ituvum oru kāraṇam. (Scoff's Periplus. Page 213-14) tūrakkiḻakkiṟku, miḷaku maṭṭumallāmal naṟumaṇap pukitaru poruḷkaḷum aṉuppappaṭṭu, paṭṭiṟkum, carkkarikkumāka, kimāṟappaṭṭaṉa.





tamiḻ ilakkiyattuḷ camaskiruta nākarīkattiṉ mutal nuḻivu





camaskiruta nuḻivu

akattiyaṉār, tammuṭiya tamiḻilakkaṇatti iyaṟṟiya pōtu, camaskiruta nākarikam, tamiḻilakkiyatti, mutaṉmutalil pātikkat talippaṭṭatu. tammuṭiya maliyiṭit tavappaḷḷiyil, uṇarccikku āṭpaṭāta aṟivu nili kaṭanta, tavaneṟi mēṟkoḷvatik kāṭṭilum, pāṇṭi nāṭṭut tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷik kaṟpatāl uḷavām uṇarccipūrva aṉupava vāḻkkiyāl peritum īrkkap paṭṭa, potiyamaliyil, iṭaṅkoṇṭirunta inta akattiyar, akattiyariṉ tapōvaṉattic cērntavarkaḷil, mikamikap piṟpaṭṭavarāvar. piṟkālap purāṇak katikaḷil, ivar, ivarukku muṟpaṭṭa akattiyar aṉivaruṭiya motta uruvamākkappaṭṭu, avvakattiyarkaḷiṉ ceyal pukaḻ aṉittum, inta akattiyariṭattilēyē koṇṭu kuvikkappaṭṭaṉa. avar peyaric cūḻak kaṭṭiviṭap paṭṭirukkum katikaḷi nām nampuvatāṉāl, tamiḻilakkiyam kūṟum anta akattiyaṉār, nalluṇarvukku appāṟpaṭṭa poṟāmi, kaṭṭuppaṭuttalākāk kaṭuṅkōpaṅkaḷukku āṭpaṭak kūṭiya cātāraṇa maṉitarāvar. katikaḷ, orupuṟam irukka, tamiḻiṉ moḻinaṭi iyalpukaḷil nuṇukkamāṉa ārāycciyi mēṟkoṇṭu akattiyaṉār, ammoḻi paṟṟiya ilakkaṇatti iyaṟṟiṉār eṉpatu, uṇmi nikaḻcciyām. avvāṟu iyaṟṟumpōtu, camaskiruta ilakkaṇak kūṟukaḷellām, utāraṇattiṟkup peyarc coṟkaḷōṭu iṇiyum ēḻu allatu eṭṭu vēṟṟumi urupukaḷ pōlvaṉa, ellā moḻikaḷukkum uḷḷa potu niliyām eṉa avar karutukiṟār. ulaka moḻikaḷ, oṉṟōṭoṉṟu uṟavu koḷḷāta, palvēṟu moḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷic cērntavi. aṭippaṭi amippilēyē avi, oṉṟōṭoṉṟu māṟupaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ataṉāl, oru moḻiyiṉ collilakkaṇa amippukkuḷ, vēṟu oru moḻiyiṉ collilakkaṇa amippit tiṇippatu iyalātu eṉpaṉa pōlum uṇmikaḷ akattiyaṉārkkut teriyā. camaskirutam virivāṉa , collilakkaṇa vakikaḷik koṇṭa taṉakkē uriya, colluru māṟṟu moḻi. āṉāl, tamiḻ, taṉi iru coṟkaḷ iṇintē tokic coṟkaḷi uruvākkavalla eḷiya teḷivāṉa collilakkaṇattiyuṭiya , oru moḻiyākum. camaskiruta muṟṟut toṭar vākkiyaṅkaḷil, collukkuc col uḷḷa , coṟṟoṭar uṟavu, accoṟkaḷiṉ ilakkaṇa uṟavu uṇartta, accoṟkaḷiṉ īṟṟil iṇikkappaṭum vikutikaḷiṉāl maṭṭumē uṇaralākum. ātaliṉ, coṟkaḷ, vākkiyattil eṅku vēṇṭumāṉālum iṭam koḷḷalām eṉpatu, ammoḻiniliyākat, tamiḻ coṟkaḷi vēṇṭiyavāṟellām, iṭam māṟṟac ceyya iyalā, uṟutiyāṉa coṟṟoṭar niliyiṉik koṇṭuḷatu. camaskiruta moḻiyiṉ ivvikutikaḷ, tam niliyil poruḷ uṇarttum collāka iṭam peṟa māṭṭār collīṟukaḷiṉāl ākkappaṭṭaṉavām ātaliṉ, taṅkaḷukkeṉap poruḷ illātaṉa. āṉāl, poruḷ uṇar coṟkaḷil, uṟaviṉi uṇarat tuṇipurivaṉa ākum. ivipōlum collīṟṟu vikutikaḷi ēṟṟuk koḷvataṉāl, vērccol, veṟum mūlamāka māṟi, am mūlamum taṉakkuttāṉē taṭam kāṇamāṭṭā mikapperiya māṟutalukku āḷākkiviṭukiṟatu. ivvakiyil tat' eṉṟa mūlam, 'am eṉṟa īṟu ēṟṟu "tēsām" eṉa ākiṟatu. haṉ" eṉṟa viṉivērccol peṟum pala urumāṟṟaṅkaḷil oṉṟu, "jakhāṉa eṉpatu. atupōla ṭah" eṉpataṉ urumāṟṟam, aṭṭok" eṉpatām. ivi, vikutikaḷ iṇivāl, coṟkaḷiṉ iyalum uruvamum vēṟupaṭum moḻikaḷiṉ aṭiyāḷaṅkaḷām. tamiḻ, itaṟku māṟākat, taṉi iru coṟkaḷ iṇintē tokic coṟkaḷi uruvākkavalla oru colliyal muṟiyiṉik koṇṭa oru moḻiyām. tamiḻil poruḷ vēṟupaṭuttum colluṟuppukaḷāka varuvaṉa 1. ammoḻiyil tamakkeṉat taṉipporuḷum, taṉi āṭciyum uṭiyavāya, ēḻām vēṟṟumi urupukaḷ eṉa vaḻaṅkappaṭum, oṭu, poruṭṭu, atu, uṭiya, iṭi, tali, tici eṉum iviyum, ivi pōlvaṉavum. tamiḻilakkaṇa āciriyarkaḷ ivaṟṟic collīṟukaḷ - collurupukaḷ eṉap poruttamuṟa aḻippar. 2. ciṟitē urupu tirinta, oṭu, il, atu, āṟām vēṟṟumip paṉmi uruvām, avi eṉṟa paṉmic cuṭṭiṉ tiripu vaṭivām a eṉpaṉa. entac coṟkaḷiṉ tiripu vaṭivam eṉpati uṇaralākā vakiyil tirintirukkum, vēṟṟumi urupukaḷ, ai., eṉṟa iraṇṭum peyarccol, eḻuvāy, viḷi allāta vēṟṟumikaḷil paṉmi vaṭivu peṟum camaskiruta muṟikku muṟṟilum māṟākat tamiḻil, peyarc collukkum, ivvēṟṟumi urupukaḷukkum iṭiyil, paṉmi kuṟikkum kuṟiyīṭukaḷ, "avarkaḷukku" eṉpatu pōl aḻikkappaṭum uṇmi nikaḻcciyāl tam mūlaccol aṟiyāvāṟu tirintuviṭṭa ai , ku eṉṟa ivvurupukaḷum ōraḷavu taṉittaṉmi vāyntaṉavē eṉpatu uṇarappaṭum.

tamiḻum, camaskirutamum, ivvāṟu amippu niliyil, muḻukka muḻukka muraṇpaṭṭiruppatāl, camaskiruta ilakkaṇa muṭipukaḷ, tamiḻilakkaṇattiṟkuc cariyākap poruntivārā. āṉāl, akattiyaṉār, camaskiruta moḻik kūṟṟuṇmikaḷ, tamiḻilum iṭam peṟṟuḷḷa eṉak karutukiṟār. ataṉāl, camaskirutattil uḷḷatupōl, tamiḻilum ēḻu vēṟṟumikaḷ irukka vēṇṭum eṉak karutukiṟār. vēṟṟumi urupēṟṟa camaskirutap peyarc coṟkaḷit tamiḻc coṟṟoṭarkaḷāka moḻipeyarkkiṟār. accoṟṟoṭarkaḷi, eḻuvāyp poruḷil varum, peyarccoṟkaḷ, avaṟṟit toṭarntu varuvaṉa eṉa iraṇṭākap pirittu, avviraṇṭām pirivukaḷit tamiḻ vēṟṟumi urupukaḷākak koḷkiṟār. pāṇiṉiyip piṉpaṟṟit tamiḻil, avar, ēḻu vēṟṟumikaḷik kāṇkiṟār. cariyākac colvatāṉāl, uruvākkukiṟār. avar māṇavar tolkāppiyaṉār, aintiram vaḻic camaskiruta ilakkaṇa āciriyarkaḷip piṉpaṟṟi, avaṟṟi eṭṭu ākkukiṟār. kātalipiṉ' eṉpatu "kātali" eṉpataṉ vēṟṟumi urupēṟṟa peyarāyiṉ, kātali etir" eṉpatum vēṟumi urupēṟṟa peyarākātō? immuṟiyil kaṇakkiṭṭāl, tamiḻ moḻiyil uḷḷa peyarccoṟkaḷiliruntu, peṟalākum viṉiyeccap peyareccat toṭarkaḷ ettaṉi uḷavō, attaṉi vēṟṟumikaḷit tamiḻp peyarkaḷ koṇṭirukka vēṇṭum.

tamiḻil camaskiruta ēḻu vēṟṟumikaḷi iṟakkumati ceytatu pōlavē, camaskiruta ceyappāṭṭu viṉiyiyum avar, iṟakkumati ceytuḷḷār. ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟāviṉi ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟiyaviṉi ākiya ellā viṉic coṟkaḷum, camaskirutattil aṭiyum, uṟutiyāṉa col tiripē. ammoḻi, ceyappāṭṭu nili, ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟāviṉikaḷ, ceyappāṭṭu viṉiyāka māṟum poḻutu, avviṉikaḷ, "ciṅkam kollappaṭṭatu" eṉpatil uḷḷatupōl, ceyappaṭu poruḷic, ceyviṉi mutalām eḻuvāyāka māṟṟum appaṇiyiṉic ceykiṟatu. viṉi mutalām eḻuvāy teriyāta pōtu, allatu avviṉi mutal kuṟippiṭat tēviyillātapōtu, allatu ceyappaṭuporuḷ vaṟpuṟutti uṇarttat tēvippaṭum pōtu ivvāṟu vaḻaṅkuvatu, payaṉuṭiyatākiṟatu. ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟiya viṉikaḷukkuc ceyappāṭṭut tirinta vaṭivaṅkaḷ tarappaṭumpōtu, itu pōlum aṭippaṭip payaṉ etuvum kāṇappaṭuvatilli; eṉṟālum ceyappāṭṭuviṉi āṭciyāl, evvitac ciṟappup poruḷum peṟappaṭuvatilliyākavum, camaskirutattil, ceyappaṭuporuḷ kuṉṟiya ceyappāṭṭu viṉiyāṭci, ceyviṉi āṭciyiṉum, moḻinaṭi uṭiyatām. ataṉpaṭi, sahpha vati eṉpatum, ‘'teṉaphīyatē'’ eṉpatum oṉṟē. piṉṉatu, vēṟu emmoḻiyil moḻipeyarppatu iyalātu eṉpatoṉṟē, vēṟupāṭu, āṅkilam camaskirutattōṭu uṟavuṭiya moḻiyāyiṉum, "he is been” eṉpatu pōlum āṭci, āṅkilattilum poruḷaṟṟatu; nakippiṟkuriyatu. tamiḻc ceyappāṭṭu viṉi amippukaḷi, akattiyaṉār pukuttiyuḷḷār. iruntum, "tāṭya te” eṉpatip “paṭu” eṉṟa viṉic colli , “aṭi” eṉpataṉ iṟantakāla viṉi yeccattōṭu iṇittu, ‘'aṭikkappaṭṭāṉ'’ eṉa avarāl, moḻipeyarkka muṭintālum, ceyappaṭuporuḷ. kuṉṟiya ceyappāṭṭu viṉiyiṉit tamiḻukkuk koṇṭuvara, avarāl muṭiyavilli. iraṇṭu viṉic coṟkaḷi oru collākat tokippaṭutti, attokic colliṉ irumūlaṅkaḷukkum, eḻuvāy allatu viṉimutalāka vēṟu vēṟupaṭṭa iruvarkaḷik koḷvatu, kāraṇakāriya muṟiyiṉiyum, tamiḻip poṟuttamaṭṭil, kāraṇakāriya muṟiyiṉiyum, tamiḻip poṟuttamaṭṭil, kāraṇa kāriya muṟiyiṉi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭa ilakkaṇa vitikaḷiyum mīṟiyatākum. ātaliṉ, ‘'aṭikkappaṭum'’ eṉpati, “aṭikka” (maṟṟoruvaṉ) aṭikkum poḻutu. eṉṟum, ‘'paṭu” tuṉpam aṉupavi eṉṟum pirittuk kāṇpatu, tamiḻiṉ ciṟappiyalpukku muraṇām.'’ aṭikkappaṭum” eṉpataṉ moḻinaṭivaṭivam, attokic colliṉ muṟpakuti, urupēlāp peyarc collāka varum, “aṭipaṭu “ allatu “aṭiyuṇ” eṉpatām. camaskirutac ceyalpāṭṭu viṉikaḷit tamiḻil moḻipeyarkkat tēvippaṭṭa tālum, akattiyaṉār māṇavar, tolkāppiyaṉār, tammuṭiya ilakkaṇatti, “eḻuttu eṉappaṭuva'’ (tol: eḻuttu : 1) eṉap pōlic ceyap pāṭṭu viṉiyōṭu toṭaṅkuvatu pōlac camaskiruta moḻiyil cintittut, tamiḻil eḻutuvārkkup peritum payaṉuṭiyatāka amintatālum, akattiyaṉār, icceyalpāṭṭu viṉiyiṉik kaṇṭupiṭittuḷḷār. iyalpāṉa tamiḻppēccu naṭiyil, entat tamiḻaṉum, enniliyilum mēṟkoḷḷātatum, camaskirutattiliruntu tamiḻil eḷitil moḻipeyarkka, camaskirutap paṇṭitarkaḷukkut tuṇi niṟkak kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatālum, ippōli ceyappāṭṭu viṉi, nam kālattil poruḷkaḷic ceyappāṭṭu vaṭivaṅkaḷil viḷakkum āṅkilattil cintittu, tamiḻil eḻutak kaṟṟuk koṇṭa kāraṇattāl, eḻuttu naṭit tamiḻil, atu oru naṭimuṟiyākiviṭṭatu, paṭu eṉṟa, moḻi marapiṟku ēlā oru collurupu, pipiḷiṉ tamiḻ moḻipeyarppu nūliṉ ovvoru pakkattiyum, turatiruṣṭavacamākat tamiḻp paṇṭitarkaḷiṉ ilakkiyap paṭippukaḷiyum pāḻpaṭuttukiṟatu.

tamiḻil, camskiruta nākarīka mutal taliyīṭukaḷil, ivi orucilavē, aṭutta taliyīṭukaḷ, coṟkaḷ, koḷkikaḷ, ceyyuṭkaṟpaṉikaḷ, purāṇa, maṟṟum piṟa kaṭṭukkatikaḷ, nampikkikaḷ, mūṭa nampikkikaḷ, viññāṉa, oḻukka, camayap pāṭaṅkaḷ, urinaṭi, pāvakikaḷ, maṟṟum piṟa piṟa , kālam cellac cella, camaskiruta nākarīkattiṉ paṭiyeṭuppu, piṟkālat tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷi maṭṭumē terinta oruvaṉukkuc cintaṉikaḷ pōl allāmal iyaṟkic cintaṉikaḷik koṇṭa taṉṉuṭiya moḻinaṭi avaṉ tāymoḻiyallāta vēṟṟu moḻi pōlum. paḻakkamilā moḻiyākat tōṉṟumaḷavu, tamiḻilakkiyam, muḻukka, muḻukka vaṭavarkaḷiṉ ātikkattiṉ kīḻ akappaṭṭuk koḷḷumaḷavu mikap peritāki viṭṭatu.

akattiyaṉāruṭiya , avar peyarāl, akattiyam eṉa aḻikkappaṭum ilakkaṇam ippōtu illi. āṉāl, jamatakkiṉiyār makaṉ tiraṇatūmākkiṉiyār iyaṟṟiya, tolkāppiyaṉār eṉap payila aḻikkappaṭum avar māṇavar iyaṟṟiya, ilakkaṇam iṉṟum uḷḷatu. atu, teḷivāka, akattiyattiyē mūlamākak koṇṭuḷatu. ittamiḻ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ, eḻuttu, col, yāppu, ākiyaṉa, paṟṟi maṭṭumallāmal, pāṭṭiṉ karupporuḷ kuṟittum ilakkaṇam vakuppatu, ivaṟṟiṉ taṉicciṟappām. pāṭṭiṉ karupporuḷ, ilakkiya marapukaḷ ākiyavaṟṟāl, tamiḻc ceyyuḷ, camaskirutac ceyyuḷiliruntu aṟavē vēṟupaṭuvatu kaṇṭa ippirāmaṇa āciriyarkaḷ, tam ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷil, ipporuḷkaḷ paṟṟiya āyviṉiyum mēṟkoḷvatu tēvi eṉa uṇarntaṉar. tiraṇatūmākkiṉiyāriṉ nūlām. avar tamiḻp peyarāl vaḻaṅkum tol kāppiyattiṉ piṟpakutikaḷ, paṇṭit tamiḻp pulavarkaḷ, tamakkē uriya taṉi iyalpiṟ pāṭiya pāṭaṟporuḷkaḷām akam, puṟam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ ovvoru nikaḻccikaḷ kuṟittum āyvu ceykiṉṟaṉa. poruḷatikāram eṉṟa tolkāppiyattiṉ īṟṟup pakutiyiliruntu, āriyarkaḷōṭu nerukkamāṉa toṭarpu koḷvataṟku muṉṉar, tamiḻar naṭattiya vāḻkki muṟi, tamiḻ uḷḷam, āriya ilakkiyaṅkaḷukku āṭpaṭāta taṟku muṟpaṭṭa tamiḻ ilakkiyam ākiyavaṟṟiṉ muḻup paṭatti varintu koḷḷalām.

atu pōlum oru paṭakkāṭci, tolkāppiyap poruḷatikārattil kāṭṭiyiruppatu pōla, āriyattukku muntiya tamiḻar nākarikam” (pre Aryan Tamil Culture) eṉṟa eṉ nūlilum, iṉi vara irukkam, paṇṭit tamiḻar (Ancient Tamilis) eṉṟa eṉ nūlilum kāṭṭiyirukkum paṭakkāṭci kaḷi, muḻumiyākkum. -

poruḷatikārattil āriya nuḻivu

tamiḻp pākkaḷ kuṟitta tammuṭiya ilakkaṇattil, āriyak karuttukkaḷi iṟakkumati ceyyum kavarcci yiliruntu, tolkāppiyaṉār viṭupaṭavilli. paṇṭit tamiḻp pākkaḷiṉ marapukaḷi allatu viti muṟikaḷi viḷakkik kūṟumpōtu, avar kālattil vaḻakkil irunta, maṟintaṉa pōka eñciyirunta aṉittut tamiḻp pākkaḷiyum uṟutiyākap piṉpaṟṟiyuḷḷār. āṉālum, tām peṟṟirunta, camaskiruta ilakkiya aṟivālum, araciyal neṟimuṟikaḷum, camutāya amippukaḷum, teyvattaṉmi yāl vakukkappeṟṟavi eṉṟa tam nampikkiyālum, avar aṭikkaṭi āḻttappaṭṭuḷḷār. camutāya urimiyōṭu, pāṭṭuṭit talivarkaḷām kalvi urimiyum aṉupavikkum mutal mūṉṟu varuṇavattavar aṭaṅkiya nāṉku varuṇattavarkaḷāka makkaḷip pirippatē camutāya amippiṉ vitimuṟiyākum eṉpatu tolkāppiyarkku uriya karuttu. āṉāl, avar kaṟṟa tamiḻ pāṭalkaḷil, āriyak koḷkippaṭi, mikavum iḻintavarkaḷāṉa, vēṭṭiyāṭum iṉattiṉ talivaṉum, mīṉ piṭikkum iṉattiṉ talivaṉum, kātaṟpākkaḷiṉ talivarāvar. immuraṇpāṭṭi viḷakka, avar eṭuttuk koḷḷa muyaṟci mikavum kuṟipāṭuṭiyatu vēṇṭāviruppāṉatu. mēlum, āriyac camutāya vitimuṟikaḷil ēṟṟuk koḷḷappaṭṭa tirumaṇa muṟikaḷ eṭṭu uḷḷaṉa. āṉāl, tamiḻp pākkaḷil iraṇṭē uḷḷaṉa. ivviru muṟikaḷiyum oṉṟu eṉakkāṭṭa, avar vīṇē muyaṉṟuḷḷār. tamiḻ akattiṇi oḻukkam, camaskirutak kāma oḻukkattiviṭa meṉmiyāṉatām; tarma artta mōkṣaṅkaḷit, tamiḻp puṟattiṇi oḻukkaṅkaḷil tiṇittuviṭa muṭiyātām eṉṟālum, uriyāciriyar palarum vīṇē muyalvatu pōlavē, tolkāppiyarum, kātalum, pōrumē vāḻkkiyiṉ kuṟikkōḷām eṉak karutum tamiḻar vāḻkki muṟiyit tarma, artta , kāma mōkṣamē vāḻkkiyiṉ kuṟikkōḷaram eṉakkarutum āriyar. vāḻkki muṟiyōṭu poruttikkāṇa muyaṉṟuḷḷār. poruḷatikāram, kaṭici atikārattil, tam kālattil tamiḻil iṭampeṟāta, āṉāl, tamakkut terinta camaskiruta eṭuttuk kāṭṭukkaḷ uḷḷa, pala ilakkiya vaṭivaṅkaḷi vivarittuk kūṟiyuḷḷār. iviyum, ivi pōlum pala iṭaṅkaḷil tamiḻukku uriyaṉa allātaṉavaṟṟit tamiḻppākkaḷ kuṟitta tammuṭiya ilakkaṇattil pukuttiyuḷḷār. ataṉāl, tolkāppiyar ivvāṟu ceyyumiṭaṅkaḷil, avaruṭiya uriyāciriyarkaḷāl, avar kūṟṟiṟkut tēviyāṉa eṭuttuk kāṭṭukaḷit tamiḻiliruntu kāṭṭa muṭiyāmal pōvatu viyappiṉit taruvatilli. tolkāppiyar, tamiḻil pukuttum, maṟṟum iru āriyak koḷkikaḷ paṟṟi avaruṭiya kālam kuṟitta āyviṉpōtu teḷivākak kūṟappaṭum. tamiḻil camaskirutac coṟkaḷ nuḻivu

akattiyaṉārum, tolkāppiyaṉārum, tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakku oli muṟiyil vaḻaṅkappaṭṭuviṭṭa camaskirutac coṟkaḷām tatpavac coṟkaḷ cilavaṟṟit tamiḻil pukuttiviṭṭaṉar. avvakic coṟkaḷ iṭikkāla uriyāciriyarkaḷiṉ uriyil eṭuttatāḷappaṭṭirukkum akattiyac cūttaraṅkaḷilum tolkāppiya ilakkaṇattilum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, ilakkaṇa vitikaḷik kuṟikkavalla marapuc coṟkaḷil perumpālaṉa vaṟṟiṟkut tamiḻc coṟkaḷik kāṇumaḷavu , tamiḻiṉ ciṟappiṉi akattiyaṉār, matittiruntār. kiṭittirukkum paḻiya tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil, camaskirutattiliruntu kaṭaṉ peṟṟu vaḻaṅkappaṭṭirukkum coṟkaḷ mikamikak kuṟivu allatu aṟavē illi. āṉāl, kālam cellac cella oru . pāṭalil iṭam peṟṟirukkum camaskirutac coṟkaḷiṉ catavikitam, appāṭaliṉ kālatti uṇarttum aṟikuṟiyām eṉak koḷḷumaḷavu, accoṟkaḷiṉ nuḻivu, paṭippaṭiyākap, perukik koṇṭē vantuḷatu. ivvāṟu, camaskirutac coṟkaḷ, tamiḻil mēlum mēlum iṭam peṟṟu vantaṉa eṉṟālum, tolkāppiyap poruḷatikārattup perumpakutik karup poruḷāka irukkum tamiḻilakkiya, marapukaḷ, āṟāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭu vari viṭāmal kaṭip piṭikkappaṭṭu vantuḷḷaṉa.

akattiyar kālam

akattiyaṉār, avar māṇavar tolkāppiyaṉār ākiyōriṉ kālam etu? akattiyaṉār ilakkaṇam, ippōtu illi. āṉāl, tolkāppiyaṉār, avari aṭikkaṭi eṭuttu āḷukiṟār. avar cūttiraṅkaḷil eṅkellām eṉṟu kūṟuvar eṉap poruḷpaṭum ‘'eṉpa'’ eṉṟa col iṭappeṟukiṟatō, aṅkellām uriyāciriyarkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟuvar āciriyar” eṉṟē poruḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. tolkāppiyar, tammuṭiya ilakkaṇatti " eḻuttu eṉṟu collappaṭupavi yāvi eṉṟāl, avi, a eṉṟa eḻuttil toṭaṅki, ṉ eṉṟa eḻuttil muṭiyum muppatu eṉṟu āciriyar kūṟuvar" eṉṟa kūṟṟōṭu toṭaṅkukiṟār.

"eḻuttu eṉappaṭum,

akaram mutal,

ṉakara iṟuvāy muppaḵtu eṉpa."

(tol : eḻuttu : 1)





ākavē, akattiyaṉār tammuṭiya ilakkaṇattit tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷ muppatu eṉṟa eṇṇilēyē toṭaṅkiṉār eṉa nām koḷḷalām. eḻuttu eṉṟa - col, tamiḻ ilakkaṇa āciriyarkaḷāl, kuṟippiṭattakka oru ciṟappāṉa niliyil āḷappaṭṭuḷatu. oliyiyum, uruvattiyum orucērak kuṟikkum vakiyil, atu peyariṭappaṭṭuḷatu. muṉṉatu olivaṭivil uḷḷa eḻuttu eṉap poruḷpaṭum. olivaṭivu eḻuttu' eṉṟum, piṉṉatu nērkkōṭukaḷākavum vaḷinta kōṭukaḷākavum variyappaṭṭa eḻuttu eṉap poruḷpaṭum. varivaṭivu eḻuttu" eṉṟum aḻikkappaṭum. āṅkilattil olivaṭiveḻuttu, varivaṭiveḻuttu eṉṟa iraṇṭiyum orucērak kuṟikkum iyalpuṭiya col etuvum illāmiyāl eḻuttu eṉṟa accolli apporuḷ kuṟikkum vakiyil āṅkilattil moḻipeyarppatu iyalātu. akṣarā eṉṟa col camaskiruta āciriyarkaḷāl ivviru poruḷ kuṟikkum vakiyil, āḷappaṭukiṟatu. āṉāl, akṣarā eṉṟa accol, kēṭkakkūṭiya oliyi uruvākkum, kāṟṟiṉ ali atirviṉ, vāṉaveḷi aṭittaḷattiṉ mūlamām. nilipēṟuṭiya oli eṉakkarutum mīmāmcakar, maṟṟum viyākkarṉikāk karuttukkaḷōṭu toṭarpuṭiyatu ākaliṉ, eḻuttu eṉṟa tamiḻccol, akṣarā eṉpataṉ moḻipeyarppu ākātu.

eḻuttukkaḷ kuṟittu akattiyaṉār kūṟum ilakkaṇap paṭiyē, tamiḻ varivaṭiveḻuttukkaḷ, urutu moḻikkākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum arāpiya eḻuttukkaḷ tavirttu, intiyāvil vaḻakkattil uḷḷa ellā eḻuttukkaḷiyum pōlavē, ‘'pirāmi'’ eṉap peyar cūṭṭappaṭṭa, acōkaṉ kalveṭṭeḻuttukkaḷiliruntē, uruppeṟṟaṉa eṉpati nām aṟivōm. eḻuttil vaṭikkappeṟṟa tamiḻc coṟkaḷiṉ, ituvari kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭāta mikappaḻiya mātiri vaṭivaṅkaḷiṉ utāraṇaṅkaḷ, pāṇṭi nāṭṭu malikkukikaḷil uḷḷa kalveṭṭukkaḷil namakkuk kiṭikkiṉṟaṉa. ikkalveṭṭukkaḷ, pittup piṭittu aliyum makkaḷ kūṭṭattiliruntu, veku tolivil uḷḷa, iyaṟkiyāṉa malikkukikaḷil taṅkaḷi aḻiyāp pēriṉpa niliyām nirvāṇa nilikkuk koṇṭu collum, amitiyāṉa tavaniliyil vāḻntu vanta peḷatta, jiṉa muṉivarkaḷiṉ tūṇṭutalāl eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. ikkalveṭṭukkaḷ namakkuk kiṭittuḷḷa tamiḻ eḻuttu muṟi, mikappaḻiya mātiri vaṭivaṅkaḷ maṭṭumaṉṟu, pirāmi vaṭiveḻuttukkaḷi, mikap poruttamuṟac colvatāṉāl, teṉṉintiya meḷariya vaṭiveḻuttukkaḷit, tamiḻ eḻuttu naṭikku mēṟkoṇṭa taṟkālika mutal muyaṟciyumām. avi, taṉi mey eḻuttiṟkum, akaramoṭu iṇintu varum uyirmey eḻuttiṟkum iṭiyil eḷitil olippataṟku vāyppāka, mey eḻuttukkaḷ, akaramoṭu iṇintē olikkappaṭum (meyyiṉ iyakkam akaramoṭu civaṇum” (tol. eḻuttu : 13 eṉat tolkāppiyar kūṟuvatu pōla) evvita vēṟupāṭum kāṭṭavilli; atāvatu mey eḻutti uṇartta, mēlē puḷḷi vikkum muṟi iṉṉamum - koṇṭu varappaṭavilli. āṉāl, avi, tamiḻukkē uriya ciṟappu meyyām “ḻa” iraṭṭittavaḻi, valintum mūkkoli eḻuttām “ṉa” karam muṉ varumpōtu melintum olikkappaṭuvatum, nākki uḷmaṭittu olittu eḻuvatumāṉa “ṟa” nakarattiṟku muṉvarum mukkoli eḻuttik kuṟikkat taṉṉāl maṭṭumē iyalvatum, iṉṟi olippu muṟiyil pulloli eḻuttām “na” karattiliruntu vēṟupāṭu kāṇa iyalātatumākiya iru cuḻi "ṉa" ākiyavaṟṟik kuṟikkum varivaṭivaṅkaḷik koṇṭuḷḷaṉa. ākak kiṭikkak kūṭiya akaccāṉṟukaḷiṉ paṭi, ki.mu. mūṉṟāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil, allatu, allatu, iraṇṭāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ toṭakkattil, kalveṭṭu vaṭippatil, nīṭittup payiṉṟu, aḻiyāppayiṟci peṟṟuviṭṭa veḷināṭṭiliruntu vantavarkaḷāl, tamiḻ, mutaṉmutalil, varivaṭivil eḻutappaṭṭatu eṉat terikiṟatu. tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷi eḻuti vippataṟkut : taṉiyāṉa neṭuṅkaṇakku illāmiyē kiṟittuva āṇṭut toṭakkattiṟku mikavum muṟpaṭṭa kālattilēyē, perumaḷavil iyaṟppaṭṭirukka vēṇṭiyavāya, tamiḻp pākkaḷ, mīḷavum peṟamāṭṭāvakiyil, aḻintu viṭṭataṟkuk kāraṇamām. ākavē, tamiḻ eḻuttu muṟi, ellōrālum, perumaḷavil mēṟkoḷḷappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭiya ki.mu. mutalām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṉṉar, akattiyaṉār vāḻntirukka muṭiyātu eṉa muṭivu ceykiṉṟēṉ. tolkāppiya ārāycci āciriyarum avar māṇavarum ataṟku, oru nūṟṟāṇṭu allatu caṟṟu atikap paṭiyāṉa piṟpaṭṭa kālattil vāḻntirukka vēṇṭum eṉak koḷḷumāṟu nammi vaṟpuṟuttukiṟatu. tirumaṇam, maṟṟum iṉiya nikaḻccikaḷukku ēṟpuṭiya nāḻiki eṉum poruḷil, ōri" eṉṟa collit tolkāppiyar āṇṭuḷḷār.

"maṟinta oḻukkattu ōriyum nāḷum,

tuṟanta oḻukkam kiḻavōṟku illi"

(-tol : poruḷ : kaḷavu : 44)





"tīya ōriyilum, tīya nāḷilum, puṇarcciyik kiviṭa vēṇṭum eṉṟa koḷki, maṟinta oḻukkamām, kaḷavuk kālattil talivaṉukku illi"

"ōri" eṉṟa attamiḻc col, "avar" (Hour) eṉṟa āṅkilaccollip pōlavē muṭinta muṭivāka horā (Hora) eṉṟa kirēkka moḻic colliliruntē peṟappaṭṭatām. "herā” eṉṟa accol kirēkka moḻiyil, ki.mu. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭil, "paruvam” eṉṟa potup poruḷil tāṉ vaḻaṅkappaṭṭatu. nāḷiṉ irupattunāṉku kūṟukaḷil, ovvoru kūṟum, ēḻu kōḷkaḷil ovvoru kōḷiṉ āṭcik kīḻ, varuvatākak koḷḷappaṭṭa, ki.mu. iraṇṭāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭiltāṉ, ‘'horā’ eṉṟa accollukku, nāḷiṉ irupattu nāṉku kūṟṟil oru kūṟṟi uṇarttum poruḷ tarappaṭṭuḷḷatu. horā eṉṟa accol, tāṉ uṇarttum cōtiṭap poruṭkuṟippōṭu kimu mutal iraṇṭu nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil, kirēkka maṉṉarkaḷāl, āṭci ceyyappaṭṭu vanta kāntārattiṟkup payaṇam ceytatu. camaskiruta nūlāciriyarkaḷ kirēkkarkaḷiṉ jōtiṭakkaliyik kaṟṟuk koṇṭapōtu, atu, camaskirutattil iṭam koṇṭatu. piṉṉar, atu, teṟkil payaṇam ceytu, tamiḻil nuḻintu viṭṭatu. “ōri’ eṉṟa col, tolkāppiyar kālattiṉ mēlvarampākum. ki.pi. mutalāṇṭiṟku muṉṉart tamiḻil iṭampeṟṟirukka muṭiyātu eṉṟu koṇṭāl, atu, oru naṭunili matippīṭām. tolkāppiyaṉār, tammuṭiya ilakkaṇa nūlil, ‘ōri eṉṟa colli āṇṭiruntālum, tamiḻarkaḷiṉ perumpakutiyiṉar, atāvatu, pārppaṉarallātār āriya nākarīkattāl aṭimi koḷḷappaṭṭu, ataṉāl kōḷkaḷ kuṟitta vāṉanūl aṟivit tolkāppiyar kālattukku mika mika muṟpaṭṭa kālattilēyē peṟṟiruntaṉar; allatu atil nampikki - koṇṭiruntaṉar eṉpati nām karuttil koḷḷa vēṇṭum. ivvāṉanūl kaliyōṭu toṭarpuṭiyaṉa, ēḻu nāṭkaḷik koṇṭa vāramum, ovvoru nāḷum ‘ antāḷiṉ mutal iraṇṭari nāḻikiyi āṭci celuttum - kōḷāl peyariṭappaṭuvatumām. ākavē, vārattiṉ nāṭkaḷiṉ, kōḷ cārpuṭiya peyarkaḷ tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷil ki.pi. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭu vari kāṇappaṭavilli.

tolkāppiyap poruḷatikārattiliruntu peṟappaṭum . tolkāppiyaṉār kālatti uṟuti ceyyat tuṇipuriyum piṟitoru ceytiyum uḷatu. akattiṇittalivaṉiṉ tōḻarkaḷākavum, avaṉ kātal oḻukkaṅkaḷukkut tuṇivarkaḷākavum varuvōruḷ, pārppārkaḷiyum kuṟippiṭukiṟār.

"pārppāṉ, pāṅkaṉ, tōḻi, cevili

cīrttaku ciṟappiṉ kiḻavaṉ kiḻattiyoṭu

aḷaviyaṉ marapiṉ, aṟuvaki yōrum

kaḷaviṉil kiḷavikku uriyar eṉpa" .



"pāṇaṉ, kūttaṉa, viṟali , paratti ,

yāṇam cāṉṟa aṟivar, kaṇṭōr,

pēṇutaku ciṟappiṉ pārppāṉ, mutalā,

muṉṉuṟak kiḷanta kiḷaviyoṭu toki it

toṉṉeṟi marapiṟ kaṟpiṟku uriyar"



"pārppār aṟivar eṉṟivar kiḷavi,

yārkkum variyār yāppoṭu puṇarntē"



tol. poruḷ : ceyyuḷ : 181, 182, 189





avvakiyil, kātal nikaḻccikaḷil peruntuṇi puripavarkaḷākap pirāmaṇarkaḷ āḷappaṭuvati, āḻntu muṭivu ceytuḷḷār. itu camaskiruta nāṭakattiṉ oru marapu ākum. akanāṉūṟṟilō, allatu puṟanāṉūṟṟilō attakiya kuṟippu etiyum kāṇa muṭiyavilliyātaliṉ tolkāppiyaṉār, ati oru koḷkiyaḷavil kūṟiyuḷḷārēyallatu, ōr uṇmi nikaḻcciyākak kūṟavilli eṉṟē nāṉ eṭuttuk koḷkiṟēṉ. puṟanāṉūṟṟil, pārppār eṉṟa col, nāṉku muṟi iṭam peṟukiṟatu; antaṇar maṟṟum avartam pulami paṟṟi, mēlum eṭṭukkuṟippukaḷ uḷḷaṉa.

"āvum āṉ iyal pārppaṉa mākkaḷum"

puṟam : 9:1





"pārppārt tappiya koṭumi"

- puṟam 34 : 3





"ēṟṟa pārppārkku īrṅki niṟiyap pūvum

poṉṉum puṉalpaṭac corintu”

-puṟam : 367 : 4 - 5



"anti antaṇar aruṅkaṭaṉ iṟukkum

muttī viḷakku” - puṟam 2 23 24



“pulaṉ aḻukkaṟṟa antaṇāḷaṉ”

puṟam : 126 : 17.



"yāṉē paricilaṉ maṉṉum antaṇaṉ”

- puṟam : 200 : 13.



“antaṇaṉ pulavaṉ koṇṭuvan taṉaṉē”

puṟam : 361 : 4



“kēḷvi muṟṟiya vēḷvi antaṇaṉ”

-puṟam : 361 : 4.



"ākkural kāṇpiṉ antaṇāḷar'’

-puṟam : 362 : 8



“iṟiñcuka peruma! niṉ ceṉṉi, ciṟanta

nāṉmaṟi muṉivar ēntuki etirē”

- puṟam : 6:19 - 20



"āṉṟa kēḷvi aṭaṅkiya koḷki,

nāṉmaṟi mutalvar,”

puṟam 26 12 13



"aṟampuri koḷki nāṉmaṟi mutalvar'’

puṟam 93:17.



“vēta vēḷvit toḻil”

puṟam : 228 : 9





iviyellām avarkaḷiṉ vētappulami, vēḷvit tī, ēṟṟuk koṇṭa koṭivaḷam ākiyavaṟṟik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉavēyallatu, kātalarkaḷiṭiyē, avarkaḷ mēṟkoṇṭa tūtu paṟṟik kuṟippiṭavilli. akanāṉūṟṟil aka vēḷvi ceytaṟiyāta oru pārppāṉam, "vēḷāp pārpāṉ" (akam : 24 : 1) aracarkaḷiṭiyē tūtuvat toḻil mēṟkoṇṭu celluṅkāl, maḻavar eṉṟa āṟali kaḷvarāl kollappaṭṭa oru pārppāṉum, "tūtu oy pārppāṉ. maṭi veḷḷōli... taṭintu uṭaṉ vīḻtta kaṭuṅkaṇ maḻavar" (akam : 337 : 7-11), kuṟuntokiyil, maṇam ākā iḷiyapārppāṉ oruvaṉiṉ uṭimikaḷam, "taṇṭoṭu piṭitta, tāḻka maṇṭalattup paṭiva uṇṭip pārppaṉa makaṉē! eḻutākkaṟpiṉ niṉ col" (kuṟuntoki : 156 : 3-5) kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉar. āṉāl, kātalariṭiyē tūtu cellum ceyal etuvum, avaṟṟil kuṟippiṭappaṭavilli. kātalariṭiyē, pārppaṉar tūtu cellum marapiṉit tolkāppiyaṉār, camaskiruta nāṭakattiliruntē tamiḻp pākkaḷukkuk koṇṭu vantiruppatāl, avar, muṟiyāṉa nāṭakaṅkaḷ, camaskirutattil vaḷarntuviṭṭa piṟkālattil, vāḻntirukka vēṇṭum. pāsara (Bhasa) eṉpavartām, camaskirutap pulavarkaḷil paḻamiyāṉavar. avar, ki.mu. mūṉṟāvatu nūṟṟāṇṭiṟku muṟpaṭṭa kālattic cērntavarāvatu iyalātu; annāṭaka marapit tolkāppiyaṉār, tammuṭiya ilakkaṇattil iṇittuk koḷvataṟkut tuṇi ceyvataṟkēṟpa, teṉṉintiyāvi uṭiya, camaskiruta nāṭakaṅkaḷiṉ, avvilakkiya marapu, cila āṇṭu kālatti eṭuttuk koṇṭirukka vēṇṭum. col, yāppu uḷḷiṭṭa ataṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷukku āṟṟiya muḻu niṟivāṉa aṭippaṭip paṇiyuṭpaṭa, tamiḻ nākarīka vaḷarccikku, akattiyar paṅku, kūṟip pārāṭṭattakka peruñceyalākum. āṉāl, paṇṭiya kaṭṭukkatiyāḷarkaḷum, iṉṟiya varalāṟṟu ārāycciyāḷarkaḷum, aṟputat tiruviḷiyāṭalkaḷik kaṟpittu, attūyavari mācupaṭutta muyaṉṟuḷḷaṉar. avvakiyil, paḻiya tamiḻp purāṇaṅkaḷ, avar, civaṉ allatu cuppiramaṇiyariṭam tamiḻ kaṟṟār. tamiḻarkaḷukkut tamiḻik kaṟpittār eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. akattiyar, tammiṭiyē vantu kāṭcitarum vari tamiḻar, eḻuttaṟivavillā ūmiyarāy iruntaṉar? eṉa nāṉ karutukiṉṟēṉ. tiruvāḷar ṭi. ār. paṇṭarkār avarkaḷ, “atuvarik kaṇṭaṟiyappaṭātirunta teṉṉāṭ ṭiṉuḷ. tirāviṭarkaḷi nākarīka makkaḷāka māṟṟiyavāṟē, mēlum mēlum ūṭuruvic ceṉṟār.'’ eṉak kūṟukiṟār. "(Carmichal Lecturas. 1918 Page 18) tiruvāḷar, ṭi.ār. irāmakiruṣṇa cāstiri avarkaḷ, tamiḻarkaḷiṉ camutāya ilakkiya vāḻkki muṟiyil akattiyar pukuttiya putumi, vakutta maṟucīramippukaḷ paṟṟip pēcukiṟār. (Proceedings and Transactions of Third Oriental Conference 1924 Page 205) iviyaṉittum vaṭikaṭṭiya camayac cārpāṉa kaṭṭukkatikaḷ. makkaḷ vāḻntu vaḷarnta cuṟṟuccūḻali aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭē eḻuntamiyāl, māṟṟavō, aḻikkavō muṭiyātatāṉa, tamakkē uriya ilakkiya marapukaḷōṭu iṇintu, muḻumiyāka vaḷarcci peṟṟa, ōr ilakkiyattiyē akattiyar kaṇṭār. muḻumiyāka vaḷarcci peṟṟa ilakkiya naṭiyōṭu kūṭiya oru moḻiyiyum avar kaṇṭār. ammoḻi ilakkiyam, kuṟitta ilakkaṇatti iyaṟṟiṉār. avvaḷavē, atuvallatu vēṟu illi.

akattiyar kālattiṟku muṉpē, eṇṇaṟṟa tamiḻp pulavarkaḷ iruntirukka vēṇṭum. illiyeṉṟāl, yāppu kuṟitta ilakkaṇatti avarāl iyaṟṟiyirukka iyalātu. tiruntiya ilakkiya naṭiyām centamiḻ kuṟittuk kūṟat tēviyilli. tamiḻc ceyyuḷ naṭiyeḻunta kālattiṟkum, akattiyaṉār kālattiṟkum iṭippaṭṭa kālakaṭṭattil, puṟattiṇi, akattaṇip pāṭalkaḷāl uruvākiya eḻucci, avvaki ilakkiyaṅkaḷi, avar kālattiṟkup piṉṉar ainnūṟu āṇṭuvariyum, paṇṭiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷ nilikulintu, camaskirutak kavitikaḷāl īrppuṇṭu, akattūṇṭutal peṟṟup putiya tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷ tōṉṟip paḻiya kaviti muṟikaḷip peritum maṟittuviṭṭa akkālam vari, koṇṭu ceṉṟatu. piṟkālap purāṇak katikaḷ, akattiyaṉār, tamiḻit tōṟṟuvittār eṉak kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ām; tamiḻ moḻiyuḷ , inta moḻiyilakkaṇa , mutal āciriyariṉ, nalvarukiyiṉ kīḻ naṭipeṟṟa āriya nuḻiviṉ perumpayaṉē, piṟkālat tamiḻilakkiyaṅkaḷ eṉṟa uṇmikkaru, ikkatiyuḷ aṭaṅkiyuḷḷatu eṉalām. ākavē, akattiyak kaṭṭukkatik kaḷañciyattukkut tamiḻilakkiyam perumpaṅku aḷikka muṭiyum eṉa, evarum etirpārkkalām. āṉāl, akattiyak kaṭṭukkatikaḷukkut tamiḻ āṟṟi yirukkum paṅku mikak kuṟivām eṉpatu aṟiya, ēmāṟṟam taruvatāy uḷḷatu. kuṟuntokiyilum, naṟṟiṇiyilum, avar paṟṟiya kuṟippu etiyum eṉṉāl kāṇa muṭiyavilli. puṟanāṉūṟṟil, vētavēḷvi ceyvōriṉ vāḻiṭamākap potiya maliyik kūṟum kuṟippu oṉṟu uḷatu. "muttī viḷakkil tuñcum, poṟkōṭṭu imayamum, potiyamum pōṉṟē ." (puṟam: 2 : 23-24) akam, puṟam eṉṟa iru toki nūlkaḷil potiyamali, pāṇṭiyarkku uriyatāka, ēḻu pākkaḷil viḷakkappaṭṭirukka, ati, akattiyarkku uriyatākkum kuṟippu etuvum avaṟṟil illi. akattiyaṉār kālam, toki nūlkaḷil mikap paḻiyaṉavāya, annāṉku toki nūl pākkaḷukku naṉimika aṇmiyāṉatu. ākavē avi avari makkaḷuḷ oruvarāka ōr ilakkaṇa āciriyarākap pārttatē allatu, oru riṣiyāka matikkavilli eṉṟa uṇmiyē, itaṟkuk kāraṇamātal kūṭum.

pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉiṉ aṉpukkuriya pulavar māṅkuṭi marutaṉār (kipi. 450) avaṉip pārāṭṭip pāṭiya maturik kāñciyil, oru pakuti uriyāciriyar nacciṉārkkiṉiyarāl, atu, akattiyarik kuṟippatākak kāṭṭumaḷavu citikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atu, neṭuñceḻiyaṉip pārttu, "pakka malikaḷil vīḻntu ōṭum aruvikaḷik koṇṭa malikku uriyavaṉē! teṉṉavarkaḷi ōṭṭiyavaṉum, aṇukuvataṟku ariya āṟṟal vāyntavaṉumākiya paḻamperum kaṭavuḷukku, aṭuttu irukka virumpuvōṉē!'’ eṉap pārāṭṭukiṟatu.

"teṉṉavaṉ peyariya tuṉṉarum tuppiṉ

toṉmutu kaṭavuḷ piṉṉar mēya

varittāḻ aruvip poruppiṉ poruna!"

- maturikkāñci : 40-42





ippakuti, poruḷ, teḷivu kāṇamāṭṭātu uḷatu. "teṉṉavaṉ” eṉpatu irāvaṇaṉik kuṟikkum eṉṟāl, irāmaṉukku aṭuttavaṉāka irukka virumpiṉāṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉ eṉpatu poruḷātal vēṇṭum. āṉāl, irāvaṇaṉit, teṉṉavaṉ eṉak kuṟippiṭum orē oru kuṟippu, atuvum mikavum piṟpaṭṭa pāṭṭu oṉṟil uḷatu.

"teṉṉavaṉ mali eṭukkac, cēyiḻi oṭuṅkak kaṇṭu,

maṉṉavaṉ viralāl ūṉṟa”

-tirunāvukkaracar tēvāram tirukkacci mēṟṟaḷi.





"teṉṉavaṉ" eṉpatu, pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷukkup potuvāka iṭṭu vaḻaṅkum oru paṭṭappeyar. appāṭṭu, āriyap purāṇak katikaḷ tamiḻnāṭṭil parava iṭam koṇṭuviṭṭa ki.pi. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭil pāṭappaṭṭa tāyiṉ attoṭarit , teṉ ticik kaṭavuḷ eṉak kūṟappaṭum yamaṉi veṉṟa civaṉukku aṭutta niliyil irukka virumpiṉāṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṉ eṉṟa poruḷ tarum vakiyil koḷḷalām. āṉāl, civaṉiṉ, ivveṟṟic ceyal, neṭuñceḻiyaṉ kālattu maturiyil, palarālum aṟiyappaṭṭiruntatu eṉa, uṟutiyākak koḷḷamuṭiyātu. mēlē kūṟappaṭṭa iru poruḷkaḷumē, kaṭṭuppāṭaṟṟa kaṟpaṉikaḷtām. āṉāl, uriyāciriyar nacciṉārk kiṉiyariṉ viḷakkam paṭumōcam. avar, mūlac coṟkaḷiyum, coṟṟoṭar kaḷiyum tām kūṟa virumpum poruḷukku ēṟṟavāṟu iṭam māṟṟip pōṭṭut, teṉṉavaṉ eṉṟa col irāvaṇaṉik kuṟippatākak koṇṭu, akattiyar, tamiḻnāṭṭiṉ talimip poṟuppiliruntu, irāvaṇaṉi akaṟṟiṉār eṉak kūṟukiṟār. tam kūṟṟukku ātaravāka, tolkāppiya uriyāciriyar iḷampūraṇar, akattiyar, irāvaṇaṉi iciyil veṉṟār eṉakkūṟuvati eṭuttuk kāṭṭukiṟār. (irāvaṇaṉit tamiḻ nāṭṭiyāḷātapaṭi pōkkiṉa, kiṭṭutaṟkariya valiyiṉiyuṭiya, paḻami mutirnta, akattiyaṉ irāvaṇaṉ teṉticiyāṇṭami paṟṟit teṉṉavaṉ eṉṟār, akattiyaṉit, teṉticiyuyarnta noymmipōka, iṟivaṉukkuc cīroppa iruntāṉ, eṉpatu paṟṟik kaṭavuḷ eṉṟār. irāvaṇaṉ āḷutal, pāyirac cūttirattu uriyāciriyar kūṟiya uriyāṉum uṇarka." (maturik kāñci 40 - 42, uri) eṉṟa avar uriyiṉik kāṇka. nacciṉārkkiṉiyār tarum ipporuḷ viḷakkam muḻukka muḻukkap piḻipaṭṭatu. maturikkāñci, akattiyaṉārik kuṟippiṭavēyilli.

cilappatikāram, paḻiya tamiḻp purāṇakkatikaḷam, āriyap purāṇakkatikaḷum, paḻiya tamiḻp paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷum, āriyap paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷum, vēṟu pirittuk kāṇāvāṟu, orē iṉattic cērnta piḻampu vaṭivamāka vaṭikkappaṭa villiyāyiṉum, oṉṟō ṭoṉṟu kalakkat toṭaṅkiviṭṭa ki.pi. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭic cērnta oru pāṭṭu, intappāṭṭum, tamiḻi mutaṉmutalil tōṟṟu vittu, makkaḷukkuk kaṟpittatākak kūṟum akattiyap purāṇattiṉ mūlattik kāṇap periya aḷavil tuṇipuriyavilli . itu, intiraṉ makaṉ cayanta kumaraṉ, vāṉulaka āṭal makaḷ uruppaci, ākiyōri, akattiyar capittatik kūṟuvatōṭu, akattiyar kuṟitta vēṟu oru kuṟippiyum tarukiṟatu. atu, māṭalaṉ eṉṟa pārppaṉa yāttirikaṉ kumariyil nīrāṭac celvataṉ muṉ, akattiyar irunta maliyi valaṅ koṇṭatik kūṟukiṟatu.

"teyva mālvarit tirumuṉi aruḷa

eytiya cāpattu intira ciṟuvaṉoṭu

talikkōl tāṉattuc cāpam nīṅkiya





malipparum ciṟappiṉ vāṉavar makaḷir'’

- cilampu : araṅkēṟṟuk kāti :1 - 4





"māmaṟi mutalvaṉ māṭalaṉ eṉpōṉ

mātava muṉivaṉ malivalam koṇṭu ,

kumariyam peruntuṟi koḷkiyiṟ paṭintu”

- cilampu : aṭikkalakkāti : 13-15





aṭutta peruṅkāppiyamākiya maṇimēkali, mēlē kūṟiya kāppiyamām cilappatikārak kālattic cērntatākak koḷḷappaṭiṉum, atu, paḻiya tamiḻilakkiya marapukaḷ ellām, camaskiruta ilakkiya marapukaḷāl, tamiḻilak’ kiyattiliruntu muḻumiyāka veḷiyēṟṟappaṭṭuviṭṭa makkaḷiṉ vāḻkki muṟi, āriya ātikkattiṉ kīḻ muḻumiyākak koṇṭavarappaṭṭuviṭṭa makkaḷukku iṭṭu vaḻaṅkiya tamiḻp peyarkaḷellām, camaskirutap peyarkaḷāl iṭam koḷḷappaṭṭuviṭṭa kālattu “nikaḻccikaḷip paṟṟiyē kūṟukiṟatu. āṉāl, intak kāppiyamum, akattiyar paṟṟiya katikaḷil oru cilavaṟṟiyē koṇṭuḷatu. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu, nīrvēṭkiyuṟṟa kāntamaṉ eṉṟa cōḻa maṉṉaṉ vēṇṭa, amara muṉivaṉām akattiyaṉaṭiya nīrkkuṭam kaviḻat tōṉṟiya kāvirip peṇṇik kuṟippiṭukiṟatu.

"kañca vēṭkiyiṉ kāntamaṉ vēṇṭa,

amara muṉivaṉ akattiyaṉ taṉātu

karakam kaviḻtta kāvirip pāvi'’.

- maṇimēkali : patikam. 10-12





skānta purāṇam, inta vēṇṭukōḷ viṭuttati, intiraṉukku ēṟṟukiṟatu. camaskirutattil kāviri, kāvēri eṉa aḻikkappaṭukiṟatu. kāvēri, kavēraṉ eṉṟa muṉivaṉ makaḷākak kūṟuppaṭṭuḷatu. ikkati, maṇimēkaliyil, "tavānīrk kāvirip pāvi taṉ tāti kavēraṉ” (malarvaṉam pukka kāti : 55-56) "kavēra kaṉṉi” (pīṭiki kaṇṭu piṟappu uṇarnta kāti : 62) eṉak kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷatu. ikkati; āknēya purāṇattil kūṟappaṭṭiruppatāka, tiruvāḷar. ṭākṭar. u. vē. cuvāmināta ayyar avarkaḷ kūṟukiṟār. (maṇi: 9:52. aṭikkuṟippu kāṇka) maṟṟoṉṟu, akattiyar āṇiyiṭa, vāṉiṭit toṅkik koṇṭirunta acurarkkuriya kōṭṭiyi aḻittu, av veṟṟi kuṟittut tōḷil toṭi aṇintu, toṭittōḷ cempiyaṉ eṉum peyar koṇṭa cōḻa maṉṉaṉ oruvaṉ, taṉ talinakaril kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattil irupatteṭṭu nāṭkaḷ naṭipeṟum intiraviḻāviṉ pōtu vantirukkumāṟu, intiraṉi vēṇṭikkoḷḷa, avaṉum ataṟku icintu vantirunta nikaḻcciyik kuṟippiṭukiṟatu.

"ōṅku uyar malayattu aruntavaṉ urippat

tūṅkeyil eṟinta toṭittōḷ cempiyaṉ,

viṇṇavar talivaṉi vaṇaṅki muṉ niṉṟu

maṇṇakattu eṉ taṉ vāṉpati taṉṉuḷ

mēlōr viḻiya viḻākkōḷ eṭutta

nālēḻ nāḷiṉum naṉku iṉitu uṟiku eṉa,

amarar talivaṉ āṅkatu nērntatu.

- maṇimēkali : viḻāvaṟi kāti :3-9





itaṉ poruḷ : ōṅki uyarnta malikku uriya, ariya tavam purinta muṉivaṉ āṇiyiṭa, vāṉil toṅkik koṇṭirunta kōṭṭiyi aḻittal, toṭi aṇinta tōḷiṉi uṭiya cōḻaṉ, vāṉavar. talivaṉām intiraṉi vaṇaṅki, avaṉ muṉ niṉṟu "maṇṇulakil, eṉakku uriya ciṟanta talinakaril periyavarkaḷ virumpa viḻāk koṇṭāṭum, irupatteṭṭu nāṭkaḷilum vantu naṉku iruppāyāka" eṉa vēṇṭik koḷḷa, tēvar talivaṉum atu nērntāṉ eṉpatām.

koṇṭāṭa maṟantuviṭṭatāl, talinakarik kaṭal kōḷām pēraḻivukku uḷḷākkiviṭṭa intiraviḻā vanta varalāṟu itu. appāṭṭiṉ piṟpakutiyil, akattiyar kuṟitta maṟṟoru kuṟippum iṭam peṟṟuḷatu. atu, paracurāmaṉ, kṣattiriya aracarkaḷiyellām aḻittu varumpōtu, kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉattuk kāval teyvam, maṟintu koḷḷumāṟu āṇiyiṭa, kāntamaṉ eṉṟa aracaṉum tuyar tīrntuviṭṭatu eṉa akattiyaṉ aṟivikkum vari, talinakarik kākkumāṟu piṉṉōṉukku āṇiyiṭṭu, ammuṉivar tavappaḷḷiyil maṟintu vāḻnta nikaḻcciyik kuṟippiṭukiṟatu.

"maṉmaruṅku aṟutta maḻuvāḷ neṭiyōṉ

taṉmuṉ tōṉṟal takātu oḷi nī eṉak

kaṉṉi ēvaliṉ kānta maṉṉavaṉ....

............................

amara muṉivaṉ akattiyaṉ taṉātu

tuyar nīṅku kiḷaviyiṉ yāṉtōṉ ṟaḷavum

kakantaṉ kāttal”

- maṇimēkali: ciṟiceykāti:25-27 maṟṟum 35-36





itaṉ poruḷ: aracar kulatti aḻitta, maḻu eṉṟavāḷ ēntiya neṭiya tirumālām paracurāmaṉ muṉ tōṉṟuvatu takātu, nī maṟintu koḷ eṉak kaṉṉit teyvam āṇiyiṭa, kānta maṉṉavaṉ tēvamuṉivaṉām akattiyaṉ kūṟum, tuyar tīrntuviṭṭatu eṉṟa col kēṭṭu, nāṉ varum vari kakantā! kāppāyāka eṉpatām.)

ki.pi. mutalām āyirattāṇṭiṉic cērnta intak katikaḷ etilum, akattiyar, teyva āciriyaṉiṭamirunta tamiḻip peṟṟatu allatu kaṟṟatik kuṟippiṭavilli. ākavē, piṟkālattē vāḻnta akattiyaṉār, mutal tamiḻilakkaṇatti eḻutiya uṇmi nikaḻcci , mutal akattiyar, tamiḻic civaṉ allatu cuppiramaṇiyariṭamiruntu kaṟṟut teṉṉintiya makkaḷukkuk kaṟṟut tantār eṉṟa purāṇak katiyāka māṟiyatu. uriyāciriyarkaḷ perukip paḻaṅkālam kuṟitta purāṇakkatikaḷik kaṭṭiviṭṭa kipi. iraṇṭām āyirattāṇṭil nikaḻntirukka vēṇṭum. pāyiram eṉappaṭum tolkāppiya muṉṉuriyil nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟuvatē, purāṇak katikaḷil paṭumōcamāṉatu. akattiyaṉār, tammuṭiya māṇavar, tiraṇatūmākkiṉiyārit tam maṉivi lōpāmuttiriyi, vitarppattiliruntu poti malikku aḻittuvarumāṟu paṇittār. atē camayam tam maṉiviyi nāṉku kōl aḷaviṟkuk kuṟivāka aṇukakkūṭātu eṉṟum eccarittār. lōpā muttiriyum, tiraṇatūmākkiṉiyum, veḷḷam perukkeṭut tōṭum vikiyik kaṭakka nēriṭṭapōtu, avaḷ veḷḷattāl aṭittuc cellātirukkat tam kikkōli nīṭṭi atip paṟṟik koḷḷumāṟu vōpā muttiriyiṭam kūṟiṉār. avvāṟē āṟṟik kaṭakkum pōtu, veḷḷap perukku mikutiyāl kōlil, oru kōl aḷavē iṭi veḷi irukka nēriṭṭatu. avvakiyil tiraṇatūmākkiṉi āciriyariṉ taṭiyuttaravi mīṟiviṭṭār. kaṭuñciṉam uṭiya āciriyar, itik kēḷviyuṟṟutum, 'nīṅkaḷ iruvarum cuvarkkam pukak kaṭavīr allarāka" eṉac cāpam iṭṭār. atu kēṭṭa māṇavarum "nāṅkaḷ ceyyāta piḻikku eṅkaḷic capitta nīyum cuvarkkam pukak kaṭavīr allarāka" eṉa etirccāpam iṭṭār. tolkāppiyam irukka akataktiyam aḻintuviṭṭati viḷakka ikkati kūṟappaṭṭatu pōlum.





13. makkaḷ vāḻkki ki.pi. mutal ainnūṟu āṇṭukaḷ



paḻantamiḻar muṟi iṉṉamum valuvāka naṭipeṟṟatu



taṅkaḷiṭiyē pirāmaṇarkaḷ vāḻat toṭaṅkiviṭṭa niliyilum, akattiyaṉārum, tolkāppiyaṉārum āriya nākarīkattit tamiḻ nāṭṭil pukutta muyaṟci mēṟkoṇṭuviṭṭa niliyilum, makkaḷil perumpakutiyiṉar, āriya nākarīkam, taṅkaḷiṭiyē iṭam peṟātatu pōlavē vāḻalāyiṉar. paḻaṅkālattil, taṅkaḷ mūtātiyar vāḻnta atē muṟiyil vāḻntaṉar; kātal koṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷiṉ toḻilkaḷ, paḻakka vaḻakkaṅkaḷ, nampikkikaḷ ākiyavaṟṟil, muṉṉōrkaḷik kaṇmūṭip piṉpaṟṟiṉar. atāvatu, avi ciṟitum māṟupaṭavēyilli. kīḻē koṭuttirukkum eṭuttuk kāṭṭukkaḷ, kiṟittuva āṇṭu piṟappataṟku muntiya, eḷiya ciṟṟūr vāḻkki eṉpati uṇarntu koḷḷap paṭippavarkku tuṇi niṟkam. cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷ kaḻittuc, camaskirutak kaṟpaṉip pāṭalkaḷ, tamiḻar aṟivi aṭimi koṇṭu, avarkaḷiṉ, aḻakiya iyaṟkiyōṭiyinta iṉiya pāṭalkaḷi aḻikkat talippaṭṭapōtu kiviṭṭuviṭṭa taṅkaḷic cūḻa uḷḷa eḷiya iyaṟkiyōṭiyinta iṉiya pāṭalkaḷi aḻikkat talippaṭṭapōtu kiviṭṭu viṭṭa taṅkaḷic cūḻa uḷḷa eḷiya iyaṟkik kāṭcikaḷiliruntē tam akattūṇṭu talit, tām peṟṟirunta anniliyic camaskiruta nākarīkattōṭu toṭarpu koṇṭuviṭṭa anniliyilum tamiḻp pāṭalkaḷ peṟṟu vantaṉa eṉpatiyum avi uṇarttum.

mali nāṭṭil uṇavu uṟpatti

makkaḷiṉ mukkiyat toḻil, uṇavu uṟpatti. atu pala iṭaṅkaḷil viḷakkappaṭṭuḷatu. “vēṅki maraṅkaḷ niṟinta, veppam mikka civanta mēṭṭu nilattil, kār kālattup perumaḻiyāl īrappakkuvappaṭṭa nilattik, kīḻmaṇ mēlmaṇṇāki naṉku puḻutipaṭumāṟu palacāl uḻutu, vitittu, muḷittu iḷampayirāka irukkum pōtu, kāṟṟu uḷ nuḻiya vaḻi ceyyavum, varuttamiṉṟik kaḷi akaṟṟavum, payir naṉku taḻippataṟku iṭam tarumāṟu, nilattu iṟukkatti nekiḻvikkavum tāḷi aṭittu, atu ceytataṉ payaṉāy, payir naṉku ceḻittu vaḷarntirukkum pōtu kaḷi akaṟṟiviṭavē ilikaḷ taḻittup peruki, aṇmiyil muṭṭi iṭṭa peṇmayiliṉ aḻakiya tōki pōlum tōṟṟam vāynta kariya taṇṭukaḷ nīṇṭu, oru cērak katirviṭṭu, katirkaḷiṉ aṭiyilum, nuṉiyilum uḷḷa maṇikaḷ orucēra muṟṟik kāytta varaki aṟukkavum, tiṉiyik koyyavum, eḷ kaṟuttu eḷḷiṉaṅkāy muṟṟavum, vaḷamāṉa koṭikaḷil veḷḷavarik kottu, koyyum nili eyta, nilattil putittup puḷippēṟap paṇṇiya matuvip pul vēynta ciṟiya kuṭicikaḷil aṉivarkkum vaḻaṅka, kotikkum naṟu neyyil vaṟukaṭali tuḷḷa, cōṟu ākki muṭikkum ariya kāṭci oru pāṭṭil viḷakkappaṭṭuḷatu.

"veppuḷ viḷinta vēṅkic ceñcuval

kārppeyal kalitta perumpāṭṭu īrattup

pūḻi mayaṅkap pala uḻutu, vittip,

palli āṭiya, palkiḷic cevvik

kaḷikāl kaḻāliṉ, tōḷtolipu nanti,

peṇmayil puṉiṟṟup peṭi kaṭuppa nīṭik,

karuntāḷ pōki, oruṅku pīḷ virintu,

kīḻum mēlum eñcāmip palakāyttu,

vālitiṉ viḷinta putuvaraku ariyat

tiṉi koyyāk, kavvi kaṟuppa, avarik

koḻuṅkoṭi viḷarikkāy kōṭpata mācu,

nilam putip, paḻuṉiya maṭṭiṉ tēṟal

pulvēy kurampik kuṭitoṟum pakarntu,

naṟuneyk kaṭali icippac cōṟu aṭṭu"

puṟam : 120 : 1-74





malināṭṭil, uḻutu uṇavup poruṭkaḷi uṟpatti ceyya vēṇṭiya, iṉṟiyamiyāta oṉṟu aṉṟu. uḻuvatu vēṇṭātē, uṇavup poruḷ kiṭikkum nili kīḻvarum , pākkaḷil viḷakkappaṭṭuḷatu. “uḻuvar uḻutu viḷivikka” vēṇṭāmal, tāmē viḷiyum uṇavup poruṭkaḷ nāṉku uḷa.. mutalāvatu, ciṉṉañciṟu ilikaḷik koṇṭa mūṅkil nel, iraṇṭāvatu, palā, iṉiya cuviyuṭik kaṉikaḷik kāyttut toṅkaviṭum. mūṉṟāvatu, koḻu koḻu eṉa vaḷarntirukkam, vaḷḷik koṭi, kiḻaṅkukaḷi maṇṇuḷ viṭṭirukkum; nāṉkāvatu, aḻakiya niṟam vāynta, kuraṅkukaḷ pāya, malikaḷ, taṉṉiṭi kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum aṭikaḷiliruntu tēṉic coriyum” "uḻavar uḻātaṉa nāṉku payaṉ uṭittē;

oṉṟē, ciṟiyili vetiriṉ nelviḷi yummē;

iraṇṭē tīñcuvip palaviṉ paḻam ūḻk kummē;

mūṉṟē, koḻuṅkoṭi vaḷḷik kiḻaṅku vīḻkkummē;

nāṉkē, aṇiniṟa ōri pāytaliṉ mītaḻintu

tiṇineṭum kuṉṟam tēṉ cori yummē”..

puṟam : 109 : 3-8
