"kiḷikaḷ tōṟum, mali mukaṭukaḷ tōṟum tēṉaṭikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭut toṅka, palā mutalām maraṅkaḷil paḻaṅkaḷ kulikuliyākat toṅka, malikaḷiliruntu aruvikaḷ māli pōl uruṇṭōṭi vara, kollikaḷ . tōṟum varaku, cāmi, tiṉipōlum patiṉāṟuvakik kūlaṅkaḷ perukiviḷiya, immalināṭu, ekkālattum peruvaḷam uṭiyatu eṉa, ataṉkaṇ pallāṇṭu vāḻntu, atip pirintu pōka nērntapōtu viyantu pārāṭṭum perumiyuṭiyatu”..

“piracam tūṅkap, perumpaḻam tuṇara

vari veḷ ḷaruvi, māliyiṉ iḻitarak,

kūlam ellām pulampuka, nāḷum

mallaṟṟu amma immalikeḻu veṟpueṉap





pirintōr iraṅkum peruṅkal nāṭu"

- naṟṟiṇi : 93: 1-5





vēṭaṉ vāḻkki

mali nāṭṭu vaḻiyil, iruḷil varuvatāl nērum iṭiyūṟukaḷukku āḷākum vakiyil iravil vāraṟka eṉṟu, ōriḷam peṇ, taṉ kātalaṉukku eṭuttuk kūṟum aṟivuriyiṉi niṉivūṭṭum vakiyil, vēṭaṉ oruvaṉ vāḻkkiyil iṭam peṟum palvēṟu nikaḻccikaḷi, oru pulavar viḷakkiyuḷḷār. "cilirttu niṟkum mayirkaḷ aṭaṅkiya kaḻuttiṉi uṭiya āṇpaṉṟi, uṇaviṉmi yāl tōlāyvaṟṟi iḷittuviṭṭa peṇ paṉṟiyuṭaṉ ceṉṟu, muṟṟa vaḷarntu tiṉik katirkaḷi aḷaviṉṟit - tiṉṟu aḻittuviṭṭatu kaṇṭa kāṉavaṉ, malivaḻiyil, cellutaṟku ariya puḻi arukē patuṅkiyiruntu, ampu. eytu koṉṟa, veḷḷiya kompukaḷik koṇṭa anta āṇ paṉṟiyi aṇi ceytu koṇṭa kariya kūntali uṭiya avaṉ maṉivi, aṟuttut, taṉ cuṟṟattār vīṭukaḷukkellām ceṉṟu pakuttuk koṭukkum uyarnta mali nāṭṭukku uriya talivaṉē kaṭuñciṉam koṇṭa kaḷiṟṟiyāṉi, puliyiṉ varukiyi etirnōkkik kāttu niṟkum iravil, nī iṅku varutali, nī añcukiṉṟāyalli; āṉāl, nāṉ peritum añcukiṉṟēṉ; ākavē, pāmpu aṭaṅkiyirukkum, īrampaṭṭa puṟṟik karaṭik kūṭṭam kārmēkam pōlac cūḻntu koṇṭu, paṟittu eṭukkum, maliyakattuc ciṟuvaḻiyil iṉi vārātē"

"piṇarccuval paṉṟi, tōlmulip piṇavoṭu

kaṇikkāl ēṉal kimmikak kavartaliṉ,

kallatar aruppuḻi alkik, kāṉavaṉ

villil tanta veṇkōṭṭu ēṟṟip

puṉiyirum katuppiṉ maṉiyōḷ, toṇṭik

kuṭimuṟi pakukkum neṭumali nāṭa!

uravucciṉa vēḻam, uṟupuli pārkkum





iraviṉ añcāy; añcuval; araviṉ

īraḷip puṟṟam kāreṉa muṟṟi,

iritēr eṇku iṉam akaḻum

varicēr ciṟuneṟi, vārā tīmē;”

- naṟṟiṇi : 336





āṟṟuppaḷḷattākkil

āṟṟup paḷḷattākku nilattil eḻunta oru pāṭal:

"poykiyiṉ mīṉ tērntuṇṭa nārikaḷ, neṟkatirppu pōril ceṉṟu uṟaṅkum. neytal varumaḷavu vaḷattāl aḻaku peṟṟa vayalil, nel aṟuvaṭi ceyyum uḻavar, muki aviḻntu malarnta itaḻkaḷiyuṭiya āmpaliṉ akaṉṟa iliyālāṉa toṉṉiyil, matuvuṇṭu, teḷinta kaṭalali eḻuppum cīrāṉa olikaḷiṉ tāḷattiṟku ēṟpa āṭi makiḻum, naṉcey vaḷamikka nāṭṭiṟku uriya vēntē paḻam : uṇṇuvāṉ virumpi, ākāyattil uyarap paṟantu, malik kukikaḷ etirolikkap pērōli eḻuppiyavāṟē ceṉṟu, āṅkuḷḷa paḻamaraṅkaḷ, paruvam kaḻintu pōkak, kāyppu māṟiviṭṭatu kaṇṭu ēmāntu, varunti, vaṟitē mīḷum paṟavakiḷip pōla, varuvārkku variyātu vaḻaṅkuvaṉ eṉṟa uṉ pukaḻturatta vantu, uṉ pukaḻpāṭum nāṉ, vaṟitē mīḷak kaṭavaṉō ?”

"poyki nāri, pōrvil cēkkum

neytalam kaḻaṉi nellari toḻuvar

kūmpuviṭu meṉpiṇi aviḻnta āmpal

akalaṭi ariyal māntit teṇkaṭal

paṭutiri iṉcīrp pāṇi tūṅkum

meṉpula vippiṉ naṉṉāṭṭup poruṉa!

palkaṉi nacii, alku vicumpu ukantu,

perumali viṭarakam cilampa muṉṉip,

paḻaṉuṭip perumaram tīrnteṉak, kiyaṟṟup

peṟātu peyarum puḷḷiṉam pōla,

niṉ nacitara vantu, niṉ icinuval paricilēṉ





vaṟuviyēṉ peyarkō?"

(puṟam : 209 :1-12)





oru toḻil naṭipeṟum nilattiṉ potuvāṉa kuṟikkōḷkaḷum, mikamika eḷiya nikaḻccikaḷum, tamiḻnāṭṭup pulavarkaḷiṭamiruntu, appulavarkaḷ, piṟkālac camaskiruta ilakkiyak kaṟpaṉip pāṭalkaḷiṉ celvāk kiṉ aḻuttamāṉa piṭiyuḷ cikkik koḷvataṟku muṉṉar, naṉmika iṉiya pāṭalkaḷip peṟṟiruntaṉa. ivvāṟu pāṭukiṟatu oru pāṭṭu:

"maṭi aṭittirunta pallāṟṟāṉum māṇpuṟṟa kuḷattu nīr, maṭitiṟantuviṭṭa vaḻi, akkuḷattiṉiṉṟum, annīrōṭu puṟam pōntu, kālvāyi aṭintu, ceṉṟu tirumpiya, periya kompiṉiyuṭiya vāḷimīṉ, akkālvāyiliruntu, cēṟupaṭṭuk kiṭakkum vayaluḷ pukuntu ōṭi, āṅku uḻutukoṇṭirukkum erumik kaṭākkaḷiṉ kāṟcēṟupaṭṭa puḷḷikaḷiyuṭiya mēṟpuṟattōṭu, nilattic cēṟupaṭa māṟimāṟi uḻum uḻavarkaḷiṉ kik kōlukkum añcātu cerukkuṟṟu, cēṟṟiṉ mēl varappu ōramākavē ōṭi, mēṟkoṇṭu pōkamāṭṭātu taṭiyuṟṟup pōy, avvarap paṭiyilēyē puraḷat toṭaṅkum.

"māṇ peruṅkuḷam maṭinīr viṭṭeṉak ,

kāl aṇintu etiriya kaṇikkōṭṭu vāḷi,

aḷḷal am kaḻaṉi uḷvāy ōṭip

pakaṭucēṟu utitta puḷḷiveṇ puṟattuc

ceñcāl uḻavar kōlpuṭi matarip

piṅkāl ceṟuviṉ aṇimutal puraḷum

naṟṟiṇi : 340 8-8





āṟṟup paḷḷattākku vāḻkkimuṟi paṟṟiya iṉṉoru ceyyuḷ itō: "cenneruppu koḻuntu viṭṭu erivatu pōṉṟa centāmari malarkaḷiṉ iṭiyiṭiyē, katir muṟṟiya cennelliṉ tāḷi arintu ari ariyākap pōṭṭup paṇi muṭinta uḻavar, taṅkaḷukkuk kuṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭuvarum vaṇṭi, cēṟṟil āḻntuviṭiṉ, atupōkkac ciṟanta karumputtaṭikaḷi aṭukki iṭi yeṭukkum, pāyum puṉalāl vaḷammikka ūriṉ talivaṉē! neṟporikaḷ pōlum, puṉkampū malarkaḷ utirntu kiṭakkum akaṉṟa nīrttuṟikaḷ aḻaku peṟa , oḷivīcum neṟṟiyum, nalmaṇam mikka malarkaḷ cūṭiya, kāṇpataṟku iṉimi tarum tiraṇṭa kūntalum, māvaṭukkaḷ pōlum kaṇkaḷum, mārpil aciyum muttuc caramum, ivaṟṟāl pūm nuṇṇiya aḻakum uṭiyāḷōr iḷam parattiyoṭu, iṭiyaṟātu ōṭikkoṇṭē irukkum puṉalil, nēṟṟu viḷiyāṭi makiḻntaṉi eṉṟu ūrār palarum kūṟuvar. ākavē, uṟutiyāka, nī, nāṇam iḻantavaṉē”.

"eri acintaṉṉi tāmari iṭiyiṭi

arintukāl kuvitta cennel liṉiñar,

kaḷ koṇṭu maṟukum cākāṭu aḷaṟṟuriṉ,

āykarumpu aṭukkam pāypuṉal ūra:

periya, nāṇili maṉṟa; pori eṉap

puṉku aviḻ akaṉṟuṟip poliya, oṉtutal,

naṟumalark kāṇvarum kuṟumpal kūntal,

māḻinōkkiṉ, kāḻ iyal vaṉamuli

eḵkuṭi eḻilnalattu orutti yoṭu, neruni

vikupuṉal ayarntaṉi”.

akam : 116 : 1-10





kaṭaṟkari nilapparappil

kaṭaṟkari nilapparappām neytal nilattiṉ mukkiyat toḻil, mīṉpiṭittal, attoḻil, pāṭal puṉiyum tam pulamikku ēlā iḻivuṭip poruḷām eṉṟu pulavar karuta villi , ‘'katiriṭṭu muṟukkit tiruttamākac ceyyappaṭṭa valiya kayiṟṟāl piṉṉappaṭṭa “ periya periya mīṉpiṭi valikaḷi, iṭipōl muḻaṅkum alikaḷ ōyāk kaṭalil irumporuṭṭu, avvalikaḷ niṟiya ēṟṟappaṭṭa tōṇiyip pākarkaḷāl piṇittuk koṇṭu cellappaṭum. aṭakkutaṟku curiya kaḷiṟṟip pōlap, paratavar celuttuvar malarkaḷ utirntu kiṭakkum putumaṇal paranta kāṉaṟcōliyil, puṉṉi, taṉ nuṇṇiya makarantap poṭikaḷi; ōyātu vīcik koṇṭēyirukkum. kīḻk kāṟṟu vantu mōtuntōṟum, nārikaḷiṉ veṇṇiṟa mutukil tūvit tūrkkum teḷinta kaṭaṟkarikkaṇ niṟkum kaṇṭal maraṅkaḷi vēliyākak koṇṭa ūr"

"vaṭikkatir tiritta vaṉñāṇ peruvali

iṭikkural puṇarip puvattu iṭumār,

niṟiyap peyta ampi, kāḻōr

ciṟiyarum kaḷiṟṟiṉ, paratavar oyyum

pōtaviḻ, putumaṇal kāṉal puṉṉinuṇ tātu

koṇṭal acivaḷi tūkku toṟum, kurukiṉ

veṇpuṟam ōciya vārkkam, teṇkaṭal kaṇṭal vēliya ur".

- naṟṟiṇi : 74 : 1-4 : 6-10





piṉvarum aḻakiya ceyyuḷil, oru mīṉavap peṇ taṉ uṟaviṉar, virintu vīṭu tirumpamāṭṭār eṉpatik kūṟit, taṉ kātalaṉit taṉ maṉiyil iravu taṅkic cellumāṟu aḻikkiṟāḷ. "kīḻkkaṭaliliruntu eḻuntu, nalla cenniṟak katirkaḷip parappip pakaṟpoḻuti oḷimayamākkiviṭṭa ñāyiṟu mēṟku maliyil maṟintuviṭum, tuṉpattit tūtu pōl muṉpōkkip piṉṉar vantu taṅkiviṭṭa tuyartaru mālip poḻuti, oḷivīcum vaḷi aṇinta makaḷir tattam māḷikikaḷil, etirkoṇṭu varavēṟka, mīṉ koḻuppi urukki eṭutta neyvārttu ēṟṟiya oḷiviḷakkukaḷiṉ pēroḷi vīcum nīla niṟakkaṭalil eḻum alikaḷ mōtum kariyiṭattē uḷḷa, āravārap pēroli eppōtum olittuk koṇṭē irukkum em pākkattil iṉṟu iruntu, emmaṉiyil emmōṭu taṅkiyiruppiṉ, uṉakku ētēṉum kuṟapiāṭu uḷatāmō? civanta nūlāl, vaḷittu vaḷittu muṭiyiṭṭuc ceyta aḻakiya vali kēṭuṟṟup pōmāṟu aṟuttuk koṇṭu ōṭiviṭṭa, etirppaṭum etiyum kollavalla cuṟāmīṉit tam vallamiyellām kāṭṭi, akappaṭuttik koḷḷātu em cuṟṟattār vaṟitē varuvār allar. ākavē taṅkic ceṉmō"

"kuṇakaṭal ivarntu kurūukkatir parappip

pakalkeḻu celvaṉ kuṭamali maṟiyap

pulampuvantu iṟutta puṉkaṇ māli

ilaṅkuvaḷi makaḷir viyaṉnakar ayara,

mīṉniṇam tokutta ūṉ ney oṇcuṭar,

nīlniṟap parappil tayaṅkutiri utippak

karicērpu irunta kaleṉ pākkattu

iṉṟu nī ivaṇi āki, emmoṭu

taṅkiṉ evaṉō? teyya; ceṅkāl

koṭumuṭi avvali pariyap pōkiya

kōṭ cuṟāk kuṟitta muṉpoṭu

vēṭṭam vāyātu emar vā ralarē”

naṟṟiṇi : 215





neytal nilattu makkaḷiṉ vāḻkki vāyppukaḷ, oru pāṭṭil viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. "nelli aṟuvaṭi ceyyum periya uḻavar, ceṅkatir parappik kāyum ñāyiṟṟiṉ veyil veppam tāṅkamāṭṭāta pōtu, teḷinta kaṭal nīril pāyntu kaḷi tīrvar. tiṇṇiya mīṉpiṭi paṭakām timili uṭiya valiya paratavar, uṇṭārkku uram ūṭṭum matuvuṇṭu, iṉiya kuravik kūttiṉ tāḷattiṟku ēṟpa āṭit tiḷippar. kaṭal alikaḷ tūvum tūvalāl taḻittu vaḷarntu puṉṉiyiṉ tēṉ niṟinta malarkaḷāl āṉa māli aṇinta āṭavar, oḷivīcum vaḷi aṇinta makaḷirkku nīrāṭalil valakki koṭuttu utavuvar, vaṇṭukaḷ vantu moykk malarnta, kuḷirnta naṟumaṇamē nāṟik koṇṭirukkum kāṉalil taḻittirukkum kaṭal muḷḷi māli aṇinta vaḷi aṇinta makaḷir, vāṉaḷāva vaḷarntirukkum paṉiyiṉ nuṅkiṉ iṉiya nīrum, potivuṟa vaḷarntirukkum karumpiṉ 225makkaḷ vāḻkki .... cāṟum, maṇal mēṭṭil niṟkum teṉṉiyiṉ iṉiya iḷanīrum ākiya mūṉṟu nīriyum kalantu kuṭittuviṭṭu, maḻinīr, āṟṟunīr, ūṟṟu nīr eṉṟa munnīrām kaṭal nīril pāyntu āṭuvar".

"nel ariyum iruntoḻuvar,

ceññāyiṟṟu veyil muṉiyiṉ,

teṇkaṭal tiri micip pāyuntu,

tiṇtimil vaṉ paratavar

veppuṭiya maṭṭum uṇṭu

taṇkuravic cīrtūṅkuntu!

tūval kalitta tēmpāy puṉṉi

mel iṇark kaṇṇi milinta mintar,

elvaḷi makaḷir talikki tarūuntu,

vaṇṭupaṭa malarnta taṇnaṟum kāṉal

muṇṭakak kōti oṇtoṭi makaḷir

irumpaṉiyiṉ kuṟumpinīrum,

pūṅkarumpiṉ tīñcāṟum,

oṅku maṇal kuvavuttāḻit

tīnīrōṭu uṭaṉ virāay

munnīr uṇṭu. munnīr pāyum"

-puṟam : 24:1-16





("munnīr uṇṭu - (paṉi nuṅkiṉ nīr, karumpiṉ cāṟu, veṅkiṉ iḷanīr - ) munnīr (āṟṟunīr, ūṟṟunīr, maḻi nīr) pāyum" eṉṟa variyil aḻakiya collōviyam amintiruppatu kāṇka)

talivaṉi, ōr iravu taṅkit talivikku āṟutal aḷikkumāṟu tōḻi vaṟpuṟuttum oru ceyyuḷ, neytal nilattu vāḻkki nili, vāḻkki muṟikaḷ paṟṟiya maṟṟoru kāṭciyit tarukiṟatu. mīṉ uṇṇum paciya kālkaḷi uṭiya veṇṇiṟak kokkuk kūṭṭam cevvēḷ murukaṉ mārpil kiṭantu oḷiviṭum veṇ muttāram pōla, cevvāṉattil eḻuntu variciyāka paṟantu. pōmāṟu, pakaṟpoḻuti mella mellak kaḻittu viṭṭa ñāyiṟu, mēṟku maliyil ceṉṟu maṟintu viṭṭāṉ. mikka nāṇattiyum melliya cāyaliyum uṭiya ciṟiyavaḷākiya ivaḷō, taṉ ciṟanta aḻaku keṭumāṟu, uṉ pirivukku ēṅki, aḻakiya kuḷirnta kaṇkalaṅka ōyātu aḻat toṭaṅki viṭṭāḷ. taliva! ataṉālum, uppaṅkaḻiyil uḷḷa cuṟā mīṉ kuttiyatāl puṇṇuṟṟa kālkaḷiyuṭiya uṉ kōvēṟu kaḻuti, nīr niṟintu kaṟuttut tōṉṟum nīṇṭa kaḻiyik kaṭakkamāṭṭātu melintu viṭṭatu. ātalālum, valiya villēnti uṉ uṭaṉ varum vīrarkaḷōṭu, ivviravil cellātu, iḷippāṟi, paṉimaraṅkaḷ ōṅki vaḷarntirukkum veṇmaṇal parantu kiṭakkum tōṭṭaṅ kaḷil, taṉ tuṇiyi aḻippāṉ vēṇṭi, aṉṟil akavum avviṭattil, ciṉṉam ciṟu pūṅkottuk kaḷiyuṭiya neytal vaḷarntukiṭakkum, uppaṅkaḻi cūḻnta eṅkaḷ nāṭṭiṉ kaṇ, nī taṅkic celvāyāka; avvāṟu taṅkic celvatāl varuvatoru kēṭu ētēṉum uṇṭō?”

”neṭuvēḷ mārpiṉ āram pōlac ,

cevvāy vāṉam tīṇṭi mīṉ aruntum

piṅkāl kokkiṉ niṟipaṟi ukappa

elli pipayak kaḻippik, kuṭavayiṉ

kal cērn taṉṟē, palkatir ñāyiṟu;

matareḻil maḻikkaṇ kaluḻa, ivaḷē

perunāṇ aṇitta ciṟumeṉ cāyal

māṇnalam citiya, ēṅki āṉātu.

aḻaltoṭaṅ kiṉaḷē; perum; ataṉāl

kaḻiccuṟā eṟinta puṇtāḷ attiri

neṭunīr iruṅkaḻip parimelintu acii,

valvil iḷiyaroṭu ellic cellātu

cērntaṉi celiṉē, citikuvatu uṇṭō ?

peṇṇi ōṅkiya veṇmaṇal paṭappi

aṉṟil akvum āṅkaṇ,

ciṟukural neytal emperuṅkaḻi nāṭē”

akam : 120





maṇal paranta pāliyil vaṟaṇṭa pāliyil, vāḻkki nili mikavum kaṭiṉamām."tūya veḷḷi āṭiyi virittu viṭṭāṟpōl tōṉṟumāṟu veyil virintu kāyum, kōṭip paruvaram nīṇṭa maliccāralil, koṭiya paciyuṭiya cennāy, vāṭiya mariyāvik koṉṟu vēṇṭu maṭṭum tiṉṟu, viṭṭoḻitta eñciya iṟicci neṭuntolivil uḷḷa - vēṟṟu nāṭṭiliruntu, kaṭattaṟku ariya appāliyik kaṭantu cellum vaḻip pōvōrkku uṇṇum uṇavākum koṭumi mikka, veppam mikunta ariya vaḻi"

"mukil virittaṉa veyilavir uruppiṉ

eṉṟūḻ nīṭiya kuṉṟattuk kavāaṉ,

oypacic cennāy, uyaṅkumari tolicci

ārīntaṉa oḻinta miccil, cēynāṭṭu

arumcuram celvōrkku valci ākum

vemmi āriṭi

- naṟṟiṇi : 43 : 1-6





pāli, taṉakkē uriya iṉiya aḻakiya kāṭcikaḷip peṟāmalilli. "vaḷinta ciṟakiṉi uṭiya paṟavakiḷiṉ uḷḷaṅkāl cuvaṭukaḷ varici variciyākap poruntiya, nīrvaṟṟiya iṭaṅkaḷ tōṟum melliya nuṇmaṇal paṭintu kiṭakka, melleṉa vīcum vāṭikkāṟṟu vaṟaṇṭu. mōtiyatāl, karumpiṉ veṇṇiṟap pū, putal tōṟum oru cēra virintu, aracaṉukku vīcappaṭum kavari pōl āṭi aḻaku ceyya, koṇṭal koṇṭalāṉa mēkaṅkaḷ nīṅkic celvatāl, māṟi māṟik kaṇ viḻittuk kāṭṭuvatupōl, ñāyiṟu tōṉṟit tōṉṟi maṟiya, pakaṟkālam kaḻiya, mālip poḻutoṭu irākkālam vantu cērtalum, paṉi viḻuntu kāl koḷḷum".

"koṭum ciṟi

uḷḷaṭi poṟitta variyuṭit taliya

nīr aḻi maruṅkiṉ īrayir tōṉṟa





vaḷarāvāṭi uḷarpunaṉi tīṇṭaliṉ,

vēḻa veṇpū virivāṉa palavuṭaṉ

vēntuvīcu kavariyiṉ pūmputal aṇiya,

maḻikaḻi vicumpiṉ māṟi ñāyiṟu

viḻittu imippatupōl viḷaṅkupu aṟiya

elli pōkiya poḻutiṉ eluṟap

paṉikkāl koṇṭa piyuḷ yāmam”.

- naṟṟiṇi : 241 : 1-10





eṉṟālum, makkaḷ peruvāriyāka, atik kaṭantu cella vēṇṭiyiruntum, pāli, peritum , pāḻuṟṟē iruntatu. ‘'pāli vaḻiyik kaṭantu cellum makkaḷ, aṟuttuppōṭṭa piraṇṭik koṭi, iṭiyāl tākkuṇṭu citaṟuṇṭa paciya pāmpiṉ tuṇṭukaḷ pōla vaḻiyarukē vaṟitē vīḻntu kiṭakkum appālik kāṭṭil, uppu vaṇikar kūṭṭam viṭṭup pōṉa kal aṭuppil, valiya villiyuṭiya āṟali kaḷvar, ūṉi, maṇam uṇṭākap puḻukki uṇpar”,

“āṟucel mākkaḷ aṟutta piraṇṭi

ēṟupeṟu pāmpiṉ pintuṇi kaṭuppa

neṟi ayal tiraṅkum attam, veṟikoḷa

umaṇcāttu iṟanta oḻikal aṭuppiṉ

nōṉcili maḻavar ūṉ puḻukku ayarum”.

akam : 179 : 5-9.





āṭumāṭukaḷiṉ mēypulamām mulli nilam

vāḻvataṟku naṉmika iṉiya iṭam mulli . "maḻi kāl iṟaṅkip peyyum maḻip paruvattup, perumaḻi peytu ōynta kaṭici nāḷaṉṟu, paṉinār koṇṭu pala kāliṭṭup piṉṉiya melliya uṟi, pālpāṉiyōṭu oru kiyil toṅka, tīkkaṭikōl mutalām karuvikaḷi oru cēra iṭṭuc curukkiya tōl piyiyum, maḻikku maṟippākum paṉi ōliyālāṉa kuṭiyiyum mutukil kaṭṭik koṇṭu, pālvili kūṟi viṟṟuc cellum iṭiyaṉ, mēypulattil, ciṟuciṟu tuḷikaḷāka ōyātu viḻuntu koṇṭē irukkum maḻiccāral, taṉ uṭaliṉ oru puṟatti naṉittuk koṇṭiruppatum, poruṭpaṭuttāmal, kikkōli nilattil ūṉṟi, oru kāli ataṉ mēl vittu, ōyvāṉa niliyil niṉṟavāṟē , itaḻkaḷik kuvittu avaṉ eḻuppum vīḷi oliyik kēṭkam āṭukaḷ, piṟarkkuriya pulam ceṉṟu mēyntuviṭāmal, avvolikku mayaṅki, avaṉikcūḻa aṭaṅki niṟkum ammulli nilattil, iravu neṭum poḻutu āyiṉum viruntiṉar vantuviṭṭāl makiḻntu varavēṟpavaḷum, kaṇavaṉ kūṟiya coṟpiḻiyātu il iruntu nallaṟam āṟṟum kaṟpuṭiyāḷum, melliya cāyalum iḷami nalam māṟā aḻakuṭiyāḷum ākiya illattaraci vāḻkiṉṟa vīṭu uḷatu."

"vāṉ iṭuppu corinta vayaṅkupeyal kaṭināḷ

pāṇi koṇṭa palkāl mel uṟi,

ñelikōl kalappi ataḷoṭu curukkip

paṟippuṟattu iṭṭa pāl noṭi iṭiyaṉ,

nuṇpal tuvali oru tiṟam naṉippat

taṇṭukāla vitta oṭuṅkunili, maṭiviḷi

ciṟutalit toḻuti ēmārttu alkum

puṟaviṉatuvē, poyyā yāṇar

allil āyiṉam, viruntuvariṉ uvakkum

mulli cāṉṟa kaṟpiṉ

melliyal kuṟumakaḷ uṟiviṉ ūrē."

naṟṟiṇi : 142





mulliyil mikavum iṉpam tarum paruvam iḷavēṉil, "paruvavaravāl ilikaḷellām paḻuttu utirntu pōṉa piṭavamarattuk kiḷikaḷellām arumpalarntu niṟintu viṭṭaṉa; putalkaḷ tōṟum paṭarntu kiṭakkum mullik koṭikaḷ pūttukkuluṅkiṉa ; koṉṟi maraṅkaḷ, poṉ pōlum malarkaḷi īṉṟaṉa. kāyāviṉ ciṟuciṟu kiḷikaḷil nīlamaṇi pōlum" paṉmalarkaḷ niṟintu viṭṭaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟkellām kāraṇamāṉa kārkālam toṭaṅki viṭṭatu. peṇmāṉ, maruṇṭu maruṇṭu viḻikkum aḻakiya kaṇkaḷi uṭiyavāya taṉ kuṭṭikaḷōṭu kaḷarnilam nōkki ōṭi viṭavē, avaṟṟiṉpāl koṇṭa āruākkātal neñcōṭu, avaṟṟit tēṭi aliyum āṇmāṉ atō virikiṟatu; kāṇpāyāka.'’

"iliyil piṭavam īrmalar arumpap,

putal ivar taḷavam pūṅkoṭi aviḻap

poṉ eṉak koṉṟi malara, maṇieṉap

paṉmalark kāyā kuṟuñciṉi kañalak,

kār toṭaṅ kiṉṟē kāli......

........................

kaḻip peyar kaḷaril pōkiya maṭamāṉ

viḻikkaṇ pētiyoṭu iṉaṉ iruntu ōṭak

kāmar neñcamoṭu akalāt

tēṭūu niṉṟa irali ēṟē”

- naṟṟiṇi : 1 - 15 : 6 - 10





kēṭka iṉippaṉa, mullikkāṭṭu olikaḷ, “tēṉaṭikaḷ kaṭṭappeṟṟirukkum neṭiya muṭikaḷiyuṭiya maliccāralil kuvinta ilikaḷi neruṅkak koṇṭa mucuṇṭiyil, vāṉattil viḷaṅkum mīṉ kūṭṭam pōlak 'kāṭci tarumāṟu, veṇṇiṟap pūkkaḷ pūttuk kuluṅkum naḷḷiraviṉ naṭuyāmattil, āṭṭuk kuṭṭikaḷi, maḻi maṟikkum ōlip pāyi mutukil koṇṭavāṟē, oru cērakkoṇṭu kāttukkiṭakkum iṭiyaṉ, iṉiya maṇam kamaḻum mulli ‘ malarkaḷit tōṉṟip pūkkaḷōṭu, vaṇṭukaḷ vantu moykkumāṟu toṭutta māliyi, atiṉiṉṟum nīrt tuḷikaḷ ōḻuka, taliyil aṇintu koṇṭu, kuḷir pōkka, eḻuppiya ciṟu tīyil taṉ uḷḷaṅkikaḷik kāya vittukkoṇṭē, āṭṭu maṟikaḷi viraṭṭuvāṉ vēṇṭi eḻuppum nīṇṭa oli, tiṉippuṉatti aḻikkavarum ciṟiya kaṇkaḷi uṭiya paṉṟiyiṉ peruṅkūṭṭatti viraṭṭuvāṉ vēṇṭi, attiṉip puṉattik kāttukiṭappār, avi varuvatu aṟintu ūtum, periya kompiṉ ōciyoṭu kalantu vantu olikkum mullikkāṭṭiṉ iṭiyē uḷḷa nāṭu"

"tēmpaṭu cimayap pāṅkarp pampiya

kuviyili mucuṇṭi veṇpūk kuḻiya

vāṉ eṉap pūtta pāṉāḷ kaṅkul,

maṟitturūut tokutta paruppuṟa iṭiyaṉ

taṇkamaḻ mulli, tōṉṟiyoṭu virii

vaṇṭupaṭat toṭutta nīrvār kaṇṇiyaṉ,

aitupaṭu koḷḷi aṅki kāyak

kuṟunari uḷampum kāriruḷ neṭu viḷi,

ciṟutāṉi paṉṟip peruniri kaṭiya

mutippuṉam kāvalar niṉittiruntu ūtum

karuṅkōṭṭu ōciyoṭu oruṅku vantu icikkum".

akam : 94 :1 - 11





"vaṭṭavaṭṭak kaṇkaḷiyum kūriya vāyalakiyum uṭiya peṇ kākki, iḷamiyāl naṭuṅkum iṟakukaḷiyuṭiya taṉ kuñcukaḷi aṉittavāṟē taṉ iṉak kākkikaḷiyum kūvi aḻittu, kuṟukiya kālkaḷi naṭṭukkaṭṭap peṟṟu, uṇavup poruṭkaḷi niṟiyac cērttu vittirukkum maṉikaḷiṉ muṉpuṟattil iṭṭuvittirukkum, karuṇik kiḻaṅkiṉ porikkaṟi yoṭu kalanta, cennel ariciyālāṉa veṇcōṟṟut tiraḷit teyvaṅkaḷukku iṭum paliyoṭu kavarntu uṇṇak kūṭiyirukkum"

"koṭuṅkaṇ kākkik kūrvāyppēṭi,

oruṅkuciṟip piḷḷi taḻīik kiḷi payirntu

karuṅkaṇ karuṇic cennel veṇcōṟu

kuruṭip paliyoṭu kavariya, kuṟuṅkāl

kūṟuṭi naṉmaṉi kuḻuviṉa irukkum"

- naṟṟiṇi : 367 51 - 5





potumakkaḷiṉ ivvāḻkki viḷakkam vaṟumiyil vāṭum oru vīṭṭiṉ paṭappiṭippōṭu muṭiyum. cōṟu ākkutal, aṟavē maṟantu pōṉamiyāl, tēyvuṟātu kaṭṭiyatu kaṭṭiyapaṭiyē kōṭu uyarntu niṟkum aṭuppil, kāḷāṉkaḷ pūttuk kiṭakka, uṭali vāṭṭum paciyāl varuntip, pāl vaṟaṇṭu pōkavē, tōl melintu, pāl varum tuḷiyum tūrntu payaṉaṟṟup pōṉa muliyil vāy vittuc cuvikkuntōṟum, pāl vārāmi kaṇṭu aḻat toṭaṅkiviṭum taṉ makaḷiṉ mukattik kāṇumtoṟum nīrāl niṟintuviṭum kaṇkaḷākiviṭum maṉiyōḷ'’.

"āṭunaṉi maṟanta kōṭuuyar aṭuppiṉ

āmpi pūppat tēmpupaci uḻavāp ,

pāal iṇmiyiṉ tōlōṭu tiraṅki

illi tūrnta pollā vaṟumuli

cuvittoṟu aḻuum taṉ makattumukam nōkki

nīroṭu niṟinta īritaḻ maḻikkaṇeṉ

maṉiyōḷ"

- puṟam : 164: 1-7





ciṟutoḻilkaḷ

ikkāla aḷavil, ciṟṟūrkaḷil iṉṟu kāṇappaṭum attaṉit toḻilkaḷum ceyalpaṭa vēṇṭiya aḷavukku, vāḻkkittaram vekuvāka muṉṉēṟiviṭṭatu. it toḻil kaḷ, pulavarkaḷukku nalla uvamikaḷāka utavalāyiṉa, ‘irum pip payaṉpaṭuttip pala karuvikaḷi vaṭittuk koṭukkum kollaṉ ulikkaḷattil, kāycciya irumpi vicittu aṭik kum cammaṭṭiyiṉ aṭikaḷit tāṅki urukkulintu pōkātu niṟkum paṭṭaṭik kallip pakivarāl aḻivuṟṟup pōkā oru peru vīraṉukku uvami kūṟiyuḷḷār oru pulavar”.

“irumpupayaṉ paṭukkum karuṅkik kollaṉ

vicittu eṟi kūṭamoṭu porūum

ulikkal aṉṉa vallā ḷaṉṉē”

-puṟam : 170 : 15 - 17





maruta nilattil kuḷattiṉ aṭikariyil vaḷarntirukkum, aram pōlum muṭkaḷi uṭiya pirampuk koṭi pōlum koṭiyi uṭiya āmpaliṉ, nīr kuṟiya nīr maṭṭattukku mēl uyarntu curuṇṭirukkum ili, acintu acintu varum vāṭikkāṟṟu vīcuntoṟum, virintu aṭaṅkum kāṭcikkuk kollaṉ ulikkaḷattil kāṟṟu aṭikka vicittu iḻuttuviṭum turuttiyi uvami kāṭṭiyuḷḷār oru pulavar"

"'paḻaṉap poyki aṭikarip pirampiṉ

aravāy aṉṉa ammuḷ neṭuṅkoṭi

aruvi āmpal akalaṭi tuṭakki,

acivaral vāṭi tūkkaliṉ, ūtuli

vicivāṅku tōliṉ vīṅkupu ñekiḻum"

- akam : 96 : 3-7





tī ōṅki erintu aṭaṅkiya niliyil, neyyil pakkuvap paṭṭu mitakkum iṟiccit tuṇṭukaḷukkup parutti nūṟkum peṇ, koṭṭi nīkki aṭittukkuvittu vittirukkum paruttik kuviyalkaḷi uvamiyākkiyuḷḷār oru pulavar,

"paruttip peṇṭiṉ paṉuval aṉṉa

neruppuc ciṉam taṇinta niṇam tayaṅku koḻumkuṟi".

-puṟam : 125 : 1-2





iraṭṭi iraṭṭiyākak kavitta katirkaḷik kuṟṟip puṭittu eṭutta varakariccōṟṟiyum, kālnaṭic cāṇaṅkaḷāl āṉa eru kuvintu kiṭakkum teruvil, kālattē viḷintirukkum vēḷiyiṉ veṇpūkkaḷi, veḷḷiya tayiril mitakka viṭṭu āycciyar ākkiya puḷik kuḻampayum vayiṟāra uṇṭu avarik kāykaḷik koyyum toḻilāḷiyip paṭampiṭittuḷḷār oru pulavar.

"kavikkatir varakiṉ avippuṟu ākkal,

tāteru maṟukil pōtoṭu potuḷiya

vēḷi veṇpū veṇtayirk koḷīi

āymakaḷ aṭṭa ampuḷi mitavi

avari koyyunar āra māntum"

puṟam : 215 :1-5





kāṇpavar karutti īrkkum takutiyaṟṟatāṉa iḻi toḻilum pulavarkaḷiṉ kaviyārvattit taṇittu viṭavilli. "ūril viḻāt toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu. appōtu paṟi koṭṭutal pōlum paṇi āṟṟa vēṇṭuvatu avaṉ , kaṭami. ākavē āṅkum cella vēṇṭum. piḷḷip pēṟṟiṟku uriya kālam. maṉivikku vantu viṭṭatu. appōtu uṭaṉiruntu avaḷukku utava vēṇṭuvatum iṉṟiyamiyātatu; ākavē, vīṭṭiṟkum cella vēṇṭum, itaṟkiṭiyil ñāyiṟum maṟiyat toṭaṅkiviṭṭāṉ. maḻi peyyavum toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu. inniliyil kaṭṭilukkuk kayiṟu piṉṉum toḻiṟkaṭami mēṟkoṇṭu viṭṭa attoḻilāḷi, appaṇiyi virintu muṭittu viṭṭu mēṟkūṟiya irupaṇikaḷ kuṟittu viriyat tuṭikkum niliyil, piṉṉum vārkōtta avaṉ ki ūci viriyum viriviṉic colla iyalātu. avviriviṉit, taṉṉi velvār yārēṉum uḷarēl varuka eṉa vañciṉam kūṟi vantirukkum vēṟṟūr vīraṉōṭu pōriṭṭu vellat tuṭikkum, āttimāli aṇintu, avvūr vīraṉiṉ uḷḷa viriviṟku uvami kāṭṭiyuḷḷār oru pulavar”. “cāṟutalik koṇṭeṉap, peṇīṟṟu uṟṟeṉap,

paṭṭa māri, ñāṉṟa ñāyiṟṟuk

kaṭṭil niṇakkum iḻiciṉaṉ kiyatu

pōḻtūṇṭu ūciyiṉ virintaṉṟu mātō!

ūrkoḷa vanta porunṉoṭu

ārpuṉi teriyal neṭuntaṉi pōrē"

-puṟam : 82





ūril nikaḻa irukkum viḻākkaḷi ūr makkaḷukku aṟivikkum aṟivippāḷarkaḷākap paḻaṅkālattil kuyavarkaḷum paṇipurintuḷḷaṉar. "arumpu kaṭṭiya katir pōl tōṉṟum, oḷivīcum malarkaḷik kottuk kottāka īṉum nocci māli aṇintu, āṟupōl akaṉṟu nīṇṭa teruvil, ūril uḷḷār yāvarum kēṭka, ‘ivvūril, iṉṉa nāḷil iṉṉa viḻā naṭipeṟum; viḻāviṟ paṅku koḷḷa varuka' eṉa aṟivittuc cellum aṟivāl mutirnta kuyavaṉē. āmpal neruṅka vaḷarumaḷavu vaḷammikka vayalkaḷiyum, poykiyiyum uṭiya ūrukku, viḻā aṟivikkac celluviyāyiṉ, avvūr makaḷiri, 'kūriya paṟkaḷum akaṉṟa alkulum uṭiyīr' eṉa avar nalam pārāṭṭi aḻittu, 'teṟittu ici eḻuppuvataṟku ēṟpuṭiya narampu kaṭṭiya yāḻil pāṭattakum pāṭalkaḷi iṉitāka icikkavalla uṅkaḷurp pāṇaṉ ceyyum tuṉpaṅkaḷukkuk kaṇakkila; avaṉ urippaṉa ellām meyyē pōl tōṉṟiṉum, aṉittum poyyē; poyyi mūṭi maṟittu meyyē pōl kūṟuvatil vallavaṉ appāṇaṉ. ākavē avaṉ kūṟuvatil peritum viḻippāy iruppīrkaḷāka eṉa itiyum aṟivittuvara iyalumō? aṟivittu vara vēṇṭukiṟēṉ eṉap peṇ orutti, kuyavaṉip pārttuk kūṟiyatāka oru ceyyuḷ :

"kaṇṇi kaṭṭiya katira aṉṉa

oṇkural noccit teriyal cūṭi,

āṟukiṭan taṉṉa akalneṭum teruvil,

cāṟueṉa nuvalum mutuvāy kuyava!

ituvum āṅkaṇ nuvaṉṟiciṉ mātō!

āmpal amaṉṟa tīmperum paḻaṉattup

poyki ūrkkup pōvō yākik,

kikavar narampiṉ paṉuval pāṇaṉ

ceyta allal palkuva; vi eyiṟṟu

aitu akal alkul makaḷir! ivaṉ

poypoti koṭuñcol ōmpumiṉ eṉavē..

- naṟṟiṇi : 200





kuyavaṉ, oruvakiyil kōyil pūcāriyākavum iruntu pali koṭuppatum ceyvaṉ. paḻamiyum veṟṟic ciṟappum vāynta mūtūril parantu akaṉṟa ūr maṉṟattil, viḻāt toṭaṅkuvataṟku muṉṉar, nīla maṇi pōlum malark kottukkaḷi uṭiya noccimāli aṇinta , uruvālum, uḷ aṟivālum mūtta kuyavaṉ, tāṉ iṭumpaliyi uṇṇumāṟu, pali uṇṇum kaṭavuḷiyum, kākkikaḷiyum aḻippatik kūṟukiṟatu piṟitoru ceyyuḷ.

"maṇikkural noccit teriyal cūṭip,

palikaḷ ārkip pārmutu kuyavaṉ .

iṭupali nuvalum akaṉtali maṉṟattu

viḻavuttalik koṇṭa paḻaviṟal mūtūr".

- naṟṟiṇi : 293 : 1-4





kuyavaṉiṉ mantirak kittiṟaṉ iṉṉamum aḻittu. viṭavilli. kuḻantikaḷi, ‘akki’ eṉappaṭum tōl nōy paṟṟik koṇṭatum, avaṟṟiṉ tāymār, kuḻantikaḷik kuyavaṉiṭam koṇṭucella, avaṉ oru kucciyi, oruvakic cemmaṇ kuḻampil naṉittu, akkoppuḷaṅkaḷ uḷḷa pakutiyic cuṟṟi, yāḷi eṉappaṭum oru kaṟpaṉip pēyiṉ uruvatti variyac, cila nāṭkaḷukku kellām annōy maṟintu pōyviṭum.

uṭikaḷi veḷuttuk kañciyūṭṭal, pāṭaṟporuḷāka amivataṟkut takutiyaṟṟa ciṟu ceyalkaḷākap pulavark niṉikkavilli. "kūttu mutalām kaḷiyāṭṭaṅkaḷ orupāl nikaḻa, ōyātu viḻāk koṇṭāṭik koṇṭirukkum mūtūrkaḷil, āṭi kaḷip parutti āṭi, paṭṭāṭi eṉa iṉam pirittu aḻukkup pōkat tuvittaḷikkum toḻilik kiyōyvatillāmal ceyvatāl vaṟumi aṟiyātu vāḻum calavit toḻil makaḷ orutti, ciṉṉam ciṟu pūttoḻilkaḷ koṇṭa ciṟanta āṭikku, iravil cōṟṟukkañci ūṭṭum ciṟappu oru pāṭṭil kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu".

“āṭiyal viḻaviṉ aḻuṅkal mūtūr

uṭi ōr pāṉmiyil peruṅki tūvā

vaṟaṉ il pulatti ellit tōytta

pukāppukaḷ koṇṭa puṉpūṅ kaliṅkam”.

naṟṟiṇi : 90 : 1-4





pāṟiyil tiṉi ularttik kāttirukkum oru peṇṇip pāṭiyuḷḷār oru pulavar. muṉki niṟiya vaḷiyalkaḷiyum, piṟa uṭal uṟuppukaḷil avaṟṟiṟku uriya aṇikaḷiyum aṇintirukkum uyarkuṭi makaḷir, kariya periya mali mītu akaṉṟa pāṟiyil centiṉi parappik kāttu niṟkiṉṟaṉar. "niṟivaḷi muṉki, nēriḻi makaḷir

iruṅkal viyal aṟic centiṉi parappi"

- kuṟuntoki : 335: 1-2.





kāycciya pālil mōr iṭṭut tayir ākkuvatum ōr uvamiyākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu. "peṇmikkuriya maṭappam uṭiyaḷāya āymakaḷ, taṉki viral muṉiyāl toṭṭut teṟitta ciṟitaḷavē āṉa mōr, kuṭam niṟiya irunta pālaṉittiyum tayirākkum nikaḻcci, kaḷam pukunta oru peruvīraṉ akkaḷattil niṟintu niṟkum nāṟpaṭi aṉittukkum uyir pōkkum nōyāki niṟkum taṉmikku uvamiyākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu.

"maṭappāl āymakaḷ vaḷḷukirt teṟitta

kuṭappāl cilluṟi pōlap

paṭikku nōy ellām tāṉ āyiṉaṉē"

- puṟam : 276 :3-5





kaṭuku miḷaku mutalām tāḷippup poruṭkaḷik kalantu uṇaviṟku maṇam ūṭṭuvatum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. kuykkoḷ koḻuntuvi' (puṟam : 160 : 1) kuyyuṭi aṭicil" (puṟam: 127 : 7)

ciṟuvar ciṟumiyariṉ āṭal pāṭal pōlum iṉiya poḻutupōkkukaḷ paṟpala iṭaṅkaḷil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. taccaṉ ceytu koṭutta ciṟiya kutiri pūṭṭappaṭṭa ciṟiya tēril amarntu ceṉṟu iṉpuṟal iyalātu eṉiṉum, atik kayiṟu koṇṭu īrttuc ceṉṟu iṉpam aṭiyum ciṟuvar ciṟumiyariṉ iṉpa viḷiyāṭṭil iṉpam kaṇṭuḷḷār oru pulavar. taccaṉ ceyta ciṟumā viyam

ūrntu iṉpuṟāar āyiṉum, kiyiṉ

īrttu iṉpuṟūum iḷiyōr.'’

-kuṟuntoki : 1 :1 - 3





tām toṭutta malarmāliyi aṇintu koṇṭu, mūṅkil pōlum parutta tōḷ uṭiyēm eṉṟa tuṇivāl kaṭal nīril pukuntu āṭiya makaḷir, kaṭaṟkarik kāṉal cōli yil parantu kiṭakkum maṇalāl ciṟṟil kaṭṭi viḷiyāṭum iṉpak kāṭciyik kāṭṭiyuḷḷār oru pulavar.

"tuḷitta kōtip paṇipperum tōḷiṉar,

kaṭalāṭu makaḷir kāṉal iḻitta

ciṟumaṉi”.

- kuṟuntoki : 326 : 1-3





oḷivīcum vaḷikaḷi aṇinta makaḷir, vaṟupaṭāta pacci nelli, avalāka iṭittu muṭittu, atu ceyyat tuṇi purinta, kariya vayiram pāynta marattāl āṉa ulakkiyi muṟṟit tali cāyttu aḻakuṟak kāṭci aḷikkum neṟpayir niṟkum vayal varappil paṭukka vittu viṭṭu viḷiyāṭac ceṉṟuviṭum ciṟappik kūṟukiṟatu oru ceyyuḷ.

“pācaval iṭitta karuṅkāḻ ulakki

āykatir nelliṉ varampaṇit tuyiṟṟi

oṇtoṭi makaḷir vaṇṭal ayarum”.

- kuṟuntoki : 238 : 13





taṉpāl aṉpu kāṭṭuvatil oppuk kāṇamāṭṭāta taṉ tōḻiyar kūṭṭattoṭu aruviyil pāyntu āṭi- avvāṟu āṭuṅkāl, nīralikaḷ tākkiyatāl, taṉṉuṭiya periya, aḻakiya, aṉpoḻukum kaṇkaḷ civantu pōkak kāṭci aḷikkum celvattirumakaḷik kāṭṭukiṟatu oru ceyyuḷ. -

“poruvil āyamoṭu aruvi āṭi

nīr alic civanta pēramar maḻikkaṇ".

naṟṟiṇi : 44 : 1:2





ūñcal āṭṭam ellōrukkum terinta maṟṟoru viḷiyāṭṭu. aḻakiya kaṇkaḷi uṭiya tōḻiyar kūṭṭam āṭṭiviṭumāṟu, kariya paṉi nārit tirittu muṟukkiya kayiṟṟi nīḷamākat toṅkaviṭṭuk kaṭṭiya ūñcal oṉṟi āṭṭik kāṭṭukiṟatu ōr aḻakiya ceyyuḷ. "peruṅkayiṟu nālum irumpaṉam piṇiyal

pūṅkaṇ āyam ūkka"

- naṟṟiṇi : 90 : 6-7





paḷḷi ceṉṟu kalvi kallāta iḷañciṟuvarkaḷ, vēppa marattu aṭiyil, neruṅkat taḻitta ataṉ ilikaḷ tarum, puḷḷi puḷḷiyākat tōṉṟum niḻalil, poṟkollaṉ pāl uḷḷa poṉṉurikalpōlum vaṭivil vaṭṭamāṉa araṅki varintukoṇṭu, nellik kāykaḷi vaṭṭukkaḷākak koṇṭu, pāṇṭil āṭum aḻakum ōriṭattil kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu.

vēmpiṉ puḷḷi nīḻal

kaṭṭaḷi aṉṉa vaṭṭaraṅku iḻittuk

kallāc ciṟāar nellivaṭ ṭāṭum"

- naṟṟiṇi : 3 : 2 - 4





kūri vēynta nalla maṉikku uriyavarākiya kuṟuntoṭikaḷi aṇinta makaḷir, taṅkaḷ maṉiyiṉ muṉpuṟattil parappiyirukkum putumaṇalil amarntu, kaḻaṟcik kāykaḷik koṇṭu kaḻaṅkāṭik kaḷittiruppar.

"kūri naṉmaṉik kuṟuntoṭi makaḷir,

maṇalāṭu kaḻaṅku".

- naṟṟiṇi : 79 : 2-3





makaḷir viḷiyāṭṭākap pala iṭaṅkaḷilum kūṟap paṭṭiruppatu ōri. pūñcāyk kōri eṉum oruvakik kōrip pullāl ceytu makarantap poṭikaḷ pūcic ceyta pāvi vittu viḷiyāṭu āyamoṭu, ōri āṭātu". "oḷḷiḻi makaḷiroṭu ōriyum āṭāy” (naṟṟiṇi 68:1-3, 155:1) “ōri āyattu oṇṭoṭi makaḷir” (puṟam : 176 : 1)

makaḷiriṉ maṟṟoru viḷiyāṭṭu "alliyam.” āṇ kōlamum peṇ kōlamum uṭiyavāka iraṇṭu pāvikaḷic ceytu koṇṭu āṭum oruvaki viḷiyāṭṭu.

"vallōṉ tiiya vari vaṉappu uṟṟa

allip pāvi āṭuvaṉappu ēyppak

kāma iruvar”.

puṟam : 33 : 16 - 18





kuruṭṭu nampikkikaḷ



iṉi, makkaḷiṭiyē nilaviyirunta kuruṭṭu nampikkikaḷ cila. kākki karintāl, viruntu varum eṉṟa nampikki, iṉṟēpōl aṉṟum iruntatu. “kaṇavaṉ pirivāṟṟāt tuṉpak kavaliyāl melintuviṭṭa eṉ tōḻiyiṉ tōḷkaḷ, ammelivu nīṅkip paṇṭē pōl ākic ciṟappuṟuvataṟkut tuṇi ceyyum vakiyil, kaṇavaṉ varukiyik karintu muṉ aṟivittatu kākki; annallatu ceyta kākkikku iṭum paliyākat toṇṭi. nakarattu vayalkaḷil muṟṟa viḷinta veṇṇel arici koṇṭu cuṭucōṟṟit tiṇṇiya tērukku uriya naḷḷi eṉṉum koṭivaḷḷaliṉ kāṭṭil vāḻum āyarkaḷiṉ pala pacukkaḷiṭam koṇṭa neyyik kalantu, ēḻu kalattil iṭṭu vittālum, atu ceyta naṉṟiyi nōkka, ippali ciṟitām” eṉak kūṟukiṟāḷ taliviyiṉ tōḻi orutti.

"tiṇtēr naḷḷi kāṉattu aṇṭar

pallā payanta neyyil, toṇṭi

muḻutuṭaṉ viḷinta veṇṇel veñcōṟu

eḻukalattu ēntiṉum ciṟitu; eṉ tōḻi

peruntōḷ nekiḻtta cellaṟku

viruntuvarak karinta kākkiyatu paliyē'’

- kuṟuntoki : 270





cakuṉaṅkaḷilāṉa nampikki aḷavumīṟi iruntatu. paṟavikaḷiṉ pōkku, avaṟṟiṉ kural, vara irukkum ākkam, iḻappukkaḷi aṟivittuviṭavallaṉa eṉa uṟutiyāka nampiṉar. "puḷḷum, poḻutum paḻittal" (puṟam: 204 : 10). paricil kiṭikkātapōtu, atu tarātuviṭutta puravalaṉip paḻikkātu, tām puṟappaṭṭu vantapōtu nikaḻnta puḷnimittam kālakkēṭukaḷiyē paḻippar. pēykaḷ, maraṅkaḷilum iṭukāṭukaḷilum vāḻum eṉṟu nampiṉārkaḷ. pēy, taṉ kūṭṭattōṭu āṭikkaḷikkum iṭukāṭṭi oru pulavar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. (puṟam : 288: 45) pōrkkaḷattil vīrappuṇ peṟṟu iṟantu vīḻntu kiṭakkum vīrarkaḷiṉ vīrap puṇkaḷit tōṇṭi, atiliruntu perukiya ceṅkurutiyāl civantu pōṉa taṉ kikaḷāl taṉatu mayirik kōti muṭittu, ataṉāl kaṟutta taṉ mēṉiyum cenniṟaṅ kāṭṭum oru pēymakaḷik kāṭṭukiṟār oru pulavar.

"porutu āṇṭu oḻinta mintar puṇ toṭṭuk

kurutic ceṅkik kūntal tīṭṭi ,

niṟam kiḷar uruviṉ pēeyp peṇṭir".

puṟam : 62:2-4





pēymakaḷ piṇattit taḻuvi, ataṉ veḷḷiya niṇattit tiṉṉum; "pēey makaḷir piṇam taḻuuppaṟṟi, viḷar ūṉ tiṉṟa vempulāl" (puṟam : 359 : 4-5) pēy, makaḷirip paṟṟik koḷḷum; avvāṟu pēyāl paṟṟap paṭṭa makaḷir, kutittu āṭuvar. "muruku meyppaṭṭa pulitti pōlat tāvupu teṟikkum". (puṟam : 259:5-6) veṇkaṭukip pukittāl pēy ōṭi maṟintuviṭum. "aiyavi pukippavum" (puṟam : 98 : 15) eṉṟellām makkaḷ nampi iruntaṉar.

ōṭukiṉṟa āṟṟiṉ iṭiyē uḷḷa, turutti eṉṟum aḻikkap paṭum mēṭṭil, palvēṟu icikkaruvikaḷ ici eḻuppa, āṭṭiṉ kaḻutti aṟuttut, tiṉiyip pirappaṅ kūṭiyil parappi vittu tām veḷippaṭavantu ulāval allatu, nōykaḷit tīrkkalākāt teyvaṅkaḷ orucēra vaḻipaṭṭu ivaḷ pēyāl paṟṟappaṭṭāḷ eṉṟa muṭiviṟku * vantu niṟkum makaḷirik kāṭṭukiṟār oru pulavar.

“maṟikkural aṟuttut tiṉippirappu iric,

cellāṟṟuk kavalip pal iyam kaṟaṅkat

tōṟṟam allatu nōykku marun tāka.

vēṟṟup perum teyvam palavuṭaṉ vāḻttip

pōyk koḷīiyaḷ ivaḷ eṉa'’

kuṟuntoki : 263 : 1-5





maṉita iṟicci, kurutikaḷi virumpuvatu pēy. ataṉāl, kaḷattil iṟavātu vīḻntu kiṭakkum vīrarkaḷiṉ puṇṇit tōṇṭat talippaṭṭuviṭum. pēy toṭṭa puṇ, āṟuvatum illi . avvīraṉ uyir piḻippatum ilaṉ, itil nampikki uṭimiyāl avaṉ maṉivi, pēy, taṉ kaṇavaṉ irukkum iṭattiyum aṇṭak kūṭātu eṉpatāl, vīṭṭu vāyilil iranti, vēppantaḻikaḷic ceruki, yāḻ mutalām icikaḷi eḻuppi, mella eḻuntu vīṭṭi miyiṭṭu meḻuki, veṇ ciṟukaṭuki eṅkum tūvi, āmpal kuḻal ūti, maṇi aṭittu, kāñcippaṇ pāṭi. māḷiki muṟṟilum naṟumaṇap puki eḻuppik kāppaṉ.

“tīṅkaṉi iravamoṭu vēmpumaṉic cerīi

vāṅkumaruppu yāḻoṭu palliyam kaṟaṅkak,

ki payappeyarttu, mi iḻutu iḻuki,

aiyavi citaṟi, āmpal ūti,

icimaṇi eṟintu, kāñci pāṭi,

neṭunakar varippil kaṭinṟi puki ik

kākkam vammō ?” -

-puṟam : 287 : 1-7





camaya vaḻipāṭukaḷ

toṉmiyāṉa vaḻipāṭṭu muṟikaḷ, aintāvatu atikārattil kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āriyattukku muntiya tamiḻar nākarīkam (Pre Aryan Tamil Culture) eṉṟa eṉṉuṭiya nūlilum viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. āṅkē eṭuttukkāṭṭiya kaṭavuḷkaḷē allāmal, vēṟu kaṭavuḷkaḷiyum tamiḻar vaḻipaṭṭaṉar. tiṅkaḷ, avaṟṟiṉ oṉṟu. kaṭal naṭuvē, mīṉ piṭi paṭakukaḷām timilkaḷil ēṟṟappaṭṭuk kāṭci aḷikkum viḷakkukaḷ pōla, cemmīṉkaḷ oḷiviṭum vicumpiṉ uccikkaṇ, muḻumati nāḷaṉṟu, am muḻumatiyik kaṇṭu, kāṭṭuvāḻ mayil pōṉṟa, cila vaḷiyalkaḷ aṇinta vaḷāy eṉ viṟaliyum nāṉum ammuḻumati, vaḷavaṉ veṇ koṟṟakkuṭi pōṉṟuḷatu eṉa eṇṇiyavāṟē toḻutōm allavō? eṉṟa pulavar viṉāvil, mati vaḻipāṭu kuṟippiṭappaṭṭuḷatu.

"munnīr nāppaṇ timilcuṭar pōlac

cemmīṉ imikkum māka vicumpiṉ

ucci niṉṟa uvavumati kaṇṭu

kaṭci maññiyiṉ curamutal cērnta

cilvaḷi viṟaliyum yāṉum valvirintu

toḻutaṉam allamō? ...........

vaḷavaṉ ..........

māli veṇkuṭi okkumāl eṉavē".

puṟam : 60





("māka vicumpu" eṉum ittoṭar, puṟam : 35, 60, 270, 400, akam : 253, maturikkāñci : 454; paripāṭal 47 ākiya pala iṭaṅkaḷil vantiruntālum, uriyāciriyarkaḷ ataṟkāṉa teḷivāṉa poruḷi uṇarttiṉārallar).

kākkikku uṇavu aḷittal, ovvoru vīṭṭilum, nāḷtōṟum tavaṟāmal naṭipeṟum oru camayac caṭaṅku. "katirkaḷ eṟittalāl naṭappār kālkaḷi veppam. paṇṇumāṟu ñāyiṟu kāyum pakaṟpoḻutil, nakaril uḷḷa periya māḷikikaḷukku varum viruntiṉarkaḷi varavēṟṟu uṇavaḷittu ōmpuvāṉ vēṇṭi, poṟṟoṭi aṇinta ammāḷiki vāḻ makaḷir, viruntiṉarkku uṇavu paṭippataṉ muṉṉar, māḷikiyiṉ muṟṟattil paliyākap pōṭṭa, kokku ukir pōlum cōṟṟit tiṉṟa kākkik kūṭṭam, poḻutu maṟiya, mīṉ aṅkāṭi pukuntu, āṅku niḻalil kuvittu vittirukkum pacci mīṉkaḷik kavarntu koṇṭu kaṭaṟkariyil viṉi oḻintu āṭik koṇṭirukkum kappaliṉ pāymarattil ceṉṟu taṅkum. itip paṭam piṭittuk kāṭṭukiṟār oru pulavar.

"katirkāl vempak, kalkāy ñāyiṟṟut

tiruvuṭi viṉ nakar varuviruntu ayarmār

poṟṟoṭi makaḷir puṟaṅkaṭi ukutta

kokku utir nimiral mānti, eṟpaṭa

akal aṅkāṭi aciniḻal kuvitta

pacciṟāk kavarnta pacuṅkaṇ kākki

tūṅkal vaṅkattuk kūmpil cēkkum”.

- naṟṟiṇi : 258 : 3-9





paḻaṅkālattil, periya teyva vaḻipāṭukaḷ, palikoṭuttal, teyvattaṉmi vāynta āṭal, pāṭalkaḷi oruṅkē koṇṭirukkum attaku oru vaḻipāṭu kīḻvarumāṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu. “ō eṉum pēroli eḻa, veḷḷi veḷēreṉa oḷi viḷaṅka ōṭivarum aruvikaḷāl viḷakkam peṟṟa maliccāralil, vēṅkiyiṉ tēṉ kamaḻum ‘ malarkaḷic cūṭikkoṇṭu, toṇṭakappaṟi eḻuppum oliyaṉ tāḷattiṟku ēṟpa, āṭavarum peṇṭirum terukkaḷil kalantu āṭi makiḻum ciṟuciṟu kuṭikaḷik koṇṭa pākkattil murukaṉ ulāvarutalik kūṟukiṟatu oru ceyyuḷ”.

kaṟaṅkuveḷ ḷaruvi piṟaṅkumalik kavāaṉ,

tēm kamaḻ iṇara vēṅki cūṭit

toṇṭakap paṟiccīr peṇṭiroṭu virii

maṟukil tūṅkum ciṟukuṭip pākkattu iyal muruku”.

- akam : 118 : 1-5





kuṉṟukaḷ vēlipōl nāṟpuṟamum niṟka, iṭiyē uḷḷa ciṟṟūril, maṉṟattil niṟkum vēṅki maraṅkaḷ, maṉa nāḷākiya nalla nāḷil pūkkat toṭaṅka, avaṟṟiṉ maṇi maṇiyāṉa arumpukaḷ malarnta, poṉ pōṉṟa malarkaḷ utirntu, akaṉṟa pāṟikaḷi aḻaku ceyyum muṟṟaṅkaḷil, kuṟavarkaḷ, taṅkaḷ maṉikaḷil, kuravi āṭṭattil kitērnta mutiya makaḷiroṭu kikōttu, ārāvāram eḻak , kuravi āṭi viḻā eṭuppati viḷakkukiṟatu oru ceyyuḷ :

kuṉṟa vēlic ciṟukuṭi āṅkaṇ

maṉṟa vēṅki maṇanāḷ pūtta

maṇiyēr arumpiṉ poṉvī tāay

viyalaṟi virikkum muṉṟil, kuṟavar

maṉimutir makaḷiroṭu kuravi tūṅkum

ārkali viḻavu"

akam : 232 : 6:11.





"kaḻ nīril mīṉ ōṭum, mēl nīril kaṇpōlum karuṅkuvaḷi malarum; uppaṅkaḻi nīrāl cūḻappeṟṟa payir viḷintu niṟkum kaḻaṉikaḷil, avaṟṟik kāppavar paṟi aṭittu eḻuppum arittu eḻum ōci kēṭṭup paṟavikaḷ ōṭiviṭum. arumpukaḷ mitakkaviṭṭa kaḷḷiyum, iṉiya tēṟaliyum naṟaviyum kuṭittu makiḻnta kōcar kuravi āṭi makiḻvatik kūṟukiṟatu, oru ceyyuḷ.

"kīḻnīrāṉ mīṉvaḻaṅ kuntu;

mīnīrāṉ kaṇṇaṉṉa malar pūkkuntu;

kaḻi cuṟṟiya viḷikaḻaṉi

arippaṟiyāl puḷ oppantu;

neruṭunīr kūum maṇal taṇkāl

meṉ paṟiyāl puḷ iriyuntu;

naṉikkaḷḷiṉ. maṉikkōcar,

tīntēṟal naṟavu makiḻntu

tīṅkuravik koḷi tāṅkuntu"

- puṟam : 396:1-9





kōcar ciṟanta vīrar; ākavē avarkaḷ. āṭiya ik kuravik kūttu oruvakip pōrkkūttu ākum.

itaṉiṉum koṭumi vāynta oru pōrkkūttu, piṉvarumāṟu viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. “ceṅkarumpu kaḻikaḷ mītu kāṟṟil acintāṭum cenneṟkatirkaḷi vēyntu kaṭṭiya pantal, viḻā eṭukkum iṭam pōlap paṟpala aḻakuṭiyatākat tōṉṟa, ōyātu nel kuṟṟum ulakki oliyōṭu, palvēṟu olikaḷ olikkum āṅku, poṉṉāl ceyta tumpip pūvām pōrp pūvuṭaṉ paciya paṉam tōṭṭiyum aṇintu koṇṭu, kaṭuñciṉam mikka vīrarkaḷ kaṭal olipōl, āravārap pēroli eḻuppiyavāṟē, kuravik kūtti, veṟi koṇṭu āṭuvar”.

“alaṅku cennel katir vēynta

āykarumpiṉ koṭik kūri

cāṟu koṇṭa kaḷam pōla

vēṟu vēṟu polivu tōṉṟak,

kuṟṟu āṉā ulakkiyāl,

kaliccummi viyal āṅkaṇ

polantoṭṭup pintumpi

mici alaṅkuḷiya paṉippōḻ ceric

ciṉamāntar veṟikkuravi

ōtanīriṟ peyarpu poṅka”

puṟam : 22 : 14-23





ki.pi. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭil āriya nākarikam, tamiḻar uḷḷattuḷ , veḷḷam pōl perukkeṭuttup pāyntu avartam vāḻkkiyiṉ ovvoru niliyiyum māṟṟiviṭṭatu. maṟṟa ceyalpāṭukaḷōṭu, āṭal pāṭalkaḷum pātippukku uḷḷākiviṭṭaṉa, paḻantamiḻariṉ iyaṟki yōṭiyinta eḷiya paṇṇici, āriyariṉ kaṟpaṉittiṟam vāynta palvēṟu paṭikaḷi uṭiyavāya mellicikaḷukku iṭam koṭuttuviṭṭatu. avarkaḷiṉ paḻiya āṭalkaḷiṉ iṭatti, āriyariṉ, eḷitil viḷaṅkalākāp palvēṟu vakiyākap pirivuṇṭa āṭalkaḷ paṟṟikkoṇṭaṉa. paḻiya āṭal, oṉṟu, coṟkaḷiṉ tuṇi vēṇṭāmal, meyp pāṭṭiṉ mūlamākavē, kātal, vīra uṇarvukaḷi veḷippaṭuttum; allatu muḻukka muḻukka vaḻipāṭu kuṟitta āṭalākum. āriya nākarīkattiṉ iṭi nuḻivāl, akkaliyil, palvēṟu nilikaḷ vaḷarntu viṭṭaṉa. āṭal pāṭal kuṟitta ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷum eḻutappaṭalāyiṉa. āṉāl, annūlkaḷ, ippōtu aḻintuviṭṭaṉa. āṉāl akkalikaḷik kulat toḻilākak koṇṭa kaliñarkaḷāl, avi payilap peṟuvatu kuṟitta kuṟippukaḷ palavaṟṟic cilappatikāram tarukiṟatu. ataṉ uriyāciriyarkaḷ, avi kuṟitta virivāṉa viḷakkaṅkaḷi aḷittuḷḷaṉar. paṇṭu āṭal nikaḻnta iṭam ūr maṉṟam. āṉāl, atu cilappatikāram mūṉṟām atikārattil kūṟappaṭṭiruppatu pōlum palnili āṭaraṅkāka urumāṟi viṭṭatu. āṉāl, immāṟṟamellām, perunakaraṅkaḷil iṭam peṟṟuḷḷaṉavē allatu ciṟṟūrkaḷil aṉṟu. nakaraṅkaḷ

urōmāṉiya nāṭṭuṭaṉ naṭattiya vāṇikam perumaḷa vil. vaḷarntuviṭṭa piṉṉar, tamiḻakattil nakaraṅkaḷ vaḷamuṭiyavākavum, celvac ceḻippu ṭiyavākavum uyarntuviṭṭaṉa. akanāṉūṟṟup pāṭal oṉṟil, cēra, cōḻa, pāṇṭiyarkaḷiṉ talinakaraṅkaḷ curukkamāka . viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.

“uṟaviṉarkku uḷavām kēṭṭiṉik kaḷintu, avarit tāṅkik koḷḷavum, uṟaviṉarāy uḷḷār, kuṟiviṉṟi uṇṇavum, ayalār, anta ayalārām nili keṭṭuk keḻutaki uṟaviṉarāka māṟi oḻukavum virumpi, ataṟkut tuṇi ceyyum poruḷ īṭṭum muyaṟciyil ūkkam mikak koṇṭu, atil viruppamum mikuntu, āttimāli aṇipavarum, etirttāri aḻikkavalla pōrmuṟikaḷi aṟintavarum ākiya cōḻarkku uriyatāṉa, aṟaṅkūr avi irukkum ciṟappiṉik koṇṭa uṟiyūri otta peṟutaṟkariya pēraṇikalaṉkaḷi aṭintu nallōr yāvarālum virumpattakkatāṉa ceyaṟkariya ceyalām poruḷ īṭṭum paṇiyi iṉitē muṭittu viṭṭōm. ākavē, pakivar araṇkaḷ palavaṟṟi veṟṟi koṇṭa, pakip paṭikaḷōṭu eppōtum māṟupāṭu koḷḷum nāṟpaṭiyiṉi uṭiya , eṉṟum pukaḻ vāṭāta vēppa māli taritta pāṇṭiyaṉukku uriya kūṭalmā nakariṉ, kālik kaṭivītiyiṉ palvēṟu maṇaṅkaḷum orucēra maṇakkum nalla neṟṟiyiṉiyum, nīṇṭa kariya kūntaliṉiyum, māmi niṟattu mēṉiyiṉiyum uṭiya nam kātaliyuṭaṉ, maliyik kuṭintu iyaṟṟiyatu pōlum, vāṉi aḷāviya nīṇṭa māḷikiyil, kaṭal nuriyi aṭittu vittatu pōṉṟa melliya malarkaḷāl āṉa paṭukki viritta, ōṅkiya kaṭṭilil, uyarnta viḷakkiṉ neṭuñcuṭar oḷiyil āṭavarkku uriya nalamelām uṭiya nam mārpil, nam kātaliyiṉ mārpakattu aṇikaḷ vaṭukkaḷic ceyyumāṟu, varivariyāṉa kōṭukaḷ niṟinta neṟṟiyiṉiyum valimikutaliṉāl muḻaṅkum, vāyvari vantu oḻukum mata nīriṉiyum, kūṟṟuvaṉukku nikarāṉa āṟṟalum nilatti aṟintu, pakivari aṇukac ceṉṟu kolvatil tappāta, niṉittālē naṭuṅkappaṇṇum, toṅkum kiyiṉiyum uṭiya koṭiya periya yāṉip paṭiyiṉiyum, neṭiya tērppaṭiyiṉiyum uṭiya cēraṉukku uriya , celvattāl ciṟanta mikap periya nakaramākiya karuvūriṉ tuṟiyil, teḷinta nīr ōṭum kuḷirnta āṉ poruni āṟu, uyarnta kariyil kuvittuḷḷa maṇaliṉ eṇṇikkiyilum, palavāka, nām taḻuvi iṉpuṟuvōm; neñcē! varuvāyāka!

"kēḷkēṭu ūṉṟavum, kiḷiñar āravum,

kēḷal kēḷir keḻīiyiṉar oḻukavum,

āḷviṉikku etiriya ūkkamoṭu, pukal ciṟantu,

āram kaṇṇi, aṭupōrc cōḻar,

aṟamkeḻu nallavi uṟanti aṉṉa,

peṟalarum ceyviṉi muṟṟiṉam, āyiṉ;

araṇpal paṭarnta muraṇkoḷ tāṉi

vāṭā vēmpiṉ vaḻuti kūṭal

nāḷaṅ kāṭi nāṟum naṟunutal,

nīḷ irum kūntal, māa yōḷoṭu,

varikuyiṉ ṟaṉṉa vāṉtōy neṭunakar,

nurimukan taṉṉa meṉpūñ cēkki,

nivanta paḷḷi, neṭum cuṭar viḷakkattu

nilam kēḻ ākam, pūṇṭup poṟippa,

muyaṅkuvam; ceṉmō ; neñcē ; varinutal

vayam tikaḻpu imiḻtarum vāypuku kaṭāttu

mīḷi moympoṭu nilaṉeṟiyāk kuṟuki

āḷkōḷ piḻiyā añcuvaru taṭakkik

kaṭumpakaṭṭu yāṉi, neṭuntērk kōti

tirumā viyaṉnakark karuvūr muṉ tuṟit

teṇṇīr uyarkarik kaviiya

taṇāṉ poruni maṇaliṉum palavē".

-akam : 93





aracarkaḷ vāḻntatāl nakaraṅkaḷ, mukkiyattuvam peṟṟuviṭṭaṉa. nakaraṅkaḷ perukavē, celvamum, aracar kaḷiṉ mukkiyattuvamum uyarntuviṭṭaṉa. tamiḻakattu mūvēntarkaḷum, paṇṭikkālattil, vēliyaṟṟuk kiṭakkum nāṭkaḷil poḻutupōkkiṟkākavum, taṅkaḷ āṇmiyi nilināṭṭik koḷvataṟkākavum ēṉiya aracukaḷi aṭimi koṇṭa pēraracāka uyarvataṟkākavum oruvarōṭoruvar pōr toṭuttuvantaṉar. ākavē, ikkālap pāṭalkaḷil, aracarkaḷiṉ koṭi vaḷap pārāṭṭu iṉṉamum iṭam peṟṟuḷatu eṉṟālum, avi, aracarkaḷiṉ, pōrkkaḷ veṟṟikaḷip pārāṭṭalāyiṉa. "pakivari veṉṟu avartam paṭṭattu yāṉiyiṉ poṉṉālāṉa neṟṟip paṭṭattik kikkoṇṭu, atu aḻittup paṇṇiya poṟṟāmari malarip pāṭip pukaḻum pāṇaṉ taliyil aḻaku peṟac cūṭṭiya ciṟanta talimi yiṉiyum, pōril puṟam kāṭṭāk kōṭpāṭṭiṉiyum uṭiya periyōṉ vaḻiyil vantavaṉē! irāppoḻutu iṅkēyē uṟaṅkiviṭṭatō , eṉa aiyuṟutaṟku ēṟpap - pēriruḷ cūḻnta aṭarnta ciṟu kāṭṭiyum, paṟi oli pōl olikkum maliyaruvi kaḷiyum uṭiya muḷḷūr malikku uriya vēntē! muḷ pōlum kūriya paṟkaḷiyuṭiya pāmpiyum naṭuṅkap paṇṇum iṭiyēṟupōl pōr muracu muḻaṅka, talimi tāṅkivanta yāṉiyum, ataṉ mītu amarntu pōriṭṭa aracum kaḷattil oruṅkē iṟantupaṭa, aḻittaṟkariya avar nāṟpaṭikaḷic citaṟa aḻittu, ciṟitum poruntāta paki, taṉ nāṭṭi aṇukavum viṭātu, taṭuttu niṟuttum elliyākat tikaḻum peṇṇiyāṟu pāyum nāṭṭiṟku uriyavaṉē : uṉ pukaḻip pāṭavallamāyiṉum, vallōmal lēmāyiṉam, uṉpāl vantu uṉpukaḻ pāṭuvōmāyiṉ, aḻikkalākāt taṉmi yarākiya uṉ kiḷiyoṭu nīyum peruvāḻvu vāḻa, innilamici uḷḷār ellāriṉum aṟivu oḻukkaṅkaḷil mācaṟṟa antaṇaṉākiya kapilaṉ, poruḷ vēṇṭi irantu cellum pulavarkaḷukku iṉip pukaḻvataṟku iṭamilli eṉak kūṟumaḷavu uṉ pukaḻum, avaṉ pukaḻum peruki niṟkappāṭiviṭṭāṉ. ataṉāl, ciṉam mikka cēṉikku uriyavaṉākiya cēraṉukkuriya mēlikkaṭalil, piṟanāṭṭup poṟkācukaḷic cumantu varum kaṭalōṭavalla periya nāvāykaḷ ōṭiya iṭattil, vēṟu ciṉṉañ ciṟu marak kalaṅkaḷ ōṭamāṭṭātatu pōlum niliyiṉarāki viṭṭōmāyiṉum, emmuṭiya vaṟumi turatta, uṉ perumpukaḻ īrttuk koṇṭuvara vantu, uṉ vaḷamik kuṇaṅkaḷil, cilavaṟṟi nāṅkaḷum pāṭip pārāṭṭiṉam".

"oṉṉār. yāṉi ōṭippoṉ koṇṭu,

pāṇar ceṉṉi poliyat tii,

vāṭāt tāmari cūṭṭiya, viḻuccar,

ōṭāp pūṭki uravōṉ maruka!

vallēm allēm āyiṉum, vallē,

niṉvayiṉ kiḷakkuva māyiṉ, kaṅkal

tuyil maṭin taṉṉa tūṅkuiruḷ iṟumpiṉ,

paṟi ici aruvi, muḷḷūrp poruna!

teṟalarum marapiṉ niṉ kiḷiyoṭum poliya,

nilamicip paranta makkaṭ kellām

puvaṉ aḻuk kaṟṟ anta ṇāḷaṉ,

irantu cel mākkaṭku iṉi iṭaṉiṉṟip,

parantici niṟkap pāṭiṉaṉ; ataṟkoṇṭu,

ciṉamiku tāṉi vāṉavaṉ kuṭakaṭal

polantaru nāvāy ōṭṭiya avvaḻip

piṟakalam celkalātu; aṉiyēm. atti;

iṉmi tūrappa, icitara vantu niṉ

vaṇmiyil toṭuttaṉam yāmē, muḷ eyiṟṟu

aravu eṟi urumiṉ muracu eḻuntu iyampa,

aṇṇal yāṉiyoṭu ventukaḷattu oḻiya

arum camam tatiyat tākki, naṉṟum

oṉṉāt tevvart tāṅkum

peṇṇiyam paṭappi nāṭu kiḻa vōyē".

puṟam. 126





pōr muracukku, allatu, kūṟuvatāyiṉ, appōr muraciṉ uṟiyum teyvattiṉ āvikkuk, kurutippali koṭuppatē pōrākak karutappaṭṭatu. pōr muracu, ataṟkeṉa amikkappaṭum taṉi irukkiyil vikkappaṭum. oru ceyyuḷ ivvāṟu kūṟukiṟatu. "muracukkuk kuṟṟamām eṉak kūṟappaṭum kuṟṟam etuvum illātavāṟu valittup piṇitta vāri uṭiya, karumarattāl ceyyappaṭṭami yāl, karumi niṟam kāṭṭum pakkam aḻaku peṟa, mayiliṉ taḻittu nīṇṭa tōki, oḷḷiya puḷḷikaḷ niṟinta nīlamaṇipōlum niṟam vāynta māli, ākiyavaṟṟip poṉ pōlum taḷirkaḷiyuṭiya uḻiñiyoṭu aḻakuṟa aṇiyappeṟṟa, kurutippali koḷḷum vēṭkiyuṭiyatāṉa pakivar uḷam naṭuṅka muḻaṅka vallatāṉa pōr muracam, nīrāṭi varuvataṉ muṉṉar eṇṇey nuriyi mukantu vittāṟpōl, melliya malarkaḷ kuvittu vikkappaṭṭa kaṭṭil oḻuṅku ceyyappaṭum”. "mācaṟa vicitta vārpuṟu vaḷpiṉ,

mipaṭu maruṅkul poliya, maññi

olineṭum pīli, oṇpoṟi maṇittār,

polaṅkuḻi uḻiñiyoṭu poliyac cūṭṭik,

kuruti vēṭki , urukeḻu muracam

maṇṇi vārā aḷavi, eṇṇey

nurimukan taṉṉa meṉpūñ cēkki”.

-puṟam : 50 7-7





oppiṭṭu nōkka, koṭumimikā, vēta vaḻi vēḷvi muṟikaḷ iṭam peṟat toṭaṅkiviṭṭa innūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyilum, pāṇṭiyap peruvīraṉ oruvaṉ kuṟittup piṉvarum pōrpparaṇip pāṭalip pāṭa, ru pulavarit tūṇṭumaḷavu, paḻiya veṟṟippalikaḷ pāl kaḷavēḷvikaḷ pāl koṇṭa peru vēṭki, tamiḻ aracar uḷḷattil, iṉṉamum, attuṇi āḻamāka vērūṉṟi iruntatu. “mikka āḻam uṭiya periya kaṭalil, kāṟṟāl tākkappaṭṭa marakkalam kaṭal nīrik kiḻittukkoṇṭu ōṭuvatupōlap, pōrkkaḷiṟu kaḷam pukuntu pakivar paṭiyit tākkip pāḻaṭittuk kaḷattil iṭam paṇṇiviṭa, avvāṟu iṭam kaṇṭa kaḷattil naṉku tīṭṭappeṟṟu oḷivīcum ili pōlum muṉiyiyuṭiya vēloṭu pukuntu, araciyum aḻittup pakivar paṭiyiyum pāḻceytu, avveṟṟip pukaḻ nāṭeṅkum parava, pakivar pōr muracik kurutip puṉali uli nīrākak koṇṭu, uliyil, taci, mūḷi mutalāyiṉa peytu, vīravaḷi aṇinta tōḷākiya tuṭuppuk koṇṭu, tuḻāvi ākkiya uṇavip pēykaḷukkup paṭittu, akkaḷattil kaḷa vēḷvi ceyta, kollum pōrvalla ceḻiya".

"naḷikaṭal irum kuṭṭattu

vaḷi puṭitta kalam pōlak

kaḷiṟu ceṉṟu kaḷaṉ akaṟṟavum,

kaḷaṉ akaṟṟiya viyalāṅkaṇ,

oḷiṟu iliya eḵku ēnti

aricupṭa amar uḻakki,

uricela, muracu veḷvi,

muṭittali aṭuppākap

puṉal kuruti ulik koḷīit,

toṭit tōḷ tuṭuppiṉ tuḻanta valciyiṉ

aṭukaḷam vēṭṭa arupōrc ceḻiya!"

puṟam : 26:7-17





nakaraṅkaḷil parattamiai

nēriṭi nili, uruvaka nili ākiya enniliyil poruḷ koṇṭālum, nakara vāḻkki eṉṟāl, nākarīka vaḷarcci eṉṟē poruḷ : nākarīkattiṉ teḷivāṉa muttiri, virumpat takātatu eṉak karutappaṭātāyiṉ, parattimi oḻukkam iṭampeṟuvatēyām. parattiyar āṭal pāṭalkaḷil naṉku payiṟci peṟṟiruppar, kaṇkavar vakiyil uṭiyaṇintiruppar. aḻakuṟa oppaṉi ceytu koḷvar. "viṟalpaṭa āṭavallāḷākiya viṟali, māṇikka : maṇikkōvi eṭṭāl āṉa mēkali aṇiyāl aḻaku peṟṟa alkulum, maṭappam poruntiya mi uṇṭa kaṇkaḷum, oḷivīcum neṟṟiyum koṇṭu", tikaḻtalik kūṟukiṟatu oru puṟanāṉūṟṟuc ceyyuḷ.

"iḻi aṇip polinta ēntu kōṭṭu alkul

maṭavaral uṇkaṇ, vāḷ nutal viṟali”

-puṟam : 89 : 1-2





eḷitil vayappaṭakkūṭiya āṭavarit taṅkaḷōṭu, āṟṟuttuṟiyilum, kuḷap paṭittuṟiyilum nīrāṭi makiḻumāṟu tūṇṭa vallavar parattiyar. āṭavaṉ oruvaṉit taṉ aḻakāl mayakkavalla uḷuram paṭitta oru paratti, avaṉ maṉivikkuk kīḻkkāṇum iṟumānta coṟkaḷik kūṟi aṉuppiṉāḷ. ‘'eṉ kūntalil āmpaliṉ muḻuppūvi aṇintu aḻaku ceytu koṇṭu, putu veḷḷam puraṇṭōṭum periya nīrttuṟiyil puṉal viḷiyāṭṭi virumpic celkiṉṟēṉ. avaḷ kaṇavaṉum, aṅkut tavaṟātu vantu, puṉal viḷiyāṭṭil eṉṉōṭu paṅku koṇṭu viṭuvāṉō eṉa avaḷ añcuvaḷāyiṉ, koṭiya pōr nikaḻumpōtu, pōr aṟam piṟaḻātu pōriṭṭup pakivari aḻikkavalla eḻiṉi eṉpāṉatu periya vēṟpaṭi pōrkkaḷattiṭattē uḷḷa avaṉuṭiya periya āṉiriyip, pakivar koḷḷāvāṟu cūḻa niṉṟu kāppatu pōla, taṉ kaṇavaṉ mārpu, eṉṉi aṇukaviṭāvāṟu, taṉ tōḻiyar kūṭṭattōṭu avaṉik kāttuk koḷvāḷāka’

"kūntal āmpal muḻuneṟi aṭiccip

perumpuṉal vanta iruntuṟi virumpi

yām aḵtu ayarkam cēṟum; tāṉ aḵtu

añcuva tuṭiya ḷāyiṉ, vempōr

nukampaṭak kaṭakkum palvēl eḻiṉi

muṉi āṉ peruniri pōlak

kiḷiyoṭum kākka, taṉ koḻunaṉ mārpē'’

kuṟuntoki : 80





viḻā nikaḻum iṭaṅkaḷil āṭavarkkuk kāma vēṭki ūṭṭik kavarntu koḷvāṉ vēṇṭip parattiyar peruṅkūṭṭamāka vantiruppar. vāḷi mīṉkaḷ, nīril, piṟaḻntu koṇṭiruppatik kaṇṭum, avaṟṟik kavarntu koḷḷak karutātu, akkuḷattil vāḻum nīrnāy, nāḷtōṟum tuyil mēṟkoṇṭuviṭum iṭamākiya, vāri vaḻaṅkum kivaṇmiyuṭiya kiḷḷivaḷavaṉukku uriya , kōyil veṇṇiyic cūḻa uḷḷa vayalkaḷ viḷintu kiṭakkum, nalla niṟam vāynta, muṟṟiya taḻikaḷāl āṉa āṭiyi, metteṉṟa akaṉṟa alkul mēl, aḻaku uṟa uṭuttukkoṇṭu, nāṉum iṅku naṭipeṟum viḻāviṟkuc cella vēṇṭum. putupputu varuvāykaḷi uṭiya ūraṉ, eṉṉi aṅku, akkōlattil kāṇpaṉāyiṉ, eṉṉi varintu koḷḷātu viṭuttuppōvatu aritiṉum aritākum". "vāḷi, vāḷiṟ piṟaḻa, nāḷum

poyki nīrnāy vikutuyil ēṟkum,

kivaṇ kiḷḷi, veṇṇi cūḻnta

vayalveḷ ḷāmpal uruva neṟittaḻi

aitu akal alkul aṇipeṟat tii,

viḻaviṟ celīiyal vēṇṭum, maṉṉō !

yāṇar ūraṉ kāṇunaṉ āyiṉ

variyā miyō aritē".

naṟṟiṇi- 390 : 1-8





paratti, āṭavar tēṭum veṟiyōṭu terukkaḷil varumpōtu, maṉiviyar, tattam kaṇavari viḻippōṭu kāttuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum. taliviyi, avaḷ tōḻi ivvāṟu eccarikkiṟāḷ. "kaḷḷamilā nōkkaminta aḻakiya kaṇkaḷ, mayirccāntaṇintu maṇam ūṭṭappeṟṟa kūntal, parutta tōḷkaḷ, oḻuṅkuṟa vaḷarnta veṇpaṟkaḷ, tiraṇṭu neruṅkiya tuṭikaḷ ākiya ittaku ciṟappukaḷāl oppuyavarvaṟṟa pēraḻakiyākiya paratti aḻakiya taḻiyāṭi uṭuttum viḻā nikaḻ kaḷam polivu peṟa vantu niṉṟa viṭṭāḷ. avaḷ pārviyiṉiṉṟum nam kaṇavarik kākka, tōḻimīr; eḻumiṉō eḻumiṉ!"

"maṭakkaṇ, takarak kūntal, paṇittōḷ,

vārnta vāl eyiṟṟuc, cērntu ceṟi kuṟaṅkiṉ

piṇiyal antaḻit tiit , tuṇi ilaḷ,

viḻavukkaḷam poliya vantuniṉ ṟaṉaḷē

eḻumiṉō eḻumiṉem koḻunark kākkum"

- naṟṟiṇi : 170:1-5





āṭavari aṭimi koḷḷum parattiyariṉ muyaṟci paṟṟiya maṟṟoru kāṭci itō; ‘'ūril viḻāvum muṭintu viṭṭatu, muḻaviṉ muḻakkamum aṭaṅki viṭṭatu; inniyiail ivaḷ yātu karutiṉaḷō eṉṟu kēṭpāyē yāyiṉ, kūṟukiṟēṉ kēḷ. oru nāḷ taḻi āṭi uṭuttu, attaḻi āṭi aciyum alkuli uṭiyavaḷāyt teruviṉkaṇ ipparatti ceṉṟāḷ. avvaḷavē, paḻiya veṟṟikaḷ pala koṇṭa maliyamāṉ tirumuṭikkāri, avvōrikku uriya nakariṉ oppaṟṟa periya teruvil veṟṟikkaḷippōṭu pukuntāṉāka , atukaṇṭa ōriyiṉ makkaḷ oṉṟutiraṇṭu āravārap pēroli eḻuntuviṭṭatu. atu kēṭṭatum, ārāyntu koṇṭa, ciṟanta vaḷiyalkaḷi aṇinta aḻakiya mēṉiyi uṭiya ivvūr makaḷirellām, taṅkaḷ kaṇavaṉmārkaḷi, avaḷ kaṇṇil paṭāvāṟu kāttuk koṇṭaṉar. ataṉāl ivarkaḷum naṉmi aṭintārkaḷ. naṭantatu itutāṉ”,

“viḻavum uḻantaṉṟu muḻavum tūṅkiṉṟu;

evaṉkuṟit taṉaḷ kol eṉṟi yāyiṉ,

taḻi aṇintu alamarum alkul teruviṉ

iḷiyōḷ iṟanta aṉittiṟkup paḻaviṟal

ōrik koṉṟa oru perum teruvil

kāri pukkanērār pulampōl,

kalleṉ ṟaṉṟāl ūrē, ataṟkoṇṭu

kāval ceṟiya māṭṭi, āytoṭi.





eḻilmā mēṉi makaḷir -

vīḻu māntaṉar, tam koḻunarik kāttē".

naṟṟiṇi : 320





aḷavukku mīṟiya parattiyar oḻukkam, illaṟa vāḻkkiyil periya pūcalukku vaḻivakuttuviṭṭatu. ūṭal eṉappaṭum, kaṇavaṉ maṉiviyarkaḷukkiṭiyēyāṉa ciṟu pūcal, marutattiṇi cārnta pāṭalkaḷiṉ karupporuḷākiviṭṭatu. kīḻ varum pāṭṭu atu kuṟittatu. "veḷḷiya neṟkatiri aṟuvaṭi ceyyum uḻavar muḻakkum taṇṇumi olikku añci, avvayalil aṭaṅkiyirunta paṟavikaḷ ellām eḻuntōṭi neruṅka amartalāl, vayal mītu tāḻa vaḷintirukkum kiḷikaḷiyuṭiya maruta marattiṉ, pūṅkottukkaḷ utirāniṟkum, iravalarkkuk koṭip poruḷākat tērkaḷiyē vaḻaṅkum vaḷḷa lākiya virāṉ eṉpāṉukkariya ciṟanta iruppiyūr pōṉṟa eṉ aḻakellām keṭuvatāyiṉum keṭuka; eṉṉi neruṅka uṉṉi viṭuvēṉallēṉ. neruṅkaviṭṭāl eṉvāy uṉṉi vilakkiṉum, eṉ kikaḷ uṉṉi aṇittuk koḷḷum. āṉāl, nīyō parattiyiṉ mārpāl mācupaṭṭa cantaṉam pūcappaṭṭa mārpiṉi uṭiyāy; apparatti taḻuviyatāl vāṭiya māliyiṉi uṭiyāy; attakiya uṉṉit toṭuvatu, toṭattakātaṉa eṉak kaḻittuviṭṭa tāḻi mutalām kalaṅ kaḷi eṭuttu āḷvataṟku oppākum. ākavē eṉ maṉikku vāraṟka; uṉṉi aṇitta apparatti neṭitu vāḻka"...

"veṇnel arinar taṇṇumi verīip

paḻaṉap palpuḷ iriyak, kaḻaṉi

vāṅkuciṉi marutat tūṅkutuṇar utirum

tērvaṇ virāaṉ iruppi aṉṉa eṉ

tolkaviṉ toliyiṉum tolika; cāra

viṭē eṉ; viṭukkuva ṉāyiṉ, kaṭiik

kavavukki tāṅkum; matukiyam kuvavumuṟi

cāṭiya cāntiṉi ; vāṭiya kōtiyi,





ācil kalam kaḻīi yaṟṟum;

vāral; vāḻiya! kaviiniṉ ṟōḷē”.

naṟṟiṇi : 350





kaṇavaṉ piḻiyi, maṉivi, orōvaḻi maṉṉittu, avaṉi ēṟṟuk koḷvatum uṇṭu. “pāṇaṉē! arukē varuka! nalla aṇikalaṉkaḷi aṇinta eṉ maṉivi, cuṟṟattār aṉivarum kūṭiyiruntu ōmpa, mutal cūl uṭiyāḷāki makavu īṉṟu, nam kuṭivaḷara vaḻiceytu, neyyōṭu kalanta veṇciṟukaṭukām vitikaḷi nam māḷikiyokum viḷaṅkap pūca, pāyalil paṭuttiruntavaḷi neruṅki aḻakiya kūntali uṭiya mutupeṇṭumākit tuyilā niṉṟaṉiyē! eṉṉē niṉ ciṟappu! eṉap palappala kūṟip pārāṭṭi makavu īṉṟu māṇpuṟṟa avaḷ aḻakiya vayiṟṟi, eṉ kikkuvaḷi malarāl taṭavik koṭuttu ētō cintaṉiyil āḻntēṉ pōl ciṟitu poḻutu aṅku niṉṟirunta eṉṉi, mella nōkki, mulliyiṉ arumpukaḷ pōlum paṟkaḷ tōṉṟa ciṟitē nakittup piṉṉar, veṭkam vantuṟavē, nilamalar pōlum kaṇkaḷit taṉ kikaḷāl mūṭikkoṇṭu makiḻnta kāṭci, eṉakku naki taruvatāy iruntatu. atu kuṟittu nakittu makiḻa pāṇaṉē! varuka”.





“vārāy pāṇa! nakukam, nēriḻi karumpuṭik kaṭuñcūl nam kuṭikku utavi, neyyō ṭimikkum aiyavit tiraḷkāḻ viḷaṅkunakar viḷaṅkak kiṭantōḷ kuṟukip putalvaṉ īṉṟeṉap peyarpeyarttu, avvarit titali alkul mutupeṇ ṭākit, tuñcutiyō? mel añcil ōti ; eṉap paṉmāṇ akaṭṭil kuvaḷi oṟṟi uḷḷiṉeṉ uṟiyum eṟkaṇṭu, mella muki nāṇ muṟuval tōṟṟit takimalar uṇkaṇ putittu uvan tatuvē'’

- naṟṟiṇi : 370</poem>}} taliviyiṉ ciṉam perumpālum taṇivatu illi. kaṇavaṉ vāyil vēṇṭip pāṇarnaṇpaṉit tūtu pōkkiṉum, atu payaṉ aḷippatilli . ciṉam māṟā oru maṉiyāḷ kūṟuvatu itu: pāṇaṉē! makappeṟṟataṉāl uṟṟa vālāmi nīṅka eḻuppiya pukiyum paṭintu, putalvaṉukkut tīṭṭum miyum iḻuki, āṭiyum aḻukkup paṭintuḷḷatu. cuṇaṅkāl aḻaku peṟṟa iḷiya koṅkiyiṉ iṉiya pāl peruka, atu karappap putalvaṉi aṇittuk koṇṭami yāl tōḷum muṭi nāṟṟam vīcukiṟatu. ākavē, ivi pōlvaṉavaṟṟāl tūymi keṭṭup pōkāmal putup polivōṭu iruppavarum, nalla pala aṇikaḷi - aṇintavarum ākiya parattiyar cērikkaṇ tēril tirintu koṇṭirukkum uṉ talivaṉukku, nāṉ takutiyuṭiyavaḷ allaḷ. ataṉāl, poṉkampi pōṉṟa narampukaḷik koṇṭa yāḻil eḻum icikku ēṟpa, iṉkural eṭuttup pāṭutalil nī vallavaṉē āyiṉum, karuti vantatip peṟuvāṉ vēṇṭi, eṉṉit toḻutu pāṭuvatu ceyyaṟka kuḷirnta nīrttuṟikaḷāl niṟinta nalla ūrukku uriyōṉākiya uṉ talivaṉi iṅkiruntu koṇṭu ceṉṟu viṭuvāyāka. palarālum pārāṭṭap peṟum takutiyuṭiyatākiya eṉ maṉiyil iruntu nī pāṭātavāṟu, teruvil neṭumpoḻutu niṟkumāṟu tēril pūṭṭappaṭṭirukkum kutirikaḷum anniliyi veṟuttuk kural eḻuppukiṉṟaṉa. ākavē, nāṉ virumpāta niliyil, payaṉil coṟkaḷi mēlum kūṟik koṇṭu niṟka vēṇṭām".

”neyyum kuyyum āṭi, miyoṭu

mācupaṭ ṭaṉṟē kaliṅkamum; tōḷum

titali meṉmulit tīmpāl piliṟṟap

putalvaṟ pullap puṉiṟunā ṟummē;

vāliḻi makaḷir cērit tōṉṟum

tērōṟku ottaṉem allēṉ; ataṉāl

poṉpuṉi narampiṉ iṉkural cīṟiyāḻ

eḻāal, valli āyiṉum; toḻāal,





koṇṭu cel, pāṇa! niṉ taṇtuṟi ūraṉi

pāṭumaṉip pāṭal kūṭātu, nīṭunilip

puraviyum pūṇnili muṉikuva;

virakil moḻiyal; yām vēṭṭatu ilvaḻiyē”.

- naṟṟiṇi : 380.





pōṟṟā oḻukkam uṭiyarākiya parattiyariṉ piṭikkuḷ akappaṭṭuk koḷḷum āṭavar ceyyum tavaṟukaḷukku aḷavē irātu eṉpatu nōkkik, kaṇavari ēṟka maṟukkum maṉiviyariṉ piṭivātattiṉ niyāyattaṉmi uṇarappaṭum parattiyar oḻukkam koṇṭa kaṇavaṉāl puṟakkaṇikkap paṭṭāḷ oruttikku avaḷ tōḻi kūṟuvatu, itu; “aṉṉi pōlum aṉpuṭiyāy! naṟiya neṟṟiyiyuṭiya ariviyē! kātil kuḻi aṇintu mārpil māli cūṭi, kiyil toṭi aṇintu viḻāk kaḷattil tuṇaṅki āṭikkoṇṭirukkum talivaṉik kippaṟṟik koṇṭuvantu viṭalām eṉṟu, eṉ aṟiyāmiyāl ācippaṭṭu, viḻākkaḷum nōkkic ceṉṟu koṇṭiruk kumpōtu, nammiṭam ayalāṉ pōl naṭantukoḷḷum avaṉ, neṭiya periya terukkōṭiyil, oru vaḷivāṉa iṭattil vēṟu oru vaḻiyākat tiṭumeṉa vantu etirppaṭa, avaṉi, “maṉiyāḷip pirintu parattiyar piṉ tiriyum uṉṉik kaṇṭittuk kēṭpār yārēṉum uḷarō? allatu oruvarumē ilarō'’ eṉṟu nāṉ kēṭka, avaṉ atupaṟṟi ētum karutātāṉ pōl, eṉṉip pārttu, ‘uṉ neṟṟip pacali naṉimiku aḻaku” eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ. pakivarālum pārāṭṭattakka perum ciṟappuṭiyāṉ eṉpati maṟantuviṭṭu, “naṇpa! nī oru nāṇili” eṉak kaṭintu kūṟiviṭṭēṉ”

"aṟiyāmiyiṉ aṉṉi ! añcik

kuḻiyaṉ, kōtiyaṉ, kuṟumpit toṭiyaṉ:

viḻavayar tuṇaṅki taḻuukam cella,

neṭunimir teruvil kipuku koṭumiṭi,

notuma lāḷaṉ katum eṉat tākkaliṉ,





'kēṭpōr uḷarkol? illi kol? pōṟṟu' eṉa

'yāḷatu pacali', eṉṟaṉaṉ; ataṉ etir'

“nāṇili eluva!” eṉṟu vanticiṉē;

ceṟunarum viḻiyum cemmalōṉ, eṉa,

naṟunutal arivi! pōṟṟēṉ;

ciṟumi perumiyiṉ kāṇātu, tuṇintē".

- naṟṟiṇi : 50





tuṇaṅkiyāvatu, āṭavarum peṇṭirum paṅku koḷḷum oruvaki āṭal; āṭuṅkāl, kikaḷi vaḷittu oruvariyoruvar taḻuvikkoḷvar. atu, makkaḷip pōlavē, pēykaḷukkum potuvāṉatu. ”kaṇṭāri uḷam naṭuṅkap paṇṇum naṭiyum, kaṇṭārkku accam ūṭṭum uruvamum uṭiya pēymakaḷ. ceṅkuṟuti tuḻāviya kūriya nakaṅkaḷik koṇṭa viralkaḷāl kaṇkaḷit tōṇṭi uṇṇappaṭṭuviṭṭa muṭi nāṟṟam uṭiya kariya piṇattiṉ taliyit toṭiyaṇinta kiyil ēntik koṇṭu, pakivarkku accam varumāṟu, etir ceṉṟu aḻikkum pōrk kaḷattip pāṭit tōḷ kuluṅka appiṇattit tiṉṉum vāyuṭiyavaḷāyt tuṇaṅkik kūttu āṭā niṟkum".

"urukeḻu celaviṉ, añcuvaru pēymakaḷ,

kuruti āṭiya kūrukirk koṭuviral,

kaṇtoṭṭu uṇṭa kaḻimuṭik karuntali

oṇtoṭit taṭakkiyiṉ ēnti, veruvara

ēṉṟu aṭu viṟaṟkaḷam pāṭittu, tōḷpeyarā

niṇamtiṉ vāyaḷ tuṇaṅki tūṅka".

tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭi 57-56





yāḻ icikkum pāṇarum kūttu āṭum kūttarum kūttiyarum, naṭaṉamāṭum viṟaliyarum oṉṟukalantē vāḻvar. oruvarukku oruvar tuṇipurintu koḷvar. avarkaḷ, perumpālum pēraracarkaḷālum, kuṟunilat talivar kaḷālum pēṇappaṭuvar. avarkaḷil oru kuḻuviṉar, orumuṟi, periya villāḷaṉākiya oru kuṟunilat talivaṉic ceṉṟu kaṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷuḷ talivaṉ, pāṇaṉ. ākavē, avaṉ ēṉiyōri nōkki, ‘'nāṉ pāṭukiṟēṉ, viṟali; nīṅkaḷellām muḻaviṉi muḻakkuṅkaḷ, yāḻkaḷil paṇṇici eḻuppuṅkaḷ ; kaḷiṟṟiṉ toṅkum kipōlum tūmpu eṉappaṭum peruvaṅkiyatti iciyuṅkaḷ; ellari eṉappaṭum calliyi vāciyuṅkaḷ. ākuḷi eṉappaṭum ciṟupaṟiyi aṟiyuṅkaḷ; patali eṉappaṭum orukaṇ mākkiṇiyiṉ oru pakkatti mellak koṭṭuṅkaḷ; namatu toḻil uṇarttum aṭiyāḷak kōlām matali mākkōli eṉ ki tāruṅkaḷ'’.

"pāṭuval; viṟali! ōrvaṇṇam, nīrum

maṇmuḻā amimiṉ; paṇyāḻ niṟumiṉ;

kaṇviṭu tūmpiṉ kaḷiṟṟuyir toṭumiṉ;

ellari toṭumiṉ; ākuḷi toṭumiṉ;

patali orukaṇ pi eṉa iyakkumiṉ;

matali mākkōl kivalam tammiṉ".

puṟam : 152 : 13-18





pāṭṭuṭit talivaṉ, avarkaḷukku iṟicci kalanta uṇavum, iṉiya matuvum, poṉṉum niṟiyat taruvaṉ eṉpatu kūṟat tēvi illi. pāṇaṉ kūṟṟil kūṟuvatāṉāl, “vēṭṭiyil tāṉ eytu koṉṟa māṉiṉ niṇattōṭu kūṭiya iṟicciyām pakkuvamākap paṇṇappaṭṭa uṇavōṭu, urukkiya āṉ neypōlum matuviyum tantu, taṉ maliyil kiṭitta kalappillāta poṉṉoṭu, palvaki maṇikkuviyalkaḷiyum, vāṅkik koḷḷuṅkaḷ eṉa vaḻinaṭiyil vāri vaḻaṅkiṉāṉ”.

"vēṭṭattil

tāṉ uyir cekutta māṉniṇap puḻukkōṭu

āṉ uruk kaṉṉa vēriyi nalkit,

taṉ malip piṟanta tāvil naṉpoṉ

paṉmaṇik kuviyoṭum viriik koṇmeṉac

curattiṭi nalki yōṉē” (puṟam : 152 : 25-30)





kaṭaṟkarit tuṟimukaṅkaḷ kiṟittuva ūḻiyiṉ toṭakkattil, urōmāpuriyōṭu naṭipeṟṟa vāṇikattil mikapperiya vaḷarcciyōṭu, kaṭaṟkari nakaraṅkaḷum mikavum, mukkiyattuvam uṭiyatāka uyarntuviṭṭaṉa. avvāṟē ilakkiyaṅkaḷilum kuṟippiṭap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa. kiḻakkuk kaṭaṟkariyil vaṭakōṭiyil kuṟippiṭakkūṭiyatu, piṟkālattil malli eṉṟum, mēlum piṟpaṭṭa kālattil makāpalipuram eṉṟum aḻikkap paṭṭatāṉa māvilaṅkit tuṟimukamām. viḷiyāṭum kūṭṭamākiya ciṟanta toṭi aṇinta makaḷir, paṉṟikaḷ uḻutu cēṟākkiya maṇṇik kiḷaṟiṉāl ataṉ kaṇ pulāl nāṟum āmi muṭṭikaḷiyum, tēṉ nāṟum āmpal kiḻaṅkukaḷiyum peṟum maṇvaḷamum, iḻum eṉum oli ōyātu olikkumāṟu nīri veḷippaṭuttik koṇṭē yirukkam matakukaḷik koṇṭa nīrvaḷamum uṭiya perumimikka māvilaṅki".

ōri āyatta oṇtoṭi makaḷir

kēḻal uḻuta iruñcēṟu kiḷippiṉ,

yāmi īṉṟa pulavunāṟu muṭṭiyit

tēṉ nāṟu āmpal kiḻaṅkoṭu peṟūum

iḻum eṉa olikkum puṉalam putaviṉ

perumā vilaṅki "

-puṟam : 176 : 1-6





ittuṟimukam maṟṟumōr iṭattil, maṇam nāṟum malarkaḷiyuṭiya curapuṉṉi , akil, cantaṉam ākiya maraṅkaḷi nīrāṭum tuṟiyil nīrāṭum makaḷirkaḷiṉ tōḷkaḷukkut teppam ākit tuṇipuriyumāṟu, karikaḷik kutti aḻikkum peruveḷḷam aṭittuk koṇṭuvantu tarum āṟṟuvaḷam mikkatum, aḻikkalākā āṟṟal vāynta tolperum nakarām ilaṅkiyiṉ peyarit tāṉ tōṉṟiya pōtē peṟṟatumākiya māvilaṅki" eṉak kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.

naṟuvī nākamum, akilum, āramum

tuṟiyoṭu makaḷirkkut tōḷ puṇi ākiya





porupuṉal tarūum pōkkarum marapiṉ

toṉmā ilaṅkik karuvoṭu peyariya naṉmā

vilaṅki”

- ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭi : 116 - 120





kāviri kaṭaloṭu kalakkumiṭattil, tollūāḻik kālattiliruntē pukaḻ peṟṟirunta pukār allatu kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉam iṭam peṟṟuḷatu. ākavē, atu pala pāṭalkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. appukārt tuṟimukam “ mēlē viritta pāyic curuṭṭa vēṇṭāmalum, ēṟṟiya pāraṅkaḷi iṟakkiviṭa vēṇṭāmalum, āṟṟu mukattut tuṟimukattuḷ pukunta periya kalattiliruntu paṇiyāṭkaḷ, paṇṭaṅkaḷi, iṭinili nakarukkuk koṇṭupōyc cērkkum” ciṟappuṭiyatākak kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu.

"mīppāy kaḷiyātu, micipparam tōṇṭātu,

pukāarp pukunta perumkalam, takāar,

iṭippulap peruvaḻic coriyum”

puṟam : 30 : 11-13





pūkkaḷ utirntu parantu kiṭakkum akaṉṟa tuṟikaḷi yuṭiya kāviriyākiya periya āṟṟiṉ perukkeṭuttu varum veḷḷa nīr, nuṇmaṇal tiraḷumāṟu mēṭākkiya veṇ maṇaṟ kuviyalkaḷiyum, putuvaruvāykaḷiyum uṭiya ūrkaḷi uṭiya celvavaḷam mikka cōḻa vēntarkaḷāl purakkappaṭum ulakamellām pārāṭṭum naṉmi mikka nalla pukaḻ uṭiyatāṉa nāṉmaṟikaḷām paḻam nūlkaḷi aḷitta mukkaṇ celvaṉ kōyil koṇṭirukkum ālamuṟṟam eṉṉum iṭattil aḻakuṟa eṭukkappaṭṭa poykikaḷic cūḻntirukkum pūñcōlikaḷil, maṉiyuṟi makaḷir, aḻakuṟac ceyyappaṭṭa maṇaṟ pāvikaḷi vittu viḷiyāṭum tuṟiyiṉi uṭiyatum, makarak koṭikaḷi ucciyil koṇṭa vāṉaḷāva uyarnta matiliyum, muṭi aṟiyappaṭāvāṟu mika uyarnta māḷikikaḷiyum uṭiya pukār” eṉṟum, pūkkaḷ malarntu maṇakkum nīṇṭa uppaṅkaḻikaḷiṉ, naṭuvaṇ, perumpukaḻ vāynta kāviriyiṉ karikkaṇ uḷḷa nakaram" eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu.

pūviri akaltuṟik kaṉivicik kaṭunīrk

kāvirip pēriyāṟṟu ayirkoṇṭu īṇṭi

ekkar iṭṭa kuppi veṇmaṇal

vippiṉ yāṇar vaḷaṅkeḻu vēntar,

ñālam nāṟum nalaṅkeḻu nal ici

nāṉmaṟi mutunūl mukkaṇ celvaṉ

āla muṟṟam kaviṉpeṟat tiiya

poyki cūḻnta poḻilmaṉi makaḷir

kicey pāvi tuṟi

makara neṟṟi vāṉtōy puricic,

cikaram tōṉṟāc cēṇuṇar nalil

pukā ar"

-akam : 181 : 11-22





pūviri neṭuṅkaḻi nāppaṇ; perum peyark

kāvirip paṭappip paṭṭiṉam.

akam : 205 : 17-12





iraṇṭu pāṭalkaḷil kūṟappaṭṭirukkum, puṟanti eṉappaṭum puṟiyāṟu, kiḻakkuk kaṭaṟkariyil uḷḷa maṟṟoru tuṟimukappaṭṭiṉam, periya alikaḷ, oliyōṭu, iyakkamum aṭaṅkiyirunta, mēkam cūḻnta iravil, koḻutta mīṉkaḷip piṭikkum paratavar, paranta kaṭalil, taṅkaḷ mīṉpiṭi timilkaḷil, kaṭaliṉ iruḷum nīṅkumāṟu ēṟṟiyirukkum viḷakkukaḷ, pōril puṟam kāṭṭāk kōṭpāṭṭiṉi uṭiya vēntaṉ pācaṟikkaṇ uḷḷa, oyātu āṭikkoṇṭēyirukkum iyalpuṭiyavāya, pōryāṉikaḷiṉ aḻakiya mukattil pūṭṭiyirukkum mukapaṭaṅkaḷiṉ oḷḷiya cuṭarpōlat tōṉṟum iṭamākiya, pāṭivarum iravalarkaḷip piṟariṭam cellāvāṟu vaḷittuk koḷḷavalla kivaṇmi vāynta kōmakaṉākiya, kutirikaḷ pūṇṭa ciṟanta tērppaṭiyuṭiya periyaṉ eṉpāṉukku uriya, malar virinta kottukkaḷik koṇṭa puṉṉi maraṅkaḷ niṟinta cōlikaḷāl cūḻappaṭṭa puṟanti” eṉṟum, ‘'kaḷ uṇṭu makiḻntirup pavaṉum, nalla tēr uṭiyavaṉum ākiya periyaṉ eṉpāṉukku uriya , kaḷ maṇakkum poṟiyāṟu” eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu.

“peruntiri, muḻakkamoṭu iyakku avin tirunta

koṇṭal iraviṉ iruṅkaṭal maṭutta

koḻumīṉ koḷpavar iruḷ nīṅku oṇcuṭar

ōṭāp pūṭki vēntaṉ pācaṟi

āṭiyal yāṉi aṇimukattu acitta

ōṭi oṇcuṭar oppat tōṉṟum

pāṭunart toṭuta kivaṇ kōmāṉ

pariyuṭi naṟṟērp periyaṉ, viriyiṉar

puṉṉiyam kāṉal puṟanti muṉtuṟi”

- akam : 100 : 5- 13





‘'naṟavumakiḻ irukki, naṟṟērp periyaṉ

kaḷkamaḻ poṟiyāṟu”

- naṟṟiṇi : 131 : 7-8





pāṇṭiyariṉ mukkiya tuṟimukam, koṟki. atu muttukkaḷ viḷikiṉṟa paranta kaṭaliyuṭiya koṟki nakarattu muṉpuḷḷa tuṟimukam ; “muttuppaṭu parappiṉ koṟki muṉ tuṟi” (naṟṟiṇi : 23 : 6) eṉṟum, vīram ceṟinta pōril valla pāṇṭiyarkaḷ, aṟaneṟi keṭātu kākkum aḻakiya koṟkip peruntuṟiyil koḷḷum muttu ‘'maṟappōrp pāṇṭiyar aṟattiṟ kākkum koṟkiam ‘peruntuṟi muttu” (akam : 27 : 9-10) eṉṟum; vīcum alikaḷ koṇṭu vantu kuvikkum, kuḷirnta iṉiya oḷiyiṉiyuṭiya muttukkaḷ. ūrpavar. virumpumāṟu naṭakkum kutirikaḷiṉ kāl vaṭukkaḷi maṟikkum nalla tēriyuṭiya pāṇṭiyaratu koṟki. ivartiri tanta īrṅkatir muttam. kavarnaṭip puravik kālvaṭu tapukkum naṟṟēr vaḻuti koṟki ' (akam : 130 : 9-11) eṉṟum, veṟṟiyē kāṇum pōril valalavarkaḷāy pāṇṭiyaratu, pukaḻ mikka ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya koṟki : muṉ tuṟiyil kiṭikkum oḷivīcum muttukkaḷ: "viṟal pōrp pāṇṭiyaṉ, pukaḻmali ciṟappiṉ koṟki muṉtuṟi avir katir muttam" (akam : 207:3-5) eṉṟum, kaṭal nīrp parappil ceṉṟu, pala mīṉkaḷiyum piṭippavar, avaṟṟuṭaṉ cērakkoṇṭa muttuc cippikaḷi, nārāl vaṭittu eṭukkappaṭṭa kaḷḷiṉ, makiḻcci mikka viliyākak koṭukkum. mikka pukaḻ vāynta koṟki", " parappil paṉ mīṉ koḷvar mukanta cippi , nārari naṟaviṉ makiḻnoṭik kūṭṭum pēricik koṟki (akam : 296 : 8-10) eṉṟum, viḷaṅkum periya kaṭalil, etirttārik kollum cuṟāmīṉkaḷi nīkkiviṭṭu valampuric caṅkukaḷi muḻkik koṇarnta periya mīṉpiṭi . paṭakukaḷi uṭiya paratavar, piṭitta accaṅkukaḷiṉ kalle ṉum oliyiṉi muḻukka āravāram mikka koṟki nakarattār varavēṟkak karicērvar eṉṟum pārāṭṭap paṭṭuḷatu.

ilaṅku irum parappiṉ eṟicuṟā nīkki,

valampuri mūḻkiya vāṉtimil paratavar

olittalip paṇilam ārppak kal eṉak

kalikeḻu koṟki etirkoḷa iḻitarum".

-akam : 350 : 10-13





cērarkaḷiṉ mukkiya tuṟimukam muciṟi . "cērarkku uriya cuḷḷiyākiya pēriyāṟṟiṉ veḷḷiya nurikaḷ citaṟi vilakumāṟu, yavaṉarkaḷ koṇṭuvanta aḻakiya vēlip pāṭṭāl ciṟappuṟṟa marakkalam, poṟkācukaḷōṭu vantu miḷakup potikaḷōṭu mīḷum, vaḷam mikka muciṟi".

cēralar,

cuḷḷiyam pēriyāṟṟu veṇnuri kalaṅka,

yavaṉar tanta viṉimāṇ naṉkalam

poṉṉoṭu vantu kaṟiyoṭu peyarum

vaḷamkeḻu muciṟi'.





mīṉ viṟṟu viliyākap peṟṟa nellik kuvittu, annellit tōṇikaḷil ēṟṟivantu maṉikaḷil niṟittu maṉikaḷil kuvintukiṭakkum miḷakup poti ‘kaḷip pēroli mikka kaṭaṟkariyil tāṟumāṟākap pōṭṭu vippar. kalaṅkaḷil vanta poṟkācukaḷi uppaṅ kaḻikaḷil niṟkum tōṇikaḷil kari cērppar. malipaṭu poruḷkaḷiyum kaṭalpaṭu poruḷkaḷiyum oṉṟu kalantu, vantu pukaḻ pāṭuvārkku vaḻaṅkuvar. kaḷvaḷam mikkatum, poṉmāli aṇinta cērarkku uriyatumāṉa, muḻaṅkum kaṭalpōl muracu muḻaṅkum muciṟi . ‘'mīṉ toṭuttu nel kuvii,

mici ampiyiṉ maṉi maṟukkuntu;

maṉik kuvi iya kaṟi mūṭiyāl

kalic cummiya karikalak kuntu;

kalam tanta poṟparīcam -

kaḻittōṇiyāl kari cērkkuntu;

malittāramum kaṭaltāramum

alippeytu varunarkku īyum -

puṉalam kaḷḷiṉ, polantārk kuṭṭuvaṉ,

muḻaṅku kaṭal muḻaviṉ muciṟi”

puṟam : 343 : 7-10





kaṭaṟkari vāḻ makkaḷ, kaṭalil kalaṅkaḷ kaviḻntu pōvati aṟintiruntaṉar. ataṉāltāṉ aṭuttuvarum pāṭṭil, aḻakiya uvami iṭam peṟṟuḷatu. “pāṇaṉ kiyil uḷḷa, yāḻukku uriya ilakkaṇap paṇpukaḷōṭu kūṭiya nalla yāḻ, aḻakiya vaṇṭukaḷ pōla im eṉum ici eḻum, nī vaḻakkamāka varum teruvil, nī varuvati etir pārttu uṉ mārpi, muṉpu tamakku uriyatākap peṟṟirunta, ciṟanta aṇikaḷi aṇintirunta parattiyar palarum, nī kiviṭṭuviṭṭa kavaliyāl, kaṇkaḷiliruntu veppam mikunta kaṇṇīr corintavāṟē, koṭiya puyal kāṟṟu kaṭumiyāka vīcuvatāl tuṉpuṟṟiruntapōtu, kaṭalil tām ēṟiyirunta marakkalam kaviḻntuviṭa, mēlum kalakka muṟṟut tāmum oru cērak kaṭalil vīḻntu āṇṭu mitantu vanta oru palakiyi, vīḻtta aṉivarum orucērap paṟṟik koṇṭu, tām tām taṉittaṉi iḻuppatu pōla, uṉ kikaḷip paṟṟi, avaravar nattam pakkam iḻukka, avariṭi akappaṭṭu nī varuntiya varuttatti nāṉ kaṇkūṭākak kaṇṭēṉ: kaṇṭa eṉṉāl yātu ceyya iyalum?"

"kaṇṭaṉeṉ; makiḻna! kaṇṭu evaṉceykō?

pāṇaṉ kiyatu paṇpuṭic cīṟiyāḻ

yāṇar vaṇṭiṉ immeṉ imirum

ērtaru teruviṉ etircci nōkki, niṉ

mārputalik koṇṭa māṇ iḻi makaḷir,

kavalē muṟṟa ceytu vīḻ arippaṉi ,

kāl ēḻuṟṟa pitaṭu kālik

kaṭalmaram kaviḻnteṉak kalaṅki uṭaṉpiṟappu

palar koḷ palaki pōl

vāṅka vāṅka niṉ tūṅku añar niliyē"



naṟṟiṇi : 30





inta atikārattil eṭuttuk kāṭṭappaṭṭa pala ceyyuḷkaḷilum, iyalpāṉa allatu kaṟpaṉit tiṟamāṉa intiyak kaliyiṉ muḻu mutal mūlamām ciṟappiyalpukaḷit taṉiyē kāṇalām. intiyak kaliyiṉ nōkkam, toṭakka niliyil aḻaku paṭuttuvatē ; oṉṟip pārttu, atip piṉpaṟṟuvataṉṟu. kaliñaṉ, ilakkiyap poruḷākiviṭṭa iyaṟkip poruṭkaḷi, 'iṟṟu' eṉal pōl, uḷḷatu uḷḷavāṟē kūṟiviṭuvati virumpavilla. māṟākat, taṉ kiyil kiṭitta apporuḷi aḻaku ceyvataṟkāṉa aḷaviṟku elliyē illi. eṭuttuk kiyāṇṭa poruḷiṉ ovvoru ciṟu kūṟum, iyaṟkiyōṭu kūṭiya tākat teḷivākap pulappaṭavalla, aṇikaḷiṉ ciṟukūṟu kaḷāl aḻaku ceyyappaṭṭirukkum. ākavē, ceyyuḷ naṭiyil, ovvoru peyarccollukku muṉpum, oru peyaraṭic col koṭukkappaṭum. avvāṟu koṭukkap paṭum iṭi oru toṭarāka amintuviṭiṉ, attoṭaril iṭam peṟṟirukkum ovvoru peyarccolliṉ muṉṉum oru peyaraṭi koṭukkappaṭum. immuṟi , kirēkkak kalik koḷkikaḷil payiṟci peṟṟirukkum ōr uḷḷattik kuḻappattiṟku uḷḷākki, āṅkilattiṟku moḻi peyarppati mikak kaṭiṉamākkum vakiyil, muḻuc ceyyuḷiyum nōkkumpōtu, ōviyaṅkaḷāl oppaṉi ceyyappaṭṭa vāyil kōpuram pōl tōṉṟum vari toṭarntu naṭipeṟum. teṉṉintiyak kaliñarkaḷiṉ uḷḷam, veppa maṇṭalak kāṭukaḷil, ilikaḷ malarkaḷ ākiyavaṟṟiṉ taṅkutaṭiyaṟṟa vaḷamikku aṭimippaṭṭup pōṉataṉ viḷivāṉa, avi paṟṟiya āḻnta cintaṉi, akkālattiya intiyak kali vaḷarccikkut tūṇṭukōlāy niṉṟatu. civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam 1920



tirunelvēli, teṉṉintiya civacittānta nūṟpatippuk kaḻakam, limiṭeṭ,



talimi niliyam : 154, ṭi. ṭi. kē. cāli, āḷvārpēṭṭi, ceṉṉi - 18.



kiḷi niliyaṅkaḷ :

appar accakam, ceṉṉi - 600 106.





inta miṉṉūlip paṟṟi


uṅkaḷukku immiṉṉūl, iṇiya nūlakamāṉa, vikkimūlattil iruntu kiṭittuḷḷatu[1].

inta iṇiya nūlakam taṉṉārvalarkaḷāl vaḷarukiṟatu. vikkimūlam patiya taṉṉārvalarkaḷi varavēṟkiṟatu. tāṅkaḷum vikkimūlattil iṇintu mēlum pala miṉṉūlkaḷi aṉivarum paṭikkumāṟu ceyyalām.

mikunta akkaṟiyuṭaṉ meyppu ceytālum, miṉṉūlil piḻi ētēṉum iruntāl tayakkam illāmal, vikkimūlattil immiṉṉūliṉ pēccu pakkattil terivikkalām allatu piḻikaḷi nīṅkaḷē kūṭa cari ceyyalām.

ippaṭippākkam, kaṭṭaṟṟa urimaṅkaḷōṭu (potukaḷa /kuṉu -Commons /GNU FDL )[2][3] ilavacamāka aḷikkappaṭukiṟatu. eṉavē, inta uriyi nīṅkaḷ maṟṟavarōṭu pakiralām; māṟṟi mēmpaṭuttalām; vaṇika nōkkattōṭum, vaṇika nōkkamiṉṟiyum payaṉpaṭuttalām

immiṉṉūl cāttiyamāvataṟku paṅkaḷittavarkaḷ piṉvarumāṟu:
